### [1] [Understanding Temporal Semantic Stability in Open-Vocabulary UAV Perception through Metric 3D Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28665)
*Saurbh Singh Jamwal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于度量3D融合的体素级评估框架，用于量化开放词汇分割模型在无人机（UAV）移动平台上产生的时序语义不稳定性，引入语义信念漂移、观测持续性等指标，发现观测持续性不足会掩盖真实的时间语义一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放词汇分割模型在无人机平台上虽实现了先进的语义感知，但从移动飞行平台观测同一物理场景时，逐帧预测存在时序不一致（语义闪烁）问题。现有评估大多关注聚合层面的语义准确性，忽略了时序稳定性这一关键维度，尤其是在观测重复次数有限的情况下，高聚合一致率可能高估真实的时间稳定性。

Method: 通过度量3D融合将逐帧预测与世界空间中的持久位置进行关联，构建体素级评估框架，联合刻画四个指标：最终语义一致性（semantic agreement）、语义信念漂移（SBD）、观测持续性（OP）以及语义不确定性。在UAVid-3D数据集上进行实验，并对观测持续性进行分层分析（persistence-stratified analysis），同时控制体素分辨率、几何关联和时序采样密度等变量。

Result: 实验揭示显著的帧间语义闪烁现象；高聚合世界空间一致性在观测支持不足时高估了时序稳定性；反复出现的体素暴露出更大的语义分歧，而信念漂移随证据累积而减少；上述行为在两个分割骨干网络上均一致，且在体素分辨率、几何关联和时序采样密度的变化下保持稳定；降低世界空间重复观测的条件反而可能提升表观聚合稳定性。

Conclusion: 观测持续性（observation persistence）是评估长时程语义可靠性的关键条件变量，语义一致性的解读必须与观测支持程度相结合，不能仅依赖聚合层面的一致性指标。

Abstract: Recent open-vocabulary segmentation models have advanced semantic perception for UAVs, but predictions from moving aerial platforms can remain temporally inconsistent across repeated observations of the same physical scene. We investigate temporal semantic stability by associating frame-wise predictions with persistent world-space locations through metric 3D fusion. We introduce a voxel-level evaluation framework that jointly characterises final semantic agreement, Semantic Belief Drift (SBD), Observation Persistence (OP), and semantic uncertainty. Experiments on UAVid-3D reveal substantial frame-wise semantic flicker and show that high aggregate world-space agreement can overstate temporal stability when locations have limited repeated-observation support. Persistence-stratified analysis shows that recurrent voxels expose greater semantic disagreement, while belief drift decreases as additional evidence accumulates. This behaviour is observed across two segmentation backbones and remains consistent under variations in voxel resolution, geometric association, and temporal sampling density. Conditions that reduce world-space recurrence can increase apparent aggregate stability, demonstrating that semantic consistency must be interpreted together with observation support. Our findings highlight observation persistence as an essential conditioning variable for evaluating long-horizon semantic reliability.

</details>


### [2] [FrameScope: Temporal Data Valuation for Stream Active Learning in Autonomous Vehicle Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28672)
*Yuheng Zhu,Man-Ki Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FrameScope是一个面向自动驾驶连续学习的时序数据估值框架，将神经正切核理论扩展到时序领域，在车辆端进行原则性的帧选择，仅向云端查询高价值帧的标签。实验表明其在多种域偏移下相比现有方法具有更高的样本效率和更低的灾难性遗忘，同时大幅降低带宽需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶环境动态多变且不断出现新场景和边界情况，静态学习模型无法保证安全可靠运行，因此需要连续学习。然而自动驾驶产生的海量视觉数据流使现有基于启发式采样的连续学习方法无法捕捉时序动态，容易遗漏关键学习机会或选择冗余帧，同时云端处理全部视频数据的方式带宽开销巨大。

Method: FrameScope将神经正切核（NTK）理论扩展到时序域，实现流式视觉数据的原则性估值。该框架在车辆端执行本地的帧选择，只将选中的高价值帧的标签查询请求发送给基于云端的oracle模型，从而实现带宽高效的连续学习。

Result: 在多个域偏移场景的广泛实验中，FrameScope一致性地优于现有方法，实现了更高的样本效率，并在自动驾驶感知任务中显著减少了灾难性遗忘。同时通过本地数据估值和选择性标签查询，大幅降低了带宽需求，支持轻量级云端标注服务的可扩展运行。

Conclusion: FrameScope通过将数据估值引入车辆端并在云端仅查询选中帧的标签，在提升连续学习性能的同时显著降低带宽开销，为自动驾驶中的可扩展连续学习提供了一种高效且原则性的解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles operate in dynamic, ever-changing environments where new scenarios and edge cases constantly emerge. As a result, static learning models are inadequate for ensuring safe and reliable operation. Continuous learning is essential for adapting to these evolving conditions and maintaining robust performance across diverse real-world settings. However, autonomous vehicles generate massive streams of visual data during operation, and existing continuous learning approaches typically rely on heuristic sampling methods that fail to capture temporal dynamics, often overlooking critical learning opportunities or selecting redundant frames. In this paper, we introduce FrameScope, a temporal data valuation framework for continuous learning in autonomous vehicles. FrameScope extends neural tangent kernel theory to temporal domains, enabling principled valuation of streaming visual data. Unlike cloud-centric methods that transmit all video data for processing, our approach performs principled, local frame selection on the vehicle and queries a cloud-based oracle model only for labels of those high-value frames. Extensive experiments across multiple domain shifts show that FrameScope consistently outperforms existing methods, achieving higher sample efficiency and significantly reducing catastrophic forgetting in autonomous vehicle perception. By valuing data on the vehicle and querying only labels for selected frames, FrameScope reduces bandwidth requirements, enabling scalable operation with a lightweight cloud labeling service.

</details>


### [3] [AdaptAV: Continuous Adaption of Vision Models for Autonomous Vehicles Using Cloud-based Oracle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28673)
*Yuheng Zhu,Dhruva Ungrupulithaya,Boluo Ge,Man-Ki Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该系统通过在云端利用充足计算资源运行高精度"oracle"模型，指导车载轻量级视觉模型的持续重训练，并将更新后的模型下发至车辆，从而在保持推理速度的同时逐步提升感知精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆中的视觉感知模型需优先保证推理速度，因此通常采用更浅层、参数更精简的架构。这类小模型泛化能力不足，在遇到新场景时性能可能大幅下降。

Method: 提出一套云-车协同系统：车辆将数据上传至云端，云端利用丰富的计算资源（包括机器学习加速器）运行高精度oracle模型，以此引导车载模型的持续重训练；训练完成后将新模型通过网络传输回车辆用于感知。

Result: 车载模型通过持续重训练，随时间推移推理精度逐步提升，克服了小型模型在陌生场景下泛化能力差的缺陷。

Conclusion: 通过云端重训练与模型回传机制，在维持车载模型推理速度优势的同时，实现了感知精度的持续改善。

Abstract: Deploying vision perception models in autonomous vehicles requires that we prioritize inference speeds, resulting in a model with shallower architectures and lesser model parameters (i.e., more pruned). Such small models do not generalize well, which could result in poor performance when encountered with novel scenarios. We propose a system that overcomes this by continuously retraining the vision models on the cloud with data uploaded by vehicles. We leverage the abundant compute resources, including machine learning accelerators, of the cloud to run a highly-accurate oracle model that will guide the retraining process of the on-vehicle model. This newly trained model is transmitted to the vehicle over the network and is utilized by the vehicle for perceptions, leading to improved inference accuracy over time.

</details>


### [4] [Multi-Agent Self-Improving Reinforcement Learning for Video Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28675)
*Mingwen Zhang,Jisheng Dang,Minqiang Yang,Bimei Wang,Bin Hu,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了冻结验证器能否作为训练信号指导视频推理中的时间证据选择。作者提出多智能体框架，将可训练的Grounder与冻结的Verifier耦合，通过组相对策略梯度和自举校准损失引导训练。两亿参数模型在零样本跨任务迁移中，在接地问答（28.7% IoU、25.4%答案接地准确率）、时间接地（46.1% IoU）和长视频问答（54.1%）基准上取得结果，相对同规模基线改进虽小但一致，主要在IoU等相关性指标上提升显著，而严格边界精度仍较弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视频推理任务中，时间监督主要通过边界回归或跨度生成等局部目标实现，验证器仅用于推理时的候选片段重排序。作者探究是否存在一种新范式：将冻结的验证器同时用作训练信号，以更好地引导证据选择过程。

Method: 提出多智能体强化学习框架MASIRL，由可训练Grounder和冻结Verifier耦合组成。Grounder采样候选轨迹和证据片段，Verifier给出查询条件下的片段评分；采用组相对策略梯度目标，奖励优于组内同输入伙伴的轨迹；同时使用自举校准损失，将时间预测向验证器偏好的跨度引导。训练在源任务上完成，无需目标数据集微调。

Result: 两亿参数模型零样本迁移至三个下游任务：接地问答基准达28.7% IoU和25.4%答案接地准确率，时间接地基准达46.1% IoU，长视频问答基准达54.1%。与同规模强基线相比，改进适度但一致，在IoU和中度重叠召回等相关性导向指标上提升最明显。

Conclusion: 在测试的基准与迁移设置下，结果支持将冻结验证器作为证据选择的训练信号这一范式，但同时表明严格边界精度仍然相对较弱，说明该方法的优势主要体现在相关性匹配而非精确时间边界预测上。

Abstract: Video reasoning tasks such as grounded video question answering and temporal grounding require selecting temporal evidence that supports the query. In many current training setups, temporal supervision is applied through local objectives such as boundary regression or span generation, while verification is used mainly to rerank candidate segments at inference time. We study whether a frozen verifier can also guide training. Our multi-agent framework couples a trainable \emph{Grounder} with a frozen \emph{Verifier}: the Grounder samples candidate trajectories and evidence segments, the Verifier assigns query-conditioned segment scores, a group-relative policy-gradient objective favors trajectories that outperform their within-input peers, and a bootstrapped calibration loss steers temporal predictions toward verifier-preferred spans. Trained on source tasks and evaluated without target-dataset fine-tuning, a two-billion-parameter instantiation transfers zero-shot across grounded question answering, temporal grounding, and long-video question answering, reaching 28.7\% intersection-over-union and 25.4\% answer-grounding accuracy on a grounded-question-answering benchmark, 46.1\% intersection-over-union on a temporal-grounding benchmark, and 54.1\% on a long-video question-answering benchmark. Relative to a strong same-scale baseline, the gains are modest but consistent, with the clearest improvements on relevance-oriented metrics such as intersection-over-union and moderate-overlap recall. Within the tested benchmarks and transfer setting, the results support frozen verification as a training signal for evidence selection, while showing that strict boundary precision remains comparatively weaker. Code and models are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/MASIRL-E50C/

</details>


### [5] [Data Diversity, Not Frequency Invariance: A Controlled and Self-Audited Study of Compression-Robust Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28685)
*Abbas Aliyev,Samir Rustamov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Frequency features and compression-invariant representation learning are widely assumed to be key to deepfake detection that survives video compression. We test this with CAFRL - block-DCT and FFT-phase streams, compression-level-conditioned band attention, and adversarial (gradient-reversal) compression invariance - and report a controlled negative. Under a pre-registered protocol with capacity- and augmentation-matched controls, a plain EfficientNet-B0 on multi-quality data beat CAFRL as specified at every compression level on the FaceForensics++ test split, by 3.66 AUC points at CRF 40 (paired, single seed). A self-audit of our own negative found four defects biased against the frequency hypothesis, and pre-specified re-tests repairing all four showed the deficit to be a recipe artifact, not an architecture failure: the baseline recipe recovered 3.96 points over the matching shipped-recipe variant. The frequency path made no detectable difference: discriminative alone (standalone validation AUC 0.91-0.98 late in training) but of no marginal value under this fusion, at two feature widths of one 4.0 M trunk, every seed-pooled interval for the intra-dataset compression contrasts including zero; on the single held-out manipulation tested, the fair variants sat below the plain backbone. The adversarial branch, as specified, added nothing and degraded its own conditioning estimator; at the fair recipe it is untested. Robustness under single-pass H.264 re-encoding came instead from data diversity: real constant-rate-factor variants beat synthetic JPEG augmentation by 7.3 points (single runs, non-overlapping intervals). The evidence is FaceForensics++-family, GAN-era and single-codec. Match controls on training recipe as well as capacity, and buy compression robustness with codec diversity before architecture.

</details>


### [6] [Projection-Aware End-to-End Learned Video Compression for 360-Degree Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28689)
*Niloofar Maani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了7种360度视频投影格式对端到端神经压缩性能的影响，发现等距柱状投影（ERP）及其填充变体在基于光流的尺度空间流模型下压缩效率最高，与传统H.265/HM-16.16编码器偏好立方体映射类投影的结论相反，表明投影效率高度依赖具体编解码器架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 360度视频需要将球面内容投影到二维平面才能编码，投影格式的选择会影响空间连续性、采样均匀性、运动估计和压缩效率。然而，现有研究主要针对传统混合编码器，投影格式对新兴的端到端神经压缩模型的影响尚不清楚，需要系统评估以指导学习驱动的360度视频压缩方案设计。

Method: 使用JVET 360Lib支持的7种投影格式，基于尺度空间流（scale-space flow）模型在JVET测试序列和通用测试条件下进行端到端评估。流程为：从源等距柱状投影转换到编码投影→多码率点压缩→重建→逆转换回球面。采用PSNR、球面PSNR、加权球面PSNR和Bjøntegaard delta rate作为评价指标，并与传统HM-16.16编码器及可微的投影-压缩-逆投影流水线进行对比。

Result: 等距柱状投影和填充等距柱状投影在尺度空间流模型下压缩效率最高，而立方体映射类和菱形十二面体投影效果较差。这与HM-16.16中立方体映射类格式（特别是等角立方体映射和调整后的立方体映射）优于等距柱状投影的结果相反。

Conclusion: 投影效率是编解码器相关的：基于光流的神经模型受益于单面投影的空间连续性，而基于块的混合编码器更适合多面布局。研究为基于学习的360度视频压缩提供了投影格式选择指导。

Abstract: 360-degree video supports immersive applications such as virtual reality, autonomous driving, and education. Because spherical content cannot be processed directly by conventional video codecs, it must first be mapped to a two-dimensional projection. Projection choice affects spatial continuity, sampling uniformity, motion estimation, and compression efficiency.
  This thesis investigates how projection format influences end-to-end neural compression of 360-degree video. Seven formats supported by JVET 360Lib are evaluated using the scale-space flow model, JVET test sequences, and common test conditions. Each sequence is converted from its source equirectangular projection to a coding projection, compressed at multiple rate points, reconstructed, and converted back. Performance is assessed using PSNR, spherical PSNR, weighted spherical PSNR, and Bjøntegaard delta rate. A differentiable pipeline combining projection conversion, neural compression, and inverse projection is also compared with 360Lib.
  Results show that equirectangular and padded equirectangular projections provide the highest compression efficiency with the scale-space flow model, while cubemap-based and rhombic dodecahedron projections are less effective. This differs from the conventional HM-16.16 codec, for which cubemap-based formats, particularly equi-angular and adjusted cubemap projections, outperform equirectangular formats. Neural models based on optical flow benefit from the spatial continuity of single-face projections, whereas block-based hybrid codecs better accommodate multi-face layouts. These findings show that projection efficiency is codec-dependent and provide guidance for selecting projections for learning-based 360-degree video compression.

</details>


### [7] [MANTLE: A Framework for Adaptive In-Situ Planetary Perception Using a Modular Uplink Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28724)
*Pranav Durai,Gary Doran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MANTLE，一个基于共享DINOv2骨干网络的多任务自适应模型，用于火星地形与地貌提取，包含地形分类（准确率92.56%）和巨石分割（IoU 0.753）两个任务头，并提出了模块化上行链路原则，支持在轨模型持续学习和扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行星表面探测任务依赖自主机器人平台进行地形理解以确保安全导航和科学目标实现，而地形分类和巨石分割等感知能力对着陆点选择和路径规划至关重要。现有方法往往是单一任务的固定模型，难以在任务生命周期内持续扩展新能力。

Method: MANTLE采用共享的DINOv2骨干网络进行高层特征提取，配有两个任务专用头：一个用于大规模地形分类的分类头（基于HiRISE轨道影像数据训练）和一个用于逐像素巨石定位的分割头（基于MSL表面级影像数据训练）。整体设计遵循模块化上行链路原则，即共享骨干冻结在轨、轻量任务头在地球训练后上行部署。

Result: 分类头在七类火星地形上达到92.56%的测试准确率；分割头达到0.753的验证IoU，并在未见过的火星车穿越路线测试集上表现出强跨sol泛化能力。

Conclusion: MANTLE展示了模块化、可扩展的多任务框架的可行性，通过共享冻结骨干加轻量任务头的架构，使未来火星探测器能够在任务生命周期内不断学习和适应，每次上行链路都增添新能力，无需完全重训模型。

Abstract: Planetary surface exploration missions rely increasingly on autonomous robotic platforms capable of interpreting complex terrain to ensure safe navigation, enable targeted science, and improve operational efficiency, as demonstrated across past Mars missions from Viking through Perseverance. Among the key perception capabilities, landform classification provides contextual information for landing site selection and scientific analysis, while boulder segmentation supports hazard assessment and path planning. This paper presents MANTLE, a multi-task adaptive network for terrain and landform extraction. The model uses a shared DINOv2 backbone for high-level feature extraction with task-specific heads: a classification head for large-scale landform classification, and a segmentation head for pixel-wise boulder localization, each trained on curated datasets built respectively from HiRISE orbital imagery and MSL surface-level imagery. The classification head achieved a test accuracy of 92.56% across seven Martian terrain classes, while the segmentation head achieved a validation IoU of 0.753 and showed strong cross-sol generalization on a held-out test set from previously unseen rover traverses. A key advantage of MANTLE is its modular, extensible design, formalized here as the Modular Uplink Principle: only a shared, frozen backbone needs to remain onboard, while subsequent perception capabilities are trained on Earth as lightweight task-specific heads and uplinked without retraining the full model. This work demonstrates two such high-impact capabilities, terrain classification and boulder segmentation, as an initial realization of a framework built to support many more over a mission's lifetime. With this foundation, future explorers need not arrive on Mars fully formed, but can continue to learn, adapt, and grow more capable with every uplink.

</details>


### [8] [RoSe-SLAM: Robust Semantic-Aware Gaussian Splatting SLAM from Dynamic Monocular Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29003)
*Wenting Wang,Jiaxin Guo,Wenzhen Dong,Yun-Hui Liu,Charlie C. L. Wang,Yeung Yam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RoSe-SLAM，一种基于语义感知的高斯溅射（Gaussian Splatting）RGB SLAM系统，利用2D基础模型的语义特征处理动态环境中的相机跟踪和静态场景重建，在动态TUM、Bonn和Wild-Mocap数据集上超越现有动态RGB SLAM基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SLAM系统基于静态假设，在动态和非结构化环境中精度显著退化。现有语义SLAM依赖手工标注的语义标签，难以有效处理动态物体的干扰，亟需一种从无标定单目输入中实现动态场景鲁棒跟踪与高质量场景重建的方法。

Method: 1) 从2D基础模型蒸馏丰富语义特征至高斯场，实现语义感知的多视图一致性；2) 提出时空运动掩码生成模块，同时捕捉长期运动监控和短期瞬态动态，实现动态物体与静态背景的稳健解耦；3) 在全局束调整中提出遮挡感知的关键帧选择机制（以遮挡为度量）和多视图语义一致性模块以提升动态环境下的建图质量；4) 结合几何运动线索与语义先验，动态过滤不可靠观测并重建精确的静态场景几何。

Result: 在动态TUM、Bonn和Wild-Mocap基准数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在轨迹估计和静态场景建图两方面均取得优于现有动态RGB SLAM基线的性能，特别是在长期动态室内环境中表现出色。

Conclusion: RoSe-SLAM通过语义感知的高斯表示与多视图一致性机制，有效解决了动态环境下的SLAM鲁棒性问题，实现了高精度的相机跟踪和静态场景重建，验证了语义先验与几何运动线索融合在动态SLAM中的有效性。

Abstract: In dynamic and unstructured environments, conventional SLAM systems generally suffer from significant accuracy degeneration due to their static assumptions. In this work, we propose Robust Semantic-aware Gaussian Splatting SLAM (RoSe-SLAM), to address the dynamic challenge by a holistic semantic scene understanding from uncalibrated monocular inputs, achieving accurate camera tracking and high-quality geometry reconstruction. Unlike conventional semantic SLAM using handcrafted semantic labels, our RoSe-SLAM exploits the semantic feature from 2D foundation model to enhance the dynamic tracking and mapping performance. By distilling the rich semantic features to our Gaussian fields, our method effectively identifies dynamic distractors and achieves semantic-aware multi-view consistency, significantly enhancing the geometric reconstruction and scene inpainting. Specifically, we propose a spatial-temporal motion mask generation module, enabling both long-term motion monitoring and short-term transient dynamics capturing, achieving robust and effective disentanglement of dynamic objects and static backgrounds. During global bundle adjustment, we propose an occlusion-aware keyframe selection mechanism to prioritize the occlusion as metric to pick the keyframes, and a multi-view semantic consistency module to improve the mapping quality in dynamic environments. By combining geometric motion cues with semantic priors, our system dynamically filters unreliable observations and reconstructs accurate static scene geometry. Extensive experiments conducted on benchmark datasets including dynamic TUM, Bonn and Wild-Mocap datasets, demonstrate that our method achieves superior performance in both trajectory estimation and static scene mapping, outperforming existing dynamic RGB SLAM baselines in long-term dynamic indoor environments.

</details>


### [9] [Chat-Edit-3D++: Interactive 3D and 4D Scene Editing via Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29137)
*Shuangkang Fang,Yufeng Wang,Yi-Hsuan Tsai,Wenrui Ding,Yi Yang,Shuchang Zhou,Ming-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Hash-Atlas网络和CE3D++框架，将3D场景编辑重构为2D atlas图像操作，实现2D编辑与3D重建流程解耦，并基于LLM实现对话式、任意文本输入的3D场景编辑，还可扩展到4D场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉-语言预训练模型的3D场景编辑方案存在两大不足：一是固定输入模式限制了文本输入的灵活性；二是编辑能力受限于单个或少数2D视觉模型，且需要复杂的pipeline设计才能将2D模型集成到3D重建过程中。

Method: 提出Hash-Atlas网络，将3D场景编辑转化为对2D atlas图像的操作，实现2D编辑与3D重建流程解耦。在此基础上构建以LLM为核心的CE3D++对话式3D编辑框架，支持任意文本输入并由LLM理解意图后自主调用相应视觉模型。同时通过运动约束将CE3D++扩展到单目4D场景，并创建轨迹数据集微调LLM，使小型LLM能够精确调度多达30种视觉工具。

Result: 实验表明CE3D++能够有效集成多个视觉模型实现多样的视觉编辑效果，具备强大的场景理解和多轮对话能力。

Conclusion: 该工作通过流程解耦和LLM驱动的自主工具调度，解决了现有3D场景编辑在输入灵活性和模型集成方面的局限，为交互式3D设计工具的发展提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Recent work on image content manipulation based on vision-language pre-training models has been effectively extended to text-driven 3D scene editing. However, existing schemes for 3D scene editing still have certain shortcomings, hindering their further development as interactive design tools. Such schemes typically adhere to fixed input patterns, limiting flexibility in text input. Furthermore, their editing capabilities are constrained by a single or a few 2D visual models and require intricate pipeline design to integrate these models into 3D reconstruction processes. To address the aforementioned issues, we propose the Hash-Atlas network, which reformulates 3D scene editing as operations on 2D atlas images, thereby achieving a workflow decoupling of the 2D editing and 3D reconstruction processes. Building on this foundation, we introduce a dialogue-based 3D scene editing approach, termed CE3D++, which is centered on a large language model (LLM) that allows arbitrary textual input from users and interprets their intentions, subsequently facilitating the autonomous invocation of the corresponding visual models. Additionally, we extend CE3D++ to monocular 4D scenes by imposing motion constraints on moving objects and further fine-tuning the LLM by creating a trajectory dataset related to editing tasks, which enables the smaller LLM to schedule up to 30 different visual tools accurately. Experimental results demonstrate that CE3D++ effectively integrates multiple visual models to achieve diverse visual editing effects, possessing strong scene comprehension and multi-round dialog capabilities. The source codes and trained models are available at https://github.com/Fangkang515/CE3D.

</details>


### [10] [Background-Free Objectness Learning for Class-Agnostic Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29232)
*Dania Batool,Liliana Lo Presti,Marco La Cascia,Filippo Vella*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出B-FOR（Background-Free Objectness Learning），一种无需背景监督的稠密类无关目标检测框架。它通过学习稠密的多尺度目标中心和位移感知尺度场来生成目标假设，避免将未标注区域隐式当作背景，从而消除目标性偏差。在PASCAL VOC、MS-COCO和Open Images上较现有类无关基线召回率提升超过+10 AR点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统目标检测器在封闭集监督下训练，将未标注区域隐式视为背景，这在不完整标注条件下引入了目标性偏差（objectness bias）：视觉上有效但未标注的目标被当作负样本，使得目标性判断与标注类别体系绑定而非通用的目标结构。这一限制对类无关检测和开放世界检测尤为严重。因此需要一种无需显式背景监督的目标性学习方法。

Method: B-FOR将检测重新表述为预测稠密的多尺度目标中心场（object-center fields）和尺度场（scale fields），目标假设从局部空间结构中涌现（emerge）。监督信号仅限可靠标注区域，通过空间结构化的软目标（spatially structured soft targets）实现，避免前背景判别。此外引入位移感知尺度场（displacement-aware scale fields），将目标范围建模为所学目标性场的空间变化属性，以支持从涌现的局部最大值解码目标。

Result: 在PASCAL VOC、MS-COCO和Open Images数据集上，B-FOR展现出对未见类别和跨数据集目标分布的强大泛化能力。相比现有类无关基线，召回率提升超过+10 AR点。消融实验证明，局部化目标性监督和位移感知尺度场对不完整标注下的类无关定位均至关重要。

Conclusion: B-FOR通过去除背景监督来消除目标性偏差，为类无关和开放世界检测提供了一种有效方案。其核心贡献在于将目标性学习从类别判定中解耦，并通过位移感知的尺度场实现从新兴局部最大值中可靠解码目标。代码已开源。

Abstract: Object detectors are typically trained under closed-set supervision, where unlabeled regions are implicitly treated as background. Under incomplete annotations, this assumption introduces objectness bias: visually valid but unlabeled objects are used as negatives, tying objectness to the annotated taxonomy rather than generic object structure. This limitation is particularly problematic for class-agnostic and open-world detection. This paper proposes Background-Free Objectness Learning (B-FOR), a dense class-agnostic detection framework that learns objectness without explicit background supervision on unlabeled regions. B-FOR formulates detection as the prediction of dense multi-scale object-center and scale fields, from which object hypotheses emerge as local spatial structures. Supervision is confined to reliable annotated regions through spatially structured soft targets, avoiding foreground-background discrimination. To support decoding from emergent local maxima, the paper further introduces displacement-aware scale fields that model object extent as a spatially varying property of the learned objectness field. Experiments on PASCAL VOC, MS-COCO, and Open Images demonstrate strong generalization to unseen categories and cross-dataset object distributions. B-FOR improves recall by more than +10 AR points over prior class-agnostic baselines. Ablation studies show that both localized objectness supervision and displacement-aware scale fields are critical for class-agnostic localization under incomplete annotations. Code available at: https://github.com/Daniaawan/B-FOR.

</details>


### [11] [AGRICAM: A Track-Mounted Crop Pollination Monitoring Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29237)
*Malika Nisal Ratnayake,Adel N. Toosi,James Cook,Romina Rader,Alan Dorin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍AGRICAM——一种专为受保护种植系统大规模授粉监测而设计的自主机器人系统。该机器人沿低成本轨道自主移动，集成双RGB相机、微气候传感器、GPS、RFID及4G网络，将采集数据上传云端并通过计算机视觉模型分析授粉昆虫活动。在商业蓝莓农场的80米工业隧道中30小时的实测成功验证了其能力，确认了传粉者均匀分布并揭示了时间与微气候对昆虫活动的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 昆虫授粉对全球粮食生产至关重要，但商业化农场规模下的传粉者监测仍具挑战。现有计算机视觉与深度学习虽能实现精细的传粉者行为分析，但监测必须在细节、空间覆盖范围以及人力/技术资源之间进行权衡。因此需要一种可规模化、低人工干预的自动化授粉监测解决方案。

Method: AGRICAM是一套自主机器人系统：沿低成本易安装的轨道在作物行间移动，不干扰农场作业和昆虫行为；平台集成双RGB相机、微气候传感器、GPS、RFID模块、运动传感器和4G蜂窝网络；通过Web界面远程配置与调度；系统自主采集昆虫位置图像及环境数据，传输至云端并使用计算机视觉模型量化传粉者访问频率和时空活动变化。系统在商业蓝莓农场的80米工业隧道中部署测试30小时。

Result: 系统成功绘制了80米长工业隧道中30小时的昆虫授粉分布图；空间分析确认了棚内传粉者均匀分布（符合农场管理预期）；数据揭示了昆虫活动随一天中时间及微气候条件变化的规律。

Conclusion: AGRICAM被证明是一种可扩展、自动化的作物授粉监测系统，能够支持数据驱动的授粉管理决策，从而提升作物生产力和粮食安全。

Abstract: Insect pollination is critical for global food production, yet monitoring pollinators at commercial farm scale remains a challenge. Recent advances in computer vision and deep learning have enabled detailed analysis of pollinator behaviour, but monitoring must trade-off detail against spatial coverage and human or technological resources. This paper presents the Automated Guided Robot for Insect and Crop Activity Monitoring (AGRICAM), a purpose-built robotic system designed to meet the requirements of large-scale pollination monitoring in protected cropping systems. AGRICAM operates autonomously on low-cost, easily installed track for movement along crop rows, without disrupting farm operations or insect behaviour. The platform integrates two RGB cameras, microclimate sensors, GPS and RFID modules, motion sensors, and 4G cellular network connectivity for data transmission. A web interface enables remote device configuration and scheduling. The system autonomously captures video and image data of insects' locations and local environmental conditions. These are transferred to the cloud and analysed using computer vision models to quantify pollinator visitation and spatio-temporal activity variation. We deployed the system on a commercial blueberry farm to demonstrate and test its capability. It successfully mapped insect pollination patterns across 80 m long industrial polytunnels over 30 hours. This data enabled spatial analyses of insect activity we used to confirm a uniform pollinator distribution within polytunnels, as desired by the farm management team. The data also highlighted variation of insect activity associated with time of day and microclimate. AGRICAM therefore has been shown to be a scalable, automated crop pollination monitor that can support data-driven decisions to enhance pollination management, thereby improving crop productivity and food security.

</details>


### [12] [Calibration and Comparative Analysis of Forward-Looking Sonar and 3D Sonar for Enhanced Underwater Object Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29433)
*Aditya Penumarti,Khanh Dong,Zi-Hao Zhang,Yongkyoon Park,Zhenqi Wu,Trung Dong,Shahriar Negahdaripour,Xiaomin Lin,Jane Shin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出利用两种声呐模态（2D强度图像和3D点云）的自动校准算法来过滤噪声特征，从而增强稀疏点云中的特征提取效果，实验表明自动校准相比手动校准提升5%性能，过滤后特征提取相对原始点云提升超过40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 声呐产生大量噪声，尤其是新型技术生成的全3D点云中噪声被放大，导致稀疏点云中特征识别困难，影响导航、识别和重建任务。

Method: 利用两种互补的声呐模态（2D强度图像和3D点云），通过自动校准技术在两模态间过滤噪声特征，以增强特征提取。

Result: 自动校准比手动校准在性能上提升5%，噪声过滤使特征提取相比原始点云提升超过40%。

Conclusion: 多模态声呐融合结合自动校准和噪声过滤可显著提升稀疏点云中的特征提取效果，并已公开代码和数据集以供复现。

Abstract: Sonars generate a significant amount of noise. With the advent of new technology capable of producing full 3D point clouds, the noise is amplified in sparse point clouds, making it challenging to recognize features for navigation, recognition, or reconstruction. To address this challenge, we propose using two different sonar modalities: one that produces a 2D intensity image and another that generates a 3D point cloud. By implementing auto-calibration, we can filter out noisy features between the modalities to enhance feature extraction. Experiments demonstrate that auto-calibration improves performance over manual calibration by 5% and that filtering enhances feature extraction by more than 40% relative to the raw point cloud. Code and datasets are given at https://theaprilab.org/fls-3d-calibrator

</details>


### [13] [Proximity3D: Shape from Capacitive Proximity on Sensing Manifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30344)
*Hao Chen,Chenming Wu,Chun Ping Lam,Xiangjia Chen,Guoxin Fang,Charlie C. L. Wang,Yeung Yam,Juncong Lin,Chengkai Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文利用弯曲电容织物作为形状传感器，将曲面表面建模为非平面感知流形，每个扫描表示为该流形上的电容近场信号。提出一个多视角前馈重建模型，聚合已知传感器视角下的场信息并恢复物体形状。仿真与物理实验表明该方法在曲面传感表面上能实现鲁棒重建，为机器人近场几何感知提供新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数现有形状重建方法假设测量定义在平面感知域（如RGB图像或深度图），无法直接处理曲面传感表面。本文旨在利用弯曲电容织物的曲面特性，探索非平面感知流形上的形状重建方法，以突破传统平面传感的局限性。

Method: 将弯曲电容织物表面建模为非平面感知流形，每次扫描表示为由曲面电极布局与邻近物体几何交互诱导的电容近场。引入多视图前馈重建模型，在已知传感器视角下聚合多个电容场并重建观测物体形状。

Result: 在仿真和物理实验中均实现了从曲面传感表面获取的电容近场信号中稳健的重建效果，验证了该方法的有效性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了利用形体内置（embodied）的曲面电容传感实现机器人近场几何感知的可行性，为非平面传感表面的形状重建开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Most shape reconstruction methods assume measurements defined over planar sensing domains, such as RGB images or depth maps. In this paper, we use a curved capacitive textile as a shape sensor, treating its surface as a non-planar sensing manifold. Each scan is represented as a capacitive proximity field on this manifold, induced by the interaction between the curved electrode layout and nearby object geometry. We introduce a multi-view feedforward reconstruction model that aggregates these fields across known sensor views and recovers the observed object shape. Simulated and physical experiments demonstrate robust reconstruction from capacitive proximity signals acquired on curved sensing surfaces, pointing toward a new route to robotic near-field geometric awareness via embodied sensing.

</details>


### [14] [HorizonNet for visual terrain navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30471)
*Bertil Grelsson,Andreas Robinson,Michael Felsberg,Fahad Shahbaz Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于360度全景图像和数字高程模型（DEM）的无人水面艇（USV）位置估计方法。通过两级CNN分别进行粗略水平线检测（估计相机姿态）和像素级水平线提取，再通过MOSSE相关滤波器在傅里叶域中将提取的水平线与DEM数据进行匹配，以最大相关得分位置作为USV位置估计。现场试验表明该方法能达到GPS级别的定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在沿海地区或群岛环境中，无人水面艇（USV）的可靠位置估计至关重要。虽然GPS被广泛使用，但在拒止或退化环境下需要替代定位方案。利用自然场景中的地平线特征与DEM数据关联，可以实现无GPS的视觉定位，但现有方法在地平线提取精度和匹配效率上存在不足。

Method: 提出两级CNN架构：第一级CNN在360度全景图像中确定近似水平线并隐式估计相机俯仰角和横滚角；随后对全景图像进行畸变校正（warping），生成近似水平相机视角下的图像；第二级CNN在矫正后的图像中逐像素提取精确水平线；最后将提取的水平线与DEM数据在傅里叶域中通过MOSSE相关滤波器进行相关性匹配，以最大相关得分位置作为USV位置估计结果。

Result: 在群岛区域的现场试验（field trial）中进行了全面的实验验证，结果表明所提出方法能够达到GPS级别的定位精度，具有实际应用前景。

Conclusion: 基于全景图像地平线提取与DEM关联的方法能够在不依赖GPS的情况下实现USV的高精度位置估计，为拒止环境下的自主导航提供了可靠的视觉定位方案。

Abstract: This paper investigates the problem of position estimation of unmanned surface vessels (USVs) operating in coastal areas or in the archipelago. We propose a position estimation method where the horizon line is extracted in a 360 degree panoramic image around the USV. We design a CNN architecture to determine an approximate horizon line in the image and implicitly determine the camera orientation (the pitch and roll angles). The panoramic image is warped to compensate for the camera orientation and to generate an image from an approximately level camera. A second CNN architecture is designed to extract the pixelwise horizon line in the warped image. The extracted horizon line is correlated with digital elevation model (DEM) data in the Fourier domain using a MOSSE correlation filter. Finally, we determine the location of the maximum correlation score over the search area to estimate the position of the USV. Comprehensive experiments are performed in a field trial in the archipelago. Our approach provides promising results by achieving position estimates with GPS-level accuracy.

</details>


### [15] [Failure or Drift? Evaluating Monocular SLAM under Synthetic and Real-World Corruptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30690)
*Abhay Skaria Thomas,Shashank Agnihotri,Margret Keuper*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究单目SLAM在对抗性条件下的鲁棒性评估问题，发现传统特征法和学习型追踪器在图像退化下行为不同：学习型追踪器倾向于将灾难性轨迹丢失转化为持续漂移；更重要的是，受控合成腐化是否保持与真实条件一致的工程结论取决于其物理保真度——结构化雨雪代理能保持真实世界排序，而简单光照代理则不能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉SLAM通常在干净轨迹上进行评估，但实际部署失败往往源于恶劣天气、光照变化、模糊和传感器伪影等条件。受控合成腐化虽然能隔离这些因素进行压力测试，但如果合成测试无法得出与真实近似条件相同的工程结论，其价值就值得质疑。本文旨在检验合成腐化对单目SLAM评估的有效性这一核心问题。

Method: 作者在图像空间、几何感知和复合腐化三种类型下评估了经典特征法SLAM系统和两个学习型追踪器，并将其行为与4Seasons数据集的真实恶劣天气条件进行对比。评估方式不是简单地汇总为单一轨迹误差，而是将显式追踪失败与仍保持运行的系统累积的漂移分开分析。

Result: 结果表明：学习型追踪器在很大程度上将灾难性轨迹丢失替换为持续（有时严重的）漂移。更关键的是，学习系统的排序会随腐化的物理保真度变化——结构化雨雾代理能保持与真实世界一致的排序，而简单的光照代理则不能。

Conclusion: 合成腐化压力测试的有效性取决于其物理保真度，仅当合成条件与原意近似的真实条件产生相同工程排序时才可靠。这也强调了在鲁棒性评估中应区分追踪失败与累积漂移，而非仅依赖单一误差指标。

Abstract: Visual SLAM is commonly evaluated on clean trajectories, although deployment failures are often caused by adverse weather, illumination, blur, and sensor artifacts. Controlled corruptions are attractive because they isolate such factors, but a synthetic stress test is useful only when it leads to the same engineering conclusion as the condition it is intended to approximate. This work examines that question for monocular SLAM. We evaluate a classical feature-based system and two learned trackers under image-space, geometry-aware, and compound corruptions, and compare their behavior with adverse conditions from 4Seasons. Rather than reducing robustness to a single trajectory error, the evaluation separates explicit tracking failure from drift accumulated by methods that remain active. The results show that learned trackers largely replace catastrophic loss with sustained, and sometimes severe, drift. More importantly, the apparent ordering of the learned systems changes with the physical fidelity of the corruption: structured rain and fog proxies preserve the real-world ordering, whereas a simple illumination proxy does not. Code is available at: https://github.com/abhaythomas/master_thesis_vslamlab_robustness.

</details>


### [16] [Driving on Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31029)
*Christian Löwens,Thorben Funke,Alexandru Paul Condurache*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MemoryDrivoR方法，通过移除自动驾驶模型的相机输入并替换为同一位置的既往驾驶记忆，来检验当前端到端驾驶基准（如NAVSIM）的得分是否真正反映模型对当前动态场景的反应能力。结果发现，在不观察当前场景的情况下，仅靠记忆在NAVSIM上几乎能达到甚至超越领先端到端方法的性能，表明NAVSIM高分并不需要规划器对当前交通场景做出反应；而在Bench2Drive和RealEngine上，记忆驱动的性能则会大幅下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前驾驶基准（如NAVSIM、Bench2Drive）采用更丰富的仿真指标来评估模型的安全与合规驾驶能力，但高分数是否真正源自模型对动态场景的实时理解与反应尚不明确。作者希望通过一种"记忆替代视觉"的探针实验，揭示基准得分中多大程度是来自对当前动态场景的反应，从而检验基准的有效性。

Method: 作者移除模型的相机输入，将其替换为同一地理位置此前驾驶中提取的记忆（涵盖道路布局、位置相关规律等静态场景信息，但不包含当前交通状态）。通过对比记忆驱动与真实视觉输入下的性能，评估各基准对动态场景反应能力的依赖程度。

Result: 在NAVSIM基准上，纯记忆驱动几乎达到甚至超过领先端到端方法的性能，说明高分并不需要实时感知当前交通场景。但在Bench2Drive和RealEngine上，记忆驱动会导致性能大幅下降，说明该效应具有基准依赖性。

Conclusion: NAVSIM基准的得分应谨慎解读，因为它可能无法充分体现模型对当前动态场景的反应能力。记忆驱动方法可作为基准有效性诊断的工具，且该效应因基准而异，需要在设计驾驶基准时考虑对动态场景感知的敏感度。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving models plan future trajectories from raw sensor input. While earlier driving benchmarks often measured deviation from the human trajectory, current benchmarks such as NAVSIM and Bench2Drive evaluate models with richer simulation-based metrics intended to capture safe and compliant driving. A high benchmark score should reflect that a model can understand the scene in front of it and act accordingly. But how much of that score specifically comes from reacting to the dynamic part of that scene?
  To probe this, we remove a model's camera input and replace it with memories from prior drives at the same location. The retrieved memories can provide persistent scene information, including road layout and location-conditioned regularities, but not the current traffic state. Surprisingly, memory is nearly sufficient on NAVSIM, reaching or even exceeding the performance of leading end-to-end methods without actually observing the evaluated scene. Our results suggest that a high NAVSIM score does not require a planner to react to the current traffic scene and should be treated with caution. This effect is benchmark-dependent: driving from memory causes substantially larger performance drops on Bench2Drive and RealEngine. We provide our code at https://github.com/boschresearch/MemoryDrivoR .

</details>


### [17] [Uncertainty-Aware Multimodal Anti-UAV Detection via Evidential Fusion and Conflict-Discounted Belief Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29235)
*Sharanda Suttorp,Seyed Sahand Mohammadi Ziabari,Ali Mohammed Mansour Alsahag*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将EDTC扩展至多模态RGB-热成像反UAV感知，提出折扣信念融合（DBF）将模态间冲突转化为不确定性质量。在Anti-UAV基准上多模态融合优于单流（测试准确率0.670 vs 0.604/0.598），实时速度≥38 FPS。然而DBF与无折扣平均在经验上不可区分，因基准以存在性为主、冲突极低，导致折扣机制失效。融合不确定性校准良好（ECE 0.057），但作为定位失败检测器不如空间方差（AUROC 0.626 vs 0.739）。该空结果是结构性的：基准的近乎全阳性与漏检编码抑制了模态间冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态反UAV系统对RGB和热成像流进行确定性融合，未建模预测不确定性，无法在流不一致时表达怀疑。尽管EDTC已利用EDL进行单模态热成像感知，但跨模态证据融合尚未被研究。

Method: 通过折扣信念融合（DBF）将多模态RGB-热成像感知中的模态间冲突转化为不确定性质量，再聚合各流意见；边界框通过选择不确定性更低的模态来解析。

Result: 多模态融合在Anti-UAV基准上优于任一单流（测试准确率0.670 vs IR 0.604、RGB 0.598），实时速度≥38 FPS；融合不确定性校准良好（ECE 0.057），但定位失败检测AUROC为0.626，低于空间方差的0.739；DBF与无折扣平均不可区分，模态间冲突接近零。

Conclusion: 该空结果是结构性的：基准的近乎普遍存在和漏检的无效编码共同抑制了模态间冲突，这一诊断界定了冲突感知融合能带来可测量收益的范围。

Abstract: Anti-UAV perception systems must remain reliable when sensor streams degrade under occlusion, fast motion, or modality-specific failure. Existing multimodal anti-UAV systems fuse RGB and thermal streams deterministically, without modeling predictive uncertainty, and cannot express doubt when streams disagree. Evidential Deep Learning (EDL) produces calibrated per-class uncertainty in a single forward pass. EDTC already exploits this for thermal-only perception, yet cross-modal evidential fusion remains unaddressed.
  This paper extends EDTC to multimodal RGB-Thermal perception via Discounted Belief Fusion (DBF), which converts inter-modal conflict into uncertainty mass before aggregating stream opinions. Bounding boxes are resolved by selecting the lower-uncertainty modality. On the Anti-UAV benchmark, multimodal fusion consistently outperforms either single stream (test Acc 0.670 vs. 0.604 IR, 0.598 RGB) at real-time speed (at least 38 FPS). However, DBF is empirically indistinguishable from undiscounted averaging: near-zero inter-modal conflict on this presence-dominated benchmark leaves the discounting step inert. The fused uncertainty is well-calibrated (ECE 0.057) yet expectedly a weaker localization failure detector than spatial variance (AUROC 0.626 vs. 0.739). The null result is structural: the benchmark's near-universal presence and vacuous miss-encoding jointly suppress inter-modal conflict, a diagnosis that delimits where conflict-aware fusion provides measurable benefit.

</details>


### [18] [RAGDiffusion++: From Macro-Retrieval to Micro-Fidelity Alignment for Garment Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29280)
*Yuhan Li,Xianfeng Tan,Fangao Zeng,Wenxiang Shang,Pipei Huang,Hao Zhou,Zhiyu Jin,Wenjun Zhang,Bingbing Ni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AR-GRPO策略，通过强化学习重塑流模型的采样分布，配合大规模数据集和属性感知奖励模型，解决服装资产生成中高频纹理细节丢失与奖励模型被利用的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的服装资产生成任务（将正面平铺服装图像恢复为真实世界场景）要求兼顾宏观拓扑准确性与微观物理保真度。现有工作RAGDiffusion解决了宏观结构幻觉问题，但微观纹理真实性仍是瓶颈。论文将其形式化为"高频轨迹坍缩"：监督微调收敛到训练分布的均值（以平滑低频纹理为主），导致高频图案（如织物纹理、精细logo）几乎无法采样。此外，直接应用强化学习后训练会触发"伪影攻击"（Artifact Hacking），模型利用通用奖励模型中的语义偏见生成欺骗性的棋盘噪声。

Method: 论文提出三大支柱：1) 内在能力增强：构建27,725对高复杂度服装数据集STGarment-Plus，并升级为双图像流FLUX架构；2) 感知奖励：训练属性感知奖励模型Garment-RM（基于50万张图像的细粒度对比学习，人类偏好准确率达84.67%）；3) 防攻击机制：提出对抗正则化GRPO（AR-GRPO）策略，在RL采样轨迹中集成动态判别器，惩罚伪影同时丰富真实高频细节。

Result: Garment-RM在50万图像上训练，实现84.67%的人类偏好准确率。AR-GRPO策略有效防止奖励模型盲区被利用，同时提升了服装纹理的微观真实性。

Conclusion: 核心洞见在于：强化学习可以在准确奖励引导下从根本上重塑流模型的采样分布，提升高保真轨迹的概率，而对抗正则化可防止模型利用奖励模型的盲区。这一原理通过数据集、奖励模型和训练策略三方面协同实现，填补了工业级微纹理真实性的空白。

Abstract: Standard clothing asset generation---restoring forward-facing flat-lay garment images from diverse real-world contexts---holds immense commercial value yet demands both macroscopic topological accuracy and microscopic physical fidelity. Although our previous work RAGDiffusion effectively eradicated large-scale structural hallucinations via retrieval-augmented macro-constraints, achieving industrial-grade micro-texture realism remains an unsolved bottleneck. We formally identify this limitation as High-Frequency Trajectory Collapse: supervised fine-tuning (SFT) converges to the conditional mean of the training distribution, which is dominated by smooth, low-frequency textures, causing high-frequency patterns (e.g., fabric weaves, intricate logos) to become nearly un-sampleable. Naively applying Reinforcement Learning (RL) post-training further triggers Artifact Hacking, where models exploit semantic biases in generic reward models by generating deceptive checkerboard noise. Our key insight is that RL can fundamentally reshape the sampling distribution of flow models---elevating the probability of high-fidelity trajectories under accurate reward guidance---while adversarial regularization prevents exploitation of reward blind spots. Realizing this principle requires three prerequisites: (i)inherent capacity, established through a 27,725-pair high-complexity garment dataset (STGarment-Plus) and a Dual-Image-Stream FLUX architecture upgrade; (ii)perceptive reward, provided by a novel attribute-aware reward model (Garment-RM) trained on 500K images via fine-grained contrastive learning, achieving 84.67% human preference accuracy; and (iii)hacking prevention, enforced by our Adversarial-Regularized GRPO (AR-GRPO) strategy that integrates a dynamic discriminator into the RL sampling trajectory to penalize artifacts while enriching authentic high-frequency details.

</details>


### [19] [Think, Look, and Revise: Inconsistency-Aware Visual Self-Correction in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29374)
*Yu Cheng,Arushi Goel,Hakan Bilen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReVISE是一个框架，通过引入验证和动态错误恢复机制来增强工具增强的多模态推理，使MLLMs能够验证工具输出、在视觉不匹配时重构查询，并在外部工具不可靠时回退到内在推理，实验证明该方法在多个基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具增强的多模态推理方法虽然能集成外部工具（如目标检测、深度估计）来缓解复杂视觉任务中的感知瓶颈，但几乎没有验证工具输出的机制，限制了模型检测和从工具故障中恢复的能力。这一缺失导致推理过程可能在错误证据上持续累积，最终产生错误结果。

Method: ReVISE框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 一个精心策划的训练数据集，监督模型的反思行为，使模型能够验证工具衍生的证据、在视觉不匹配时重构查询、并在外部工具不可靠时回退到内在接地（intrinsic grounding）；(2) 基于强化学习的目标奖励机制，鼓励内部反思并惩罚空间错位（spatial misalignment）。

Result: 在多个基准上的实验表明，ReVISE相较于现有方法具有一致的性能提升，证明了错误检测与纠正在工具增强的多模态推理中的重要性。

Conclusion: 该工作凸显了工具故障验证与动态恢复的关键价值，为工具增强多模态推理提供了一个更稳健的范式，即模型不应盲目信任外部工具的输出，而应具备自我验证和灵活回退的能力。

Abstract: Tool-augmented multimodal reasoning integrates external tools (e.g., object detection, depth estimation) into multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to address perceptual bottlenecks in complex visual tasks. However, existing approaches rarely verify tool outputs, limiting their ability to detect and recover from tool failures. We propose ReVISE, a framework that equips MLLMs with verification and dynamic error recovery for tool-augmented reasoning. ReVISE introduces (1) a curated training dataset that supervises reflective behaviors, enabling models to validate tool-derived evidence, reformulate queries when visual mismatches arise, and fall back to intrinsic grounding when external tools are unreliable; and (2) a reinforcement learning based targeted rewards that encourage internal reflection and penalize spatial misalignment. Experiments on several benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements over existing methods, highlighting the importance of error detection and correction in tool-augmented multimodal reasoning.

</details>


### [20] [Text-Guided Diffusion-Based Adversarial Attacks on Chest X-Ray Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29456)
*Basudha Pal,Arjun Narayanan,Neha Ajith,Vikas R Bhat,Muhammad Umair*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于文本引导扩散模型的对抗性攻击框架，用于胸部X射线多疾病分类任务。该方法通过优化可学习的文本条件（冻结扩散生成器和目标分类器），利用所学图像先验而非像素级操作生成对抗样本，在多个分类器架构上均优于FGSM、PGD和Carlini-Wagner攻击，显著降低分类性能（AUROC降至0.3885-0.5646），同时保持较高图像保真度，且人眼无法察觉大部分对抗性修改。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有胸部X射线（CXR）模型鲁棒性评估主要依赖像素空间攻击（如FGSM、PGD、C&W），这类攻击施加数值约束的微小扰动，但可能不代表真实放射学中可能出现的图像变化。在多疾病CXR分类中，模型需同时评估多个重叠病理，像素级攻击的局限性尤为突出。因此需要探索更具临床可信度的生成式威胁模型，以揭示人在过度依赖像素空间攻击时可能忽略的模型脆弱性。

Method: 提出文本引导的扩散对抗攻击框架：通过在扩散生成过程中优化可学习的文本条件向量，同时保持扩散生成器和目标分类器参数冻结，使对抗样本通过学习到的图像先验生成，而非直接操作像素。框架在二分类（肺不张）和多疾病CXR分类任务中，对多种分类器架构进行评估，并与FGSM、PGD和Carlini-Wagner等经典攻击方法进行对比。

Result: 该框架在所有测试中一致产生最大的分类器性能退化：二分类AUROC降至0.3885-0.5646，多疾病设置下降至0.4441-0.4878。同时图像保真度优异（SSIM 0.9080, LPIPS 0.1670, FID 51.23）。重要的是，尽管模型预测发生显著变化，临床医生对95.9%的二分类和73.8%的多疾病对抗图像的判读保持不变，揭示了人类与机器判读之间的临床重要差异。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了像素空间攻击在评估医学AI鲁棒性方面的局限性：现有的对抗攻击基准可能低估了模型的真实脆弱性。生成式威胁模型（如文本引导扩散攻击）能在视觉和临床可信的图像变化下暴露模型故障，凸显了将医学AI鲁棒性评估扩展到像素空间攻击之外（向生成式威胁模型方向）的必要性，以确保临床安全部署。

Abstract: As artificial intelligence is increasingly integrated into chest X-ray (CXR) interpretation, triage, and clinical decision support, understanding its vulnerability to adversarial manipulation is critical for safe deployment. Existing robustness evaluations, however, predominantly rely on pixel-space attacks that introduce numerically constrained perturbations but may not represent plausible radiographic variation. This limitation is particularly important in multi-disease CXR classification, where models simultaneously evaluate multiple overlapping pathologies and adversarial failures may alter several diagnostic predictions. We propose a text-guided diffusion-based adversarial framework that optimizes learnable text conditioning while keeping the diffusion generator and target classifier frozen, enabling adversarial generation through a learned image prior rather than direct pixel manipulation. We evaluate the framework across multiple classifier architectures in both binary atelectasis and multi-disease CXR classification and compare it with FGSM, PGD, and Carlini-Wagner attacks. Our approach consistently produced the greatest degradation in classifier performance, reducing AUROC to 0.3885-0.5646 in binary classification and 0.4441-0.4878 in the multi-disease setting, while achieving superior image fidelity (SSIM 0.9080, LPIPS 0.1670, FID 51.23). Importantly, clinician interpretation remained unchanged for 95.9% of binary and 73.8% of multi-disease adversarial images despite substantial changes in model predictions. These findings reveal a clinically important discrepancy between human and machine interpretation and demonstrate the need to extend medical AI robustness evaluation beyond conventional pixel-space attacks toward generative threat models that can expose failures under visually and clinically plausible image variations.

</details>


### [21] [Seeing Through Extreme Visual Sparsity: Surface Understanding from a Single Random Visual Patch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29475)
*Sindhuja Penchala,Sudip Mittal,Noorbakhsh Amiri Golilarz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出稀疏表面理解框架（SSUF），一个统一的双任务学习框架，将四种预训练架构（ConvAE、ViT、Swin Transformer、MAE）适配用于表面重建和材质分类。在Touch-and-Go数据集上采用仅10%可见的稀疏观测协议进行评估。结果显示Swin Transformer在分类上最优（准确率89.21%），MAE在重建上最优（PSNR 16.06 dB），ViT在两者间取得最佳平衡，所有模型推理时间均小于5ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从不完整的视觉观测中识别表面材质是机器人感知和环境理解中的挑战性问题。作者旨在探索在严重视觉稀疏条件下，预训练架构能否同时支持表面重建与材质分类，并公平比较不同架构在这两个任务上的表现。

Method: 提出SSUF统一双任务学习框架，适配四种预训练架构（ConvAE、ViT、Swin Transformer、MAE）实现表面重建与材质分类的同步学习。采用10%可见度的稀疏观测协议（其余区域掩码）。为确保公平比较，为重建导向模型（ConvAE、MAE）添加分类头，为分类导向模型（ViT、Swin）添加重建解码器。评估指标包括重建质量（PSNR/SSIM）、分类性能（准确率/F1/ROC-AUC）、模型复杂度和推理效率。

Result: Swin Transformer在分类任务中最佳：准确率89.21%、F1分数0.8922、ROC-AUC 0.9813。MAE在重建任务中最佳：PSNR 16.06 dB、SSIM 0.4501。ViT在重建与分类之间取得最佳整体平衡。所有模型均实现实时推理（每张图像小于5ms）。

Conclusion: 预训练架构能够在严重视觉稀疏条件下支持材质识别任务，但准确的图像重建仍然具有挑战性。不同架构在重建和分类任务上展现出各自的优势，提示应根据具体任务需求选择合适的预训练架构。

Abstract: Surface material recognition from incomplete visual observations remains a challenging problem in robotic perception and environmental understanding. This paper discusses Sparse Surface Understanding Framework (SSUF), a unified dual-task learning framework that adapts four pretrained architectures-Convolutional Autoencoder (ConvAE), Vision Transformer (ViT), Swin Transformer, and Masked Autoencoder (MAE) for si-multaneous surface reconstruction and material classification. Experiments were conducted on the Touch-and-Go dataset using a sparse observation protocol in which only 10% of the original image remained visible while the remaining regions were masked. To enable a fair comparison, reconstruction-oriented models were extended with classification heads, whereas classification- oriented models were augmented with reconstruction decoders. The resulting architectures were assessed using reconstruction quality, classification performance, model complexity, and in-ference efficiency metrics. Experimental results revealed distinct strengths across the models. Swin Transformer achieved the best classification performance with an accuracy of 89.21%, an F1-score of 0.8922, and a ROC-AUC of 0.9813. In contrast, MAE produced the highest reconstruction scores among evaluated models, with a PSNR of 16.06 dB and an SSIM of 0.4501, while ViT provided the best overall balance between reconstruction and classification performance. Furthermore, all models achieved real-time inference, requiring less than 5 ms per image. Over-all, the results show that pretrained architectures can support material recognition under severe visual sparsity, while accurate image reconstruction remains challenging.

</details>


### [22] [GeoAgent: Evaluating VLM Geolocalization Through Embodied Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29483)
*Arka Mukherjee,Soham Roy,Kartikeya Trivedi,Shreya Ghosh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoAgent，一个基于智能体环境的基准测试，要求多模态代理在Street View环境中自主导航来逐步推理以完成图像地理定位任务。研究发现现代VLM在国家和大洲级别的预测表现良好，但在区域级别上挣扎；相比静态图像基线，智能体导航显著提升了准确率；同时发现模型在发达/发展中地区存在严重偏差，且不具备基于错误先验的自我修正能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像地理定位研究大多局限于静态图像的检索、分类和预测，未能忠实还原真实场景中需要自主探索环境后再做判断的任务本质。作者认为，真实的地理定位任务应涉及具身导航——多模态代理需要自主探索周边环境、收集观测信息后才能提交预测，因此需要一个新的基准来评估这一能力。

Method: 作者构建了GeoAgent基准，让智能体在Street View环境中自主导航，通过序列推理逐步细化地理定位预测。该基准要求代理在真实街道场景中移动和观察，结合视觉信息进行多轮推理，最终给出定位结果。同时与静态图像基线方法进行对比实验，分析不同前沿VLM架构在此任务上的表现。

Result: 现代VLM在区域级别难以辨别模式，但在国家和大陆级别预测上表现良好；相比静态图像基线，智能体导航显著提升了各已有度量下的准确率；模型在发达/发展中地区背景下存在严重偏差；模型在给定错误先验时自我改进能力较差。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了具身导航与地理空间推理任务的挑战，并指出当前VLM在细粒度地理定位、公平性和自我修正方面仍存在明显不足，为未来研究指明方向。

Abstract: Modern Vision-Language Models (VLMs) perform well above the human baseline in image geolocalization, a task critically important in disaster response, OSINT verification, and location privacy. However, most efforts to study AI behavior on the task remain limited to static image-based retrieval, classification, and predictions. We argue that faithful recreation of the task should involve embodied navigation, where a multimodal agent autonomously explores its surroundings to gather observations before submitting a prediction. To this end, we introduce \textbf{GeoAgent}, an agentic environment-based benchmark that requires agents to navigate Street View environments to refine their geolocalization through sequential reasoning. Our analysis shows that modern VLMs struggle to discern regional patterns while succeeding at country- and continent-level predictions. When compared to static image-based baselines, agentic navigation significantly improves accuracy across established metrics. We also note severe bias in a developed/developing region context across frontier model architectures and poor self-improvement capabilities given incorrect priors. Overall, our work establishes the challenges of embodied navigation and geospatial reasoning. We publicly release our code and the GeoAgent environment: https://geoagent-benchmark.github.io

</details>


### [23] [FuncRoom-Agent: Sequential Feed-Forward 3D Functional Indoor Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29519)
*Hao Feng,Zhi Zuo,MingJian Liang,Jingyu Hu,Xiaowei Hu,Liupengfei Wu,Dian Zhang,Guoxin Fang,Zhengzhe Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Function-Room Generation，一种以显式功能目标为导向的室内3D场景生成设置，通过递归领域特定语言（DSL）、顺序前馈场景构建框架和基于执行落地的过程奖励机制（ScenePRM），避免了耗时的大模型"生成-评估-修改"循环，实现了更快、功能更完整的室内场景生成，并在通用及功能导向的室内场景生成上均取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的室内3D场景生成方法主要追求视觉合理性，缺乏对显式功能目标的建模；而具备可控制的agentic和可执行方法虽提升了可控性，却依赖成本高昂的测试时"生成-评估-修正"循环，导致功能房间生成速度慢且计算开销大。

Method: 三个技术贡献：（1）设计递归DSL语言，以分层对象组合形式组织功能房间（房间结构→大件家具→支撑面/密集小物体），将房间表示为带显式几何和功能关系的分阶段可执行程序；（2）提出顺序前馈场景构建框架，将递归构建轨迹蒸馏为场景构建专家，推理时专家逐阶段生成可执行DSL代码，由确定性执行器直接实例化，无需教师代理、在线评论器或迭代修复；（3）提出ScenePRM，一种基于执行落地的过程奖励框架，通过功能、几何、关系及未来可构建性反馈的强化学习改进专家。

Result: 建立了功能导向的基准测试，在通用室内场景生成和功能房间生成两项任务上均取得最优性能（SOTA），在功能完整性、关系正确性、几何可执行性和生成效率方面均有更强表现。

Conclusion: 通过将功能房间生成形式化为递归可执行程序，并以执行落地奖励驱动强化学习，可以在无需昂贵迭代搜索的情况下获得更高效、功能更完整的室内场景生成，确立了功能导向生成作为室内场景生成新研究方向的可行性。

Abstract: We introduce Function-Room Generation, a new indoor 3D scene generation setting that creates rooms supporting explicit functional goals rather than merely visually plausible layouts. Existing agentic and executable methods improve controllability, but often depend on costly test-time generate--evaluate--revise loops, making functional room generation slow and computationally expensive. We address this challenge with three technical contributions. First, we design a recursive domain-specific language to effectively organize the hierarchical object compositions required by functional rooms, from room structure and major furniture to dense support-surface and nested small objects. It represents rooms as staged executable programs with explicit geometric and functional relations. Second, we propose a sequential feed-forward scene construction framework that distills recursive construction traces into a scene construction expert. At inference time, the expert writes executable DSL code stage by stage, and a deterministic executor directly instantiates each stage without teacher agents, online critics, or iterative repair. Third, we introduce ScenePRM, an execution-grounded process reward framework that improves the expert through reinforcement learning with functional, geometric, relational, and future-constructability feedback. We further establish a function-oriented benchmark and show state-of-the-art performance on both general indoor scene generation and function-room generation, achieving stronger functional completeness, relation correctness, geometric executability, and generation efficiency.

</details>


### [24] [InspectorGPT: A Comparative Reasoning Enhanced VLM for Comprehensive Industrial Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29783)
*Weifei Chen,Honghao Zhang,Zhiyuan You,Xinyi Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出InspectorGPT，一个基于比较推理的视觉语言模型框架，通过将查询图像与无缺陷参考图像进行对比来检测工业异常，并支持像素级分割。通过Chain-of-Thought微调和Group Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO）进行训练，并通过任务向量融合将分割分支与推理分支分离训练后再融合，在多个基准上实现了优越的多维性能和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统无监督工业异常检测依赖于建模正常特征分布，泛化能力有限；基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的零样本检测方法存在两个问题：一是推理导向的后训练可能导致异常判别能力崩溃（比基础VLM更差），二是只能提供文本决策或粗糙边界框，缺乏像素级分割能力。作者从人工检测原理出发（通过与无缺陷参考图像对比来识别异常），希望构建一个既能细粒度分割又能进行详细推理的统一框架。

Method: InspectorGPT以比较推理为核心：(1) 输入为正常参考图像和查询图像，模型通过对比识别差异并执行多种检测任务并附带详细推理；(2) 通过Chain-of-Thought（CoT）微调内化比较推理能力；(3) 使用Group Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO）配合定制、可验证的奖励进行强化训练；(4) 引入InspectorGPT-Seg分支实现像素级异常掩码，但发现分割监督和语义推理存在冲突（分割提升判别但削弱推理，联合训练难以平衡），因此将两个分支分离训练，再通过任务向量融合（task-vector fusion）进行组合。

Result: 大量实验表明InspectorGPT在多维性能上表现优越，并且能够泛化到未见过的基准数据集，验证了比较推理方法在综合工业检测中的有效性。

Conclusion: 比较推理是一种有效的工业异常检测范式，通过分离训练推理分支和分割分支再融合，可以同时获得强异常判别能力和详细语义推理能力，实现对未见类别的泛化以及像素级异常定位。

Abstract: Industrial anomaly detection is a critical component of modern manufacturing. Most traditional unsupervised methods rely on modelling normal feature distributions, inherently limiting generalization to unknown categories. To improve generalizability, some recent methods incorporate vision-language models (VLMs) for zero-shot detection via text prompts. However, we observe that reasoning-oriented post-training can cause anomaly discrimination to collapse, with some fine-tuned models performing worse than their base VLMs. Existing methods also provide only textual decisions or coarse boxes, without pixel-level segmentation. A more explicit detection principle comes from human inspection: anomalies are identified by comparing a query image with a defect-free reference. Inspired by this, we propose InspectorGPT, a VLM framework centered on comparative reasoning. Given a normal reference and a query image, InspectorGPT compares them to identify discrepancies and perform multiple inspection tasks with detailed reasoning. We internalize this capability through Chain-of-Thought (CoT) fine-tuning and Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with tailored, verifiable rewards. We further introduce InspectorGPT-Seg for pixel-level anomaly masks. Segmentation supervision improves anomaly discrimination but weakens semantic reasoning, while joint training fails to balance them. We therefore train the two branches separately and combine them through task-vector fusion. Extensive experiments demonstrate superior multi-dimensional performance and generalization to unseen benchmarks, validating comparative reasoning for comprehensive industrial inspection.

</details>


### [25] [Beyond Global Realism: Virtual Try-On Evaluation and Optimization with Dimension-wise Garment Fidelity Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29804)
*Kaidong Zhang,Yukang Ding,Xiaoyu Liu,Ying Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DAT框架，将虚拟试衣（VTON）的服装一致性评估分解为七个可解释维度（轮廓、颜色、领口袖型、主要装饰与结构、材质纹理、细节保真度、logo保留），通过两阶段学习（50K弱监督+10K高质量多模型投票标注）训练专用评估模型，并采用加权交叉熵损失处理标签不平衡。该评估模型还可作为强化学习奖励信号集成到Qwen-Image-Edit优化中，实现自适应维度加权奖励聚合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估指标（PSNR、SSIM、KID、FID）无法有效衡量生成服装与参考服装之间的一致性，尤其难以捕捉服装保真度的多维度特征，亟需更精细、可解释的评估框架。

Method: 1) 将服装一致性分解为七个可解释维度，每个维度建模为离散属性级预测任务；2) 采用两阶段学习范式：50K弱监督+10K多模型投票的高质量标注精调；3) 使用加权交叉熵损失缓解各评估维度的严重标签不平衡；4) 将评估模型集成到Qwen-Image-Edit的强化学习优化中，对维度级奖励进行自适应聚合以强调优化不足的方面。

Result: DAT（8B参数）在均衡准确率、SROCC和PLCC指标上达到SOTA性能，超越Gemini-3.1、Qwen3.7-plus和GPT-5.5等强专有模型，同时作为有效的奖励引导信号提升VTON生成质量。

Conclusion: DAT不仅是一种更精细、可解释的VTON评估框架，还能作为强化学习中的优化信号反哺生成模型，实现评估与生成的良性闭环。

Abstract: Virtual try-on (VTON) requires not only realistic generation but also faithful preservation of garment characteristics. However, existing evaluation metrics such as PSNR, SSIM, KID and FID struggle to measure the consistency between the generated and reference garments, particularly in capturing the multi-dimensional characteristics of garment fidelity. To address this, we propose DAT: a Dimension-wise Assessment framework for virtual Try-on, which decomposes garment consistency into seven interpretable dimensions: silhouette, color, neckline and sleeve shape, major decoration and structure, material texture, fine-detail fidelity, and logo preservation, each formulated as a discrete attribute-level prediction task. To train this specialized assessment model, we adopt a two-stage learning paradigm comprising large-scale weak supervision on 50K samples, followed by refinement on 10K higher-quality annotations obtained via multi-model voting. Furthermore, we employ weighted cross-entropy loss to mitigate the severe label imbalance inherent across evaluation dimensions. Beyond its role as an evaluation framework, the assessment model can be integrated into reinforcement learning optimization of Qwen-Image-Edit for VTON, where dimension-wise rewards are adaptively aggregated to emphasize under-optimized aspects during training. Experimental results show that our method (8B parameters) achieves state-of-the-art performance in terms of balanced accuracy, SROCC, and PLCC, outperforming strong proprietary models such as Gemini-3.1, Qwen3.7-plus, and GPT-5.5, while also serving as an effective optimization signal for reward-guided VTON generation

</details>


### [26] [OptiGeo: Efficient Monocular Geometry for Embodied Perception in Optically Challenging Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29881)
*Muxin Liu,Tianbo Liu,Jing Xia,Xiaoyang Lyu,Xiaoshan Wu,Bo Wang,Peng Dai,Zhongrui Wang,Shaoshuai Shi,Xiaojuan Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OptiGeo是一个偏差感知训练框架，通过在基座模型训练中引入干净几何教师和残差修剪对齐来修复光学挑战区域（透明、反射、镜面）中的传感器监督偏差，仅用30M参数即可超越300M级单目模型和十亿级多视图基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目深度估计在开放域泛化上已很强大，但在透明、反射和镜面环境中传感器常产生缺失或有偏的深度数据，现有方法依赖场景特定预处理、辅助模块或后处理微调，导致架构冗余并使通用几何模型过度特化于狭窄光学场景。作者将这一问题重新定位为基座模型训练中的局部失败模式，识别出传感器引起的监督偏差是关键瓶颈。

Method: OptiGeo是一个偏差感知训练框架，核心包含两个组件：(1) 使用干净几何教师模型来恢复有偏的真实深度监督；(2) 采用残差修剪对齐机制修正监督信号。方法上，作者将面向透明目标的渲染重新定义为一种紧凑的干净光学几何来源，而非大规模领域特定微调数据集。模型仅需一个小型目标渲染集即可学习透明物体的几何结构，纠正真实传感器无法可靠监督的局部几何畸变。

Result: 尽管只有30M参数，OptiGeo在透明场景基准上超越了300M规模的单目模型和十亿级的多视图基线，同时在通用零样本深度估计和边界锐度上保持竞争力。真实世界导航测试验证了其在光学挑战场景中作为高效感知模块的实用性。

Conclusion: OptiGeo证明了将光学失败视为基座模型训练中的局部监督偏差问题是可行的，通过小规模干净渲染数据即可高效修正传感器偏差，避免了架构冗余和过度特化，为光学挑战场景中的可靠单目深度估计提供了轻量且可部署的解决方案。

Abstract: Monocular depth estimation has achieved strong open-domain generalization, yet reliable robotic deployment remains difficult in transparent, reflective, and specular environments, where depth sensors often produce missing or biased depth. Existing methods often handle such optical failures with scene-specific preprocessing, auxiliary modules, or post-hoc fine-tuning. While effective in constrained settings, these designs increase architectural redundancy and can over-specialize general geometry models to narrow optical scenarios. We revisit this problem as a localized failure mode within base-model training and identify sensor-induced supervision bias as a key bottleneck: models inherit sensor failure patterns from biased real-depth supervision in optically challenging regions. We then introduce OptiGeo, a bias-aware training framework that rehabilitates biased real supervision using a clean-geometry teacher and residual-trimmed alignment. We redefine transparency-targeted rendering as a compact source of clean optical geometry, rather than a large domain-specific fine-tuning set. With only a small targeted rendering set, OptiGeo learns the geometric structure of transparent objects and regions, correcting local geometry distortions that real sensors cannot reliably supervise. Despite only 30M parameters, OptiGeo outperforms substantially larger 300M-scale monocular models and billion-scale multi-view baselines on transparent-scene benchmarks, while remaining competitive on general zero-shot depth and boundary sharpness. Real-world navigation cases further validate its practicality as an efficient perception module in optically challenging scenes.

</details>


### [27] [Off-Manifold Refinement: Guiding Video Generators with a Frozen World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29904)
*Hai Nguyen-Truong,Tuan-Anh Vu,Dang Huynh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Off-Manifold Refinement (OMR)方法，一种推理时的物理一致性改进技术。OMR在单次采样轨迹中，通过向生成器速度注入基于V-JEPA 2.1世界模型自监督损失的梯度来直接纠正物理违反行为，无需多次解码/重编码或后训练。在VideoPhy-2上，OMR将联合语义遵循与物理常识指标从47.0%提升至52.0%（绝对提升5.0pp），且运行时间仅为基线1.71倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成器在物理动力学上存在系统性失败（物体漂浮、轨迹违反重力、接触消失），而标准去噪和流匹配目标是拟合视觉数据分布，并不显式惩罚物理违反。现有的物理一致性改进方法代价高昂：候选选择方法需要生成和评分多个视频，基于梯度的世界模型引导需反复解码和重编码，生成器内部精炼引入扰动和重去噪循环，后训练则需要大量精心策展的数据和额外优化。

Method: OMR在推理阶段向单个采样轨迹注入世界模型反馈。在预定中间ODE步骤中，将自适应器空间的V-JEPA 2.1惊讶能量的梯度引入生成器速度。这种外部修正将隐变量偏离未修正的采样轨迹，移向冻结预测器认为物理上更合理的区域。一个小型训练的潜变量到嵌入适配器使推理时梯度可计算，视频生成器和世界模型均保持冻结。

Result: 在固定的400提示VideoPhy-2详细子集上，OMR将联合语义遵循与物理常识指标从基线Wan2.2-T2V-A14B的47.0%提升到52.0%（绝对提升5.0个百分点，相对提升10.6%）。在50提示效率子集上，OMR的运行时间仅为基线的1.71倍，而非奖励/搜索替代方案的乘法成本。

Conclusion: OMR通过直接将世界模型反馈注入单一采样轨迹，在保持推理成本相对较低（1.71倍基线而非倍数级增长）的同时实现了显著的物理一致性改进，是一种无需修改生成器或世界模型、无需重复解码/编码的高效物理约束方法。

Abstract: Modern video generators routinely fail at physical dynamics: objects float, trajectories violate gravity, contacts vanish. Standard denoising and flow-matching objectives fit visual data distributions but do not explicitly penalize such physical violations. Existing remedies can improve physical consistency, but typically add substantial inference or training cost. Candidate-selection methods generate and score multiple videos, while gradient-based world-model guidance repeatedly decodes and re-encodes intermediate estimates. Generator-internal refinement adds perturbation and re-denoising loops, whereas post-training requires curated data and additional optimization. We propose Off-Manifold Refinement (OMR), an inference-time method that instead injects world-model feedback directly into a single sampling trajectory. During scheduled middle ODE steps, we augment the generator velocity with the gradient of an adapter-space V-JEPA 2.1 surprise energy. This external correction can move the latent away from the uncorrected sampling trajectory and toward regions ranked as more physically plausible by the frozen predictor, after which the generator continues rendering from the corrected state. A small trained latent-to-embedding adapter keeps the gradient tractable at inference, and both the video generator and the world model remain frozen. On our fixed 400-prompt VideoPhy-2 detailed subset, OMR lifts the joint Semantic-Adherence-and-Physical-Commonsense metric from 47.0% to 52.0% (+5.0pp absolute, +10.6% relative) over the base Wan2.2-T2V-A14B sampler. On a separate fixed 50-prompt efficiency subset, it requires $1.71 \times$ the base runtime rather than the multiplicative cost of reward/search alternatives. Project page: https://itruonghai.github.io/omr.

</details>


### [28] [Matrix-Game 3.5: Enhancing Real-Time Streaming Interactive World Models with Patch Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29910)
*Runjia Qian,Zile Wang,Jihai Zhang,Kai Zou,Wei Yu,Jiaxing Li,Zexiang Liu,Yaokun Li,Fei Kang,Kaichen Huang,Mengyin An,Haobo Zhang,Biao Jiang,Jiahua Wang,Haofeng Sun,Yang Liu,Yangguang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Matrix-Game 3.5是一个面向实时交互式世界生成的框架，通过统一几何感知记忆框架、静态-动态解耦世界表示和两阶段渐进式实时蒸馏三大改进，实现了分钟级长时程、几何一致且支持精确相机控制的实时交互生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式世界模型旨在将视频生成从离线片段合成扩展为持久模拟交互式虚拟世界，但现有方法难以同时保持场景几何、动态一致性和相机控制，同时支持实时自回归生成。

Method: 提出三项关键改进：(1) 统一几何感知记忆框架，包含patch-memory和tiled-PRoPE组件，通过显式3D patch检索与投影相机条件实现几何一致相机控制和长时程场景记忆，无额外可学习参数；(2) 静态-动态解耦世界表示，分别建模静态场景几何与动态主体，保持几何一致性和主体身份；(3) 两阶段渐进式实时蒸馏框架，通过Perceptual Flow Matching和基于课程学习的Self-Rollout DMD将双向扩散模型转换为少步因果生成器，实现分钟级实时交互生成。

Result: 在涵盖Unreal仿真环境、开放世界游戏和互联网视频的统一训练语料上，Matrix-Game 3.5在长时程场景召回、精确相机控制、主体一致性、提示词驱动世界生成以及稳定实时开放世界交互方面均取得强性能。

Conclusion: Matrix-Game 3.5通过几何感知记忆与解耦表示相结合的方式，显著推进了实时交互式世界生成在几何一致性和长时程一致性方面的能力，为游戏、机器人、具身智能和XR等应用提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: Interactive world models extend video generation from offline clip synthesis toward persistent simulation of interactive virtual worlds, enabling applications in games, robotics, embodied agents, and XR. Achieving stable long-horizon interactive generation, however, remains challenging, as the model must simultaneously preserve scene geometry, dynamic consistency, and camera control while supporting real-time autoregressive generation. Building upon Matrix-Game 3.0, we present Matrix-Game 3.5, as shown in Figure 1, which advances real-time interactive world generation toward geometry-aware and long-horizon consistent simulation through three key improvements. First, we propose a unified geometry-aware memory framework, whose patch-memory and tiled-PRoPE components introduce no additional learnable parameters, combining explicit 3D patch retrieval with projective camera conditioning to enable geometry-consistent camera control and faithful long-horizon scene recall. Second, we introduce a static-dynamic disentangled world representation that separately models static scene geometry and dynamic subjects, preserving both geometric consistency and subject identity throughout long-horizon generation. Third, we develop a two-stage progressive real-time distillation framework that converts a bidirectional diffusion model into a few-step causal generator through Perceptual Flow Matching and curriculum based Self-Rollout DMD, enabling minute-long real-time interactive generation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that, with a unified training corpus spanning Unreal simulation environments, open-world games, and internet videos, MatrixGame 3.5 achieves strong performance in long-horizon scene recall, precise camera control, subject consistency, prompt-driven world generation, and stable real-time open-world interaction.

</details>


### [29] [Towards Continual Test-Time Adaptation of Vision-Language Models in Open-Vocabulary Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29923)
*Chandler Timm C. Doloriel,Yunbei Zhang,Sarthak Kumar Maharana,Muhammad Salman Siddiqui,Tor Kristian Stevik,Fadi Al Machot,Kristian Hovde Liland,Habib Ullah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DAF（Diversify, Anchor, Filter）框架，通过边际多样性损失、跨模态锚定一致性损失和特征显著性过滤，稳定开放词汇语义分割在持续测试时分布偏移下的视觉-语言对齐，在多个数据集上显著优于熵最小化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放词汇语义分割依赖视觉-语言对齐来识别任意文本定义的类别，但该对齐在持续的测试时分布偏移下非常脆弱。诊断分析发现熵最小化会导致补丁级类别坍缩、持续更新会侵蚀视觉-语言对齐、低偏移样本产生的冗余梯度浪费计算资源。

Method: 提出DAF稳定框架，包含三个组件：(1) 边际多样性损失（marginal diversity loss），抵抗熵最小化导致的类别坍缩；(2) 跨模态锚定一致性损失（cross-modal anchor consistency loss），通过冻结源模型约束特征漂移；(3) 特征显著性过滤（feature salience filtering），跳过低价值反向传播以抵消源锚定的计算开销。

Result: 在五个数据集（自然场景、自动驾驶、水下影像、遥感）及其损坏变体上评估。相比熵最小化坍缩的情况，DAF保持稳定：在Pascal VOC20-C上mIoU提升超8点，LoveDA上超9点，Foggy Cityscapes上超3点；对激进自适应和学习率选择具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DAF框架有效解决了开放词汇语义分割在持续测试时分布偏移下的稳定性问题，通过多样性和锚定机制防止类别坍缩和特征漂移，同时以过滤策略控制计算开销，显著优于基线方法。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary semantic segmentation (OVSS) relies on vision-language alignment to recognize arbitrary text-defined categories, yet this alignment is fragile under continual test-time distribution shift. Our diagnostic analysis reveals that entropy minimization drives patch-level class collapse, continual updates erode vision-language alignment, and redundant gradients from low-shift samples waste computation. We propose Diversify, Anchor, and Filter (DAF), a stabilization framework that augments entropy-based adaptation with a marginal diversity loss that resists collapse, a cross-modal anchor consistency loss that constrains feature drift relative to a frozen source model, and feature salience filtering that skips low-value backward passes to offset part of the source-anchor overhead. We evaluate on five datasets spanning natural scenes, autonomous driving, underwater imagery, and remote sensing with their corrupted variants. Across the evaluated continual shifts, DAF remains stable where entropy minimization collapses, improving mIoU by over 8 points on Pascal VOC20-C, over 9 points on LoveDA, and over 3 points on Foggy Cityscapes compared to the source model, and is robust to aggressive adaptation and learning rate choices.

</details>


### [30] [Discrete Diffusion Bridges for Spatiotemporally Aligned Image Translation and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29997)
*Xing Xie,Jiawei Liu,Shijun Zhou,Huijie Fan,Zhi Han,Yandong Tang,Liangqiong Qu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出离散扩散桥（DDB）框架，通过混合吸收机制与信息引导噪声调度，解决标准离散扩散在图像翻译和生成中的时空错位问题，实现高效且高质量的图像语义编辑与结构翻译。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准离散扩散在图像翻译和生成中存在根本性的时空错位问题：空间上，纯掩码（pure mask）状态作为吸收态完全丢弃了源图像丰富的结构先验；时间上，随机掩码顺序与推理时的"先易后难"（easy-first, hard-last）解码机制本质矛盾。这导致模型难以在保留源图结构的同时完成语义变换。

Method: DDB构建域间直接且高效的轨迹。空间上，提出混合吸收机制（hybrid absorption），将吸收态重新定义为掩码与源token的随机混合，从而将源先验作为空间锚点注入潜在空间；时间上，设计信息引导的噪声调度（information-guided noise schedule），通过量化语义变化来优先在较早时间步破坏高信息区域，使模型利用不变区域的稳健上下文来学习解决困难的语义变化。

Result: 大量实验验证了框架在多种生成范式中的通用性和鲁棒性。DDB在文本引导的语义操作与纯结构图像翻译中有效平衡了编辑对齐与结构保真度，同时在文本到图像生成中具有天然互补性，并在极低采样步数下保证稳健的高质量解码。

Conclusion: DDB通过混合吸收与信息引导调度两大创新，从空间和时间两个维度系统地解决了离散扩散中的错位问题，为图像翻译与生成提供了通用且高效的框架，并已开源代码与模型。

Abstract: We propose Discrete Diffusion Bridges (DDB), a novel framework designed to resolve the fundamental spatiotemporal misalignment of standard discrete diffusion in image translation and generation. By corrupting data into a pure mask state via a random schedule, the conventional forward process induces a twofold misalignment: spatially, this pure-mask destination entirely discards the rich structural priors of the source image; temporally, the random masking order inherently contradicts the ``easy-first, hard-last'' decoding mechanism used during inference. To address this, DDB constructs a direct and efficient trajectory between domains. Spatially, we introduce a hybrid absorption mechanism that redefines the absorbing state to a stochastic mixture of mask and source tokens, effectively injecting source prior as spatial anchors into the latent space. Temporally, we design an information-guided noise schedule that quantifies semantic variation to prioritize the corruption of high-information regions at earlier timesteps. This ensures the model learns to resolve difficult semantic changes using robust context from invariant regions. Extensive experiments validate the versatility and robustness of our framework across diverse generative paradigms. DDB effectively balances edit alignment with structural fidelity across both text-guided semantic manipulation and pure structural image translation, while inherently complementing text-to-image generation and guaranteeing robust high-quality decoding under extremely low sampling steps. Code and models are available at \href{https://github.com/HKU-HealthAI/DDB}{https://github.com/HKU-HealthAI/DDB}.

</details>


### [31] [Aligning Multi-Trajectory Supervision with Policy Optimization for VLA Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30122)
*Tian Zhang,Zhuo Huang,Hongrui Ye,Yu Wu,Zengmao Wang,Kaixuan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种将多轨迹监督与策略优化对齐的VLA驾驶框架，通过可行域约束增强轨迹、Pareto最优性筛选轨迹、可行性优先优势分配与动态蒸馏机制，在NAVSIM v1/v2上取得91.4 PDMS和89.1 EPDMS，并恢复440/658个失败场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA驾驶方法将多轨迹模仿学习与GRPO结合，轨迹选择对最终性能至关重要。然而，一些高分但不可行的轨迹虽能提升模仿效果，却会在GRPO阶段产生与当前策略可行行为分布不一致的优势估计，驱动策略远离安全合规行为。

Method: 1) 将增强轨迹约束在真值可行域的邻近流形上，替代传统聚合评分，采用Pareto最优性准则仅保留非支配候选轨迹，从源头过滤冲突样本；2) 提出可行性优先优势分配，根据每个rollout组的可行性构成自适应调整Pareto信用，对完全不可行的组引导至安全参考；3) 引入动态蒸馏机制，跨精炼轮次更新教师轨迹，持续传递有效监督。

Result: 在NAVSIM v1和v2上，单轨迹推理下分别达到91.4 PDMS和89.1 EPDMS，恢复658个初始失败场景中的440个，比原始GRPO基线高11.1%。

Conclusion: 通过从源头过滤不可行轨迹、自适应优势分配和动态蒸馏三个互补机制，实现了多轨迹监督与策略优化的有效对齐，将扩展监督的优势逐步转化为策略改进，显著提升了驾驶性能和安全恢复能力。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) driving methods increasingly combine multi-trajectory imitation learning with group-relative policy optimization (GRPO), making trajectory selection critical to final performance. However, some high-scoring trajectories that improve imitation can degrade subsequent GRPO by inducing advantage estimates misaligned with the current policy's feasible behavior distribution, driving updates away from safe and compliant behaviors. To address this, we propose a novel framework that aligns multi-trajectory supervision with policy optimization. To address the policy gradient bias induced by infeasible noisy trajectories outside the feasible region, augmented trajectories are constrained to a neighboring manifold of the ground-truth feasible region, and a Pareto-optimality criterion is adopted in place of the conventional aggregate score, retaining only non-dominated candidates and thereby filtering out conflicting samples at the source. To ensure that expanded trajectory supervision is effectively absorbed during policy optimization, we introduce two complementary mechanisms: feasibility-first advantage assignment and dynamic distillation. The former adapts Pareto credit to the feasibility composition of each rollout group and guides fully infeasible groups toward safe references. The latter updates teacher trajectories across refinement rounds to continually transfer useful supervision. Together, they progressively translate the benefits of expanded supervision into policy improvement. On NAVSIM v1 and v2, our method achieves 91.4 PDMS and 89.1 EPDMS, respectively, under single-trajectory inference, and recovers 440 of 658 initially failed scenes, 11.1\% higher than the original GRPO baseline.

</details>


### [32] [CedarCypress3D: an annotated UAV-LiDAR dataset of individual trees in planted cedar and cypress forests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30149)
*Katsuto Shimizu,Fumiaki Kitahara,Tomohiro Nishizono,Hideki Saito,Masayoshi Takahashi,Shingo Obata,Shunsuke Tei,Naoyuki Furuya,Tomoya Goto,Eiji Kodani,Yusuke Yamada*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍CedarCypress3D，一个在日本柳杉和日本扁柏人工林中采集的、人工标注的UAV-LiDAR数据集，包含两个地形特征不同的场地、34个圆形样地、1,627棵标注树木，并提供部分样地的地面LiDAR数据及语义标签，用于支持单木实例分割和语义分割研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于UAV-LiDAR的单木测量对森林清查、生态系统监测和可持续森林管理具有重要意义，但温带森林中公开可用的带标注UAV-LiDAR数据集非常有限，制约了点云单木分割等机器学习方法的开发和评估。

Method: 在日本柳杉（Cryptomeria japonica）和日本扁柏（Chamaecyparis obtusa）人工林中采集UAV-LiDAR点云，跨越两个地形特征不同的场地共34个圆形样地；通过野外样地调查测量1,627棵树，并将其与UAV-LiDAR点云中的对应树木进行人工标注匹配；对22个子集样地提供地面LiDAR点云，并对UAV-LiDAR数据中的树木点额外赋予语义标签（树干与非树干）。

Result: 生成了一个包含高质量人工标注的UAV-LiDAR数据集，涵盖多平台LiDAR数据（UAV与地面）及配套野外实测数据，数据公开可通过DOI访问（https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.22168721）。

Conclusion: CedarCypress3D为温带人工林中的单木实例分割和语义分割方法提供了高质量基准数据集，还可支持树木属性预测和多平台LiDAR分析等相关研究，填补了温带森林公开标注UAV-LiDAR数据集的空白。

Abstract: Individual tree measurements derived from Light Detection and Ranging (LiDAR) mounted on Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAV) provide valuable information for forest inventory, ecosystem monitoring, and sustainable forest management. Recent advancements in machine learning have increased the demand for annotated datasets to develop and evaluate point cloud-based approaches, especially for individual tree segmentation. However, publicly available annotated UAV-LiDAR datasets in temperate forests are limited. In this article, we present CedarCypress3D, a manually annotated UAV-LiDAR dataset collected in Japanese cedar (Cryptomeria japonica) and Japanese cypress (Chamaecyparis obtusa) plantations in Japan. The dataset consists of UAV-LiDAR point clouds and field survey measurements from 34 circular plots across two sites with different topographic characteristics, along with terrestrial LiDAR point clouds available for a subset of 22 plots. A total of 1,627 trees were measured in the census field survey and manually annotated to match the corresponding trees in the UAV-LiDAR point clouds. For the subset of plots with terrestrial LiDAR data, semantic labels (i.e., stem and non-stem) were additionally assigned to tree points in the UAV-LiDAR data. CedarCypress3D provides high-quality annotated UAV-LiDAR data for developing and evaluating individual tree instance segmentation and semantic segmentation methods in temperate planted forests. The dataset can also support research on tree attribute prediction and multi-platform LiDAR analysis. The dataset is publicly available at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.22168721.

</details>


### [33] [Real-Time Scene-Adaptive Tone Mapping for High-Dynamic Range Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30400)
*Gongzhe Li,Linwei Qiu,Peibei Cao,Fengying Xie,Xiangyang Ji,Qilin Sun*

Main category: cs.CV
