*Xuerui Su,Liya Guo,Qizhi Pei,Qipeng Guo,Zhongbo Tian,Lijun Wu,Kai Chen,Zun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于开发与评估专业学术智能体（InternReviewer和InternAdvocate）的综合框架，通过大规模学术数据集、arXiv检索工具以及基于统一目标指标和奖励系统的智能体强化学习（RL）范式来优化智能体，实验表明该框架能显著提升推理深度和引用准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成专业学术内容（如同行评审和反驳意见）需要领域推理与事实依据的紧密协同，现有方法在学术智能体的系统化训练和评估方面仍存在不足，需要一个全面的框架来支持此类智能体的开发。

Method: 1) 构建大规模高质量学术数据集；2) 集成高效arXiv检索工具实现主动证据收集；3) 采用由统一目标指标和奖励系统驱动的智能体强化学习（RL）范式进行优化；4) 采用多维度标准避免主观模型评判偏差，包括参考锚定的语义对齐、结构合规性，以及通过实时交互日志交叉核验引文以消除幻觉的严格验证机制。

Result: 实验结果表明，在该闭环框架内训练得到的智能体在推理深度和引用准确性方面均表现出显著提升。

Conclusion: 该闭环框架验证了智能体强化学习结合多维奖励系统与严格引文验证机制在学术智能体训练中的有效性，为专业学术内容生成领域提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Generating professional scholarly content, such as peer reviews and rebuttals, requires an intricate synergy between domain reasoning and factual grounding. This work presents a comprehensive framework for the development and evaluation of specialized scholarly agents, InternReviewer and InternAdvocate. We first establish a large-scale, high-quality scholarly dataset and integrate a high-efficiency arXiv retrieval tool to enable active evidence gathering. To optimize these agents, we implement an agentic Reinforcement Learning (RL) paradigm driven by a unified objective metric and reward system. This system avoids the biases of subjective model-based judging by employing multi-dimensional criteria, including reference-anchored semantic alignment, structural compliance, and a strict verification mechanism that cross-checks citations against real-time interaction logs to eliminate hallucinations. Experimental results demonstrate that agents trained within this closed-loop framework exhibit significant improvements in reasoning depth and citation accuracy.

</details>


### [47] [Preference Elicitation for Policy Optimization and Application to Aligning Heart Transplantation with Human Values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28620)
*Itai Zilberstein,Ioannis Anagnostides,Zachary W Sollie,Arman Kilic,Tuomas Sandholm*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段的偏好引导算法，通过在两两比较中学习切割平面来快速收缩属性权重空间并消除劣势区域，从而收敛到用户的线性效用函数，并将其应用于心脏移植分配策略优化，在用户研究中聚合社区一致的效用函数，最终获得接近最优（竞争比0.95）的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的偏好引导方法（如器官分配）通常让利益相关者比较算法的决策（如患者A vs 患者B），这种"决策层面"的方法混淆了手段与目的。作者希望直接对分配结果（outcome）进行偏好引导，学习效用函数以用于策略优化，从而更好地与人类价值观对齐。

Method: 提出两阶段偏好引导算法：第一阶段通过两两比较学习切割平面（cutting planes），快速收缩属性权重的可能空间，并通过消除被支配区域来热启动第二阶段；第二阶段可证明地收敛到用户的真实效用函数。算法针对线性效用函数设计，并应用于心脏移植分配问题，兼顾移植后结果、等待名单死亡率、地理便捷性和公平性等多竞争目标。

Result: 通过用户研究学习并聚合了社区一致的效用函数，并用于优化心脏移植策略。与事后最优（hindsight optimum）相比，现状策略的竞争比仅为0.54，而所提方法的竞争比达到0.95，接近最优，显著优于现有策略。

Conclusion: 直接在结果层面而非决策层面进行偏好引导，能够更有效地学习用户效用函数并优化策略。"本文的方法在心脏移植分配中实现了接近最优的性能，展示了直接结果级偏好引导在AI系统与人类价值观对齐方面的显著优势。

Abstract: Preference elicitation is essential for aligning AI systems with human values. Prior approaches (e.g., for organ allocation) often ask stakeholders to compare the decisions of an algorithm (e.g., patient A vs. patient B). Such a decision-level approach conflates the means with the ends. Instead, we elicit preferences directly over allocation outcomes to learn a utility function for policy optimization. We construct a novel preference elicitation algorithm for linear utilities that outperforms prior techniques in practice. Our algorithm has two phases. The first phase learns cutting planes through pairwise comparisons to rapidly shrink the space of possible attribute weights and warm-starts the second phase by eliminating dominated regions. The second phase then provably converges to the user's utility function. We apply our technique to heart transplant allocation where a policy must balance competing objectives such as post-transplant outcomes, waitlist mortality, geographic ease, and equity. Using our algorithm, we conduct a user study to learn and aggregate a community-aligned utility function, and use it to optimize heart transplant policies that are significantly better aligned with human values. Compared to the hindsight optimum, the status quo policy achieves a competitive ratio of just 0.54, while our method is near-optimal with a competitive ratio of 0.95.

</details>


### [48] [Efficient Geothermal Well-Control Optimization via Diffusion-Surrogate Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28791)
*Ruimin Dai,Guodong Chen,Randy Harsuko,Kunpeng Liu,Nori Nakata*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对增强型地热系统（EGS）实时决策中高维控制空间与昂贵高保真模拟的挑战，本文提出一种扩散代理引导的强化学习框架，利用条件扩散模型预测储层温度和压力场的演化，结合奖励模型构建代理环境，并与PPO算法集成实现高效的井控优化，在裂缝性EGS基准上验证了其性能与计算效率的兼顾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EGS的长期生产期涉及高维控制空间和大量耗时的高保真热液模拟，直接使用数值模拟器进行强化学习策略训练计算成本过高，亟需一种能降低对高保真模拟依赖的同时保持控制性能的高效决策方法。

Method: 提出扩散代理引导的强化学习框架：以储层温度和压力场为系统状态、注入速率为控制动作；用条件扩散模型构建代理环境以预测储层状态演化，并训练独立的奖励模型估计经济回报；将代理环境与PPO算法集成进行策略训练。

Result: 在裂缝性EGS基准上的实验表明：扩散代理能准确复现多控制阶段的储层状态演化；代理辅助的PPO策略在井控性能上与直接模拟器PPO及现有优化方法相当，同时显著减少了高保真模拟的调用次数。

Conclusion: 扩散模型构建的代理环境能够高效支持EGS井控优化的强化学习训练，在保证控制质量的同时大幅降低计算成本，证明了基于扩散代理的强化学习在 geothermal 井控优化中的潜力。

Abstract: Real-time decision-making for enhanced geothermal systems (EGS) is challenging because long-term production periods involve high-dimensional control spaces and a large number of time-consuming high-fidelity hydrothermal simulations. Reinforcement learning provides a natural framework for state-dependent sequential control, but direct policy training with numerical simulators is computationally expensive. To address this issue, we propose a diffusion-surrogate guided reinforcement learning framework for long-horizon EGS well-control optimization. The reservoir temperature and pressure fields are used as system states, while injection rates are selected as control actions. A learned surrogate environment is constructed using conditional diffusion models to predict the evolution of reservoir temperature and pressure fields and a separate reward model to estimate the corresponding economic return. The surrogate environment is then integrated with Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) for efficient policy training. Experiments on a fractured EGS benchmark show that the diffusion surrogate can accurately reproduce reservoir-state evolution over multiple control stages. The resulting surrogate-assisted PPO policy achieves competitive well-control performance compared with direct simulator-based PPO and existing optimization methods, while substantially reducing the dependence on expensive high-fidelity simulations. These results demonstrate the potential of diffusion-based surrogate environments for efficient reinforcement learning in geothermal well-control optimization.

</details>


### [49] [The Role of Network Topology and Opponent Information in Shaping Cooperation in Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28977)
*Seongho Son,Stephen Hailes,Mirco Musolesi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了在深度强化学习框架下，图拓扑结构如何影响人工智能体在两人迭代囚徒困境（IPD）中合作行为的涌现。实验发现，节点的邻居数量和平均路径长度是影响合作涌现的主要因素；合作伙伴选择能促进合作，而对手身份信息会阻碍合作策略的传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献多关注基于策略模仿的智能体适应模型，仅依赖累积收益来建模合作行为，缺乏对智能体通过深度强化学习自主习得合作策略的探索，以及不同图拓扑对该学习过程影响的研究。

Method: 将每个智能体表示为图中的一个节点，其邻居构成可交互的对手池。每个智能体使用深度强化学习学习玩两人迭代囚徒困境，并在每个回合中提供不同类型的对手信息（行动历史和对手身份）。在不同图拓扑下进行实验对比，分析各拓扑特征对合作涌现的影响。

Result: 实验结果表明，节点的邻居数量和平均路径长度是影响合作涌现的两个主要因素。此外，合作伙伴选择机制通过限制对手池的多样性促进互惠合作，而向智能体提供对手身份信息则阻碍了合作策略的扩散。

Conclusion: 图拓扑结构（尤其是邻居数量与平均路径长度）对基于深度强化学习的合作涌现具有决定性影响；信息结构的设计（是否提供身份信息）同样显著影响合作策略的传播，这为理解AI系统中合作的涌现条件提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Several works have investigated the influence of graph topology on cooperation among artificial agents, while the majority of the literature has focused on modelling agents' adaptation through strategy imitation, which relies solely on the cumulative payoffs of others. This paper investigates scenarios in which each agent learns to play the two-player Iterated Prisoner's Dilemma (IPD) using deep reinforcement learning. Each agent is represented as a node in a graph, where its neighbours constitute the pool of opponents with whom it can interact. During each IPD episode, agents are provided with different types of information about their opponent, consisting of action history and opponent identity. Experimental results across different graph topologies show that the number of neighbours per node and the average path length are the main factors affecting the emergence of cooperation. We also show that, while partner selection fosters mutual cooperation by limiting the diversity of the opponent pool, providing agents with the identity of their opponent hinders the proliferation of cooperative strategies.

</details>


### [50] [Learning to Follow In-Context Watermark Instructions via Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29030)
*Yepeng Liu,Tianyi Chen,Xuandong Zhao,Dawn Song,Yuheng Bu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ICWBench基准，用于评估LLM遵循上下文水印指令的能力（兼顾可检测性与回答质量），并发现现有前沿LLM均无法在所有三类指令上同时达到两个目标。为此提出两阶段自包含训练方法：第一阶段通过自蒸馏加logits扰动（SDLP）使模型学会遵循ICW指令，第二阶段用自动验证器作为奖励进行强化学习。在Qwen3-14B和GPT-OSS-20B上将平均TPR@1%FPR分别从0.100提升至0.974、从0.337提升至0.968，同时保持高回答质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文水印（ICW）为LLM提供了一种无需访问模型内部即可由第三方调用的水印接口，但其可靠性取决于模型能否在不降低回答质量的前提下遵循水印指令。此前缺乏对此能力的系统性量化评估，且现有模型表现未知，因此需要构建基准并开发有效的训练方法来提升LLM的ICW指令遵循能力。

Method: 本文提出两阶段训练方法：（1）自蒸馏与logits扰动（SDLP）——使用同一基座LLM同时作为教师和学生，通过指令等价的解码时logits扰动使教师遵循ICW指令，再训练学生匹配教师的输出分布；（2）以自动验证器作为奖励的强化学习阶段。同时构建ICWBench基准，包含三个可验证的ICW指令族，分别从可检测性和回答质量两个维度打分。

Result: 在14个前沿专有和开源LLM上的评估表明，没有任何评估模型能在所有三个指令族上同时达到可检测性和回答质量两个目标。应用所提方法后，Qwen3-14B和GPT-OSS-20B的平均TPR@1%FPR分别从0.100提升至0.974、从0.337提升至0.968，且在困惑度评估和LLM-as-a-Judge下均保持高回答质量。

Conclusion: 现有LLM在ICW指令遵循方面存在显著不足，所提出的两阶段自包含训练方法无需更强模型蒸馏、无需人工标注、无需预存在ICW能力，能够大幅提升模型的ICW可检测性同时维持回答质量，为LLM水印的实用化提供了有效路径。

Abstract: In-context watermarking (ICW) prepends an instruction to a query asking the model to embed a statistically detectable signal in its response. It thus equips LLMs with a watermarking interface that third parties can invoke without access to model internals. Its reliability hinges on the LLM following the instruction without degrading answer quality, yet how well current LLMs do so has not been measured. We introduce $\mathsf{ICWBench}$, a benchmark of three verifiable ICW instruction families, each scored on both detectability and answer quality. Evaluating 14 frontier proprietary and open-source LLMs, we find that none of the evaluated LLMs achieves both objectives across all three families. To address this, we propose a self-contained two-stage training method, requiring no distillation from a stronger model, no manual annotation, and no pre-existing ICW IF ability. The first stage, self-distillation with logits perturbation (SDLP), uses the same base LLM as both teacher and student: an instruction-equivalent decoding-time logits perturbation makes the teacher follow the ICW instruction, and the student is trained to match the teacher's output distribution. The second stage applies reinforcement learning with the automatic verifier as the reward. Applied to Qwen3-14B and GPT-OSS-20B, our method raises average TPR@$1\%$FPR across three ICW instructions from $0.100$ to $0.974$ and from $0.337$ to $0.968$, respectively, while maintaining high response quality under both perplexity evaluation and LLM-as-a-Judge.

</details>


### [51] [Beyond Correctness: Validity-Oriented Evaluation of Biomedical LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29127)
*Rodrigo de Oliveira,Federico Pittino,James Gwinnutt,Jay Nanavati*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种可扩展、以效度为导向的管线，用于在高质量人工评判稀缺时评估生物医学LLM评判器；通过确定性、指标驱动的突变扩充人类标注基准，并从正确性、稳健性和格式合规三个维度评估评判器。以Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct为对象，比较四种训练范式，发现SFT→RL（先监督微调再强化学习）在正确性、合规性和稳健性上表现最佳，且在可分解任务上可达或超越前沿模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学LLM评判器的评估面临高质量人工评判稀缺的瓶颈，需要一种无需大量人工标注就能可靠评估评判器质量的可扩展方法。

Method: （1）用确定性、指标驱动的基因突变扩充现有带人类标注的生物医学基准，生成可审计的偏好对；（2）从三个部署相关维度评估评判器：与指标派生金标的一致正确性、随机采样下的稳健性、以及输出格式合规性；（3）对Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct在四种训练范式（base、SFT、RL、SFT→RL）下进行系统评估。

Result: 基础模型和单阶段训练（纯SFT或纯RL）在结构化医学判别任务（如PICO抽取、临床计算）上表现不佳；SFT→RL在所有维度上表现最优，且增益集中于可分解任务（PICO、MedCalc），在部分场景达到或超越前沿模型。

Conclusion: SFT→RL是构建生物医学LLM评判器的有效训练策略，本工作提出的效度导向管线为缺乏大规模人工标注场景下的评判器评估提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We propose a scalable, validity-oriented pipeline for evaluating biomedical LLM judges when high-quality human judgments are scarce. First, we augment existing human-labelled biomedical benchmarks with deterministic, metric-grounded mutations that produce auditable preference pairs. Second, we evaluate judges beyond aggregate correctness using three deployment-relevant dimensions: correctness against metric-derived gold labels, robustness under repeated stochastic sampling, and compliance with the requested output format. We use this pipeline to assess Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct under four regimes: (1) base, using the instruct model as is; (2) SFT, distillation-based supervised fine-tuning only; (3) RL, GRPO-based reinforcement learning only; and (4) SFT$\rightarrow$RL, SFT followed by RL. The base and single-stage regimes struggle on structured medical discrimination such as PICO extraction and clinical calculations, whereas SFT$\rightarrow$RL performs best across correctness, compliance, and robustness; gains concentrate on decomposable tasks (PICO, MedCalc), at times matching or outperforming frontier models.

</details>


### [52] [JudgePanel: A Compact Judge with Panel Deliberation via Adaptive Multi-Reward Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29168)
*Yiyue Qian,Shinan Zhang,Huan Song,Hannah Marlowe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出JudgePanel框架，通过将多智能体面板审议能力蒸馏到单个紧凑的Judge模型中，并引入AdaReward自适应多奖励强化学习算法，实现了以单模型推理成本获得多智能体审议质量的评估效果。仅14B参数的主干模型在四个评估基准上超越高达70B的专用评判模型，且可通过数百个标注样本快速适应新领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-Judge范式虽被广泛采用，但存在两个核心矛盾：单一模型评判受限于固有模型偏差影响评判质量，而多智能体评估协议通过多样化审议虽能缓解此问题，却在推理阶段代价过高。因此需要一种既能获得多智能体审议的可靠性，又能保持单模型推理成本的经济性的评判方案。

Method: 方法包含三个核心组件：(1) 从多个强评估器组成的集成中收集面板审议轨迹（包括讨论、分歧和解决的结构化模式），通过SFT训练紧凑的Judge模型掌握多智能体审议能力；(2) 提出AdaReward算法，一种自适应多奖励强化学习方法，在RL训练过程中根据不同目标饱和速率动态重新平衡各奖励分量的权重，以进一步提升评判质量；(3) 设计轻量级领域特化模块，支持仅用数百个标注样本快速适应新的评估领域。

Result: 实验结果表明：(1) JudgePanel是首个以单模型推理成本为单个紧凑评判模型配备多智能体面板审议能力的框架；(2) 使用14B主干网络的JudgePanel在四个评估基准上超越了参数规模达70B的评判专用模型，展现出强位置一致性，并能通过数百个样本快速特化到新领域。

Conclusion: JudgePanel成功弥合了多智能体审议的高质量与单模型推理的高效率之间的鸿沟，证明了将多智能体面板审议能力蒸馏到单模型中是可行且有效的。AdaReward自适应奖励机制和轻量级领域特化模块进一步提升了模型的评判质量与可部署性。

Abstract: The LLM-as-a-Judge paradigm has emerged as a scalable alternative to human evaluation. However, single-model judges are limited by their inherent model biases, while multi-agent evaluation protocols that mitigate this through diverse deliberation are prohibitively expensive at inference time. To this end, we propose \textbf{\modelname}, which equips a compact \underline{Judge} model with multi-agent \underline{Panel} deliberation capability. Specifically, we first train on panel deliberation traces from an ensemble of strong evaluators, capturing structured patterns of discussion, disagreement, and resolution. To further improve judgment quality beyond SFT, we introduce \textit{AdaReward}, an adaptive multi-reward RL algorithm that dynamically rebalances reward component weights as different objectives saturate at different rates during RL training. For practical deployment, we further design a lightweight domain specialization module for rapid adaptation to new evaluation domains with few hundred labeled samples. As a result, (i) \textit{Novel}: the first framework to equip a single compact judge with multi-agent panel deliberation capability at single-model inference cost; (ii) \textit{Effective \& Reliable}: JudgePanel with a 14B backbone outperforms judge-specialized models up to 70B across four evaluation benchmarks, demonstrates strong position consistency, and rapidly specializes to new domains with few hundred samples.

</details>


### [53] [Scaffolding Foundation Models into Physical-World Agents Pushes the Frontier of Long-Horizon Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30396)
*Zixing Lei,Gengze Zhou,Xiong-Hui Chen,Jiazhao Zhang,Yiyang Huang,Hang Yin,Haoqi Yuan,Qi Wu,Weixin Li,Siheng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NavMCP 是一个智能体脚手架框架，通过将 VLM 推理智能体与导航基础模型（NFM）执行器耦合，实现长时程物理世界探索。VLM 负责决策（找什么证据、去哪里找、何时停止），NFM 负责将语义子目标转化为闭环导航。通过意图、观察、记忆三个通道实现协作，无需重新训练任一模型，在多个 EQA 基准和 Unitree Go2 真机上取得 SOTA 结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程物理世界智能体需要同时具备远距离目标推理能力和可靠的闭环行为。然而现有基础模型存在能力分裂：VLM 能推理和适应高层计划但缺乏鲁棒的导航接地能力，NFM 能执行语义导航但只能进行受限的回合制操作，无法进行持续的任务级推理。如何在不重训练模型的前提下，将两者的互补能力整合为持久具身交互是核心挑战。

Method: NavMCP 提出一个智能体脚手架框架，包含三个关键设计：(1) 双模型耦合架构——VLM 作为推理智能体决定寻求什么证据、搜索哪里、何时停止，NFM 作为执行器将每个语义子目标接地到闭环导航中；(2) 三个协作通道——意图通道将证据需求转化为导航调用，观察通道将导航轨迹转化为带来源接地的轨迹证据，记忆通道跨调用累积发现、负证据和未解决目标；(3) 无需重训练，通过结构化交互协议将孤立导航回合转变为持续具身交互。类似于 MCP（Model Context Protocol）的智能体通信范式。

Result: 在 Embodied Question Answering 任务上，NavMCP 在 HM-EQA、MT-HM3D 和 EXPRESS-Bench 三个基准上均取得 SOTA 结果。在匹配智能体和执行器骨干的条件下，相比回合制接口在 HM-EQA 上提升 14.9 个百分点。在 Unitree Go2 真机上达到 78.3% 成功率，且随任务时程增加，与最强基线的差距从 10 点扩大到 45 点，表明优势在长时程任务中尤为显著。

Conclusion: NavMCP 验证了将互补基础模型通过脚手架方式整合为长时程物理世界智能体的可行性。结果表明，通过精心设计的结构化协作协议，无需重新训练两个模型即可实现持续具身推理，且性能在长时程任务中优势更加明显，为未来构建更复杂的多基础模型协作系统提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Long-horizon physical-world agents must reason over distant goals while grounding decisions in reliable closed-loop behavior. Today's foundation models split these capabilities: vision-language models (VLMs) infer missing information and adapt high-level plans but remain brittle and inefficient at repeated navigation grounding, while navigation foundation models (NFMs) robustly execute semantic goals but operate as bounded episodes without persistent task-level reasoning. We introduce NavMCP, an agentic scaffolding framework that couples a VLM reasoning agent with an NFM executor for long-horizon exploration. The VLM decides what evidence to seek, where to search, and when to stop, while the NFM grounds each semantic sub-goal into closed-loop navigation. Three channels structure their collaboration: intent translates evidence needs into navigation calls, observation converts rollouts into source-grounded trajectory evidence, and memory accumulates findings, negative evidence, and unresolved goals across calls. This design turns isolated navigation rollouts into persistent embodied interaction without retraining either model. On Embodied Question Answering, NavMCP achieves state-of-the-art results on HM-EQA, MT-HM3D, and EXPRESS-Bench. Under matched agent and executor backbones, it outperforms an episodic interface by 14.9 percentage points on HM-EQA. On a Unitree Go2, NavMCP reaches 78.3% success, with its margin over the strongest baseline growing from 10 to 45 points as the task horizon increases. These results demonstrate the potential of scaffolding complementary foundation models into long-horizon physical-world agents.

</details>


### [54] [RACER: Reinforced Agent Collaboration for Explainable Reasoning on Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29263)
*Yuwei Lou,Hao Hu,Yuzhou Jiang,Zongfei Zhang,Liang Wang,Jincai Liu,Jidong Ge,Xianping Tao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RACER框架，通过多智能体协作与强化学习，在知识图谱上实现可解释推理，显著缓解大语言模型的幻觉问题并在多跳推理任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在需要多跳领域知识的复杂推理任务中常出现幻觉且推理能力不足。虽然引入知识图谱可提供结构化、可验证的信息源，但现有KG增强LLM范式依赖单智能体路径提取和固定提示，缺乏适应性且面临巨大搜索空间。

Method: RACER框架包含三部分：(1) 语义感知动作剪枝与教师引导强化学习机制，高效从大规模KG提取高质量推理路径；(2) 跨任务累积共享记忆图与注意力驱动的多路径知识精炼模块，缓解单路径生成缺陷；(3) 四角色多智能体协作系统（GraphAgent、TemplateAgent、AnswerAgent、CriticAgent）动态精炼提示并评估答案。

Result: 在CommonsenseQA和OpenBookQA数据集上的大量实验表明，RACER相较于最先进的KG增强LLM基线平均提升5%，提供稳健且高度可解释的推理能力。

Conclusion: RACER通过多智能体协作与强化学习相结合的方式，有效解决了KG增强LLM推理中的搜索空间大、路径单一和提示固定等问题，实现更准确且可解释的复杂推理。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) often suffer from hallucination and struggle with complex reasoning tasks requiring multi-hop domain knowledge. While integrating Knowledge Graphs (KGs) provides a structured and verifiable information source, current KG-enhanced LLM paradigms usually rely on single-agent path extraction and fixed prompting, lacking adaptability and facing huge search spaces. To address these challenges, we propose RACER, a Reinforced Agent Collaboration framework for Explainable Reasoning on knowledge graphs. RACER employs a semantic-aware action pruning and teacher-guided reinforcement learning mechanism to efficiently extract high-quality reasoning pathways from large-scale KGs. Furthermore, to mitigate single-path generation pitfalls, we introduce a cross-task accumulated shared memory graph paired with an attention-driven multi-path knowledge refinement module. Finally, RACER orchestrates these components through a four-role multi-agent collaboration system (GraphAgent, TemplateAgent, AnswerAgent, and CriticAgent) to dynamically refine prompts and evaluate answers. Extensive experiments on CommonsenseQA and OpenBookQA datasets demonstrate that RACER significantly outperforms state-of-the-art KG-enhanced LLM baselines with an average improvement of 5\%, offering robust and highly interpretable reasoning capabilities.

</details>


### [55] [Plant-Inspired AI: Plants as Inspiration for Novel Problem Formulations, and Two Case Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29356)
*Deepayan Sanyal,Joel Michelson,Carla E. Cao,Adam B. Roddy,Maithilee Kunda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出植物的智能行为可以启发新的AI框架，通过两个案例（Boquila trifoliolata叶拟态和根-茎协调生长）展示植物在多变环境中解决问题的计算原理，并探索将这些原理转化为AI任务公式的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AI框架如监督学习、树搜索和约束满足虽然强大，但遗漏了某些类型的问题。植物生物学研究揭示了植物具有复杂的适应性行为，可作为新AI框架的灵感来源，弥补现有框架的不足。

Method: 作者通过两个具体植物案例（叶拟态和根-茎协调生长）提取底层计算原理，识别其中未被现有AI模块覆盖的问题，并针对每个案例提出初步的任务公式化方案。

Result: 确定了两个植物智能行为对应的新问题框架，其中叶拟态高度特化于Boquila属植物，而根-茎协调生长在大多数植物中普遍存在；两者均指出了当前AI未解决的对应问题类型。

Conclusion: 植物行为能够为AI提供新的问题求解框架，这些框架不仅适用于类似植物的场景，还可推广到非植物问题，为AI研究开辟新的方向。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) has long been inspired by studies of biological intelligence. Reinforcement learning, for instance, drew inspiration from studies involving animal learning and is now a powerful paradigm for solving many real-world problems. Recently, plant biologists have uncovered a wide range of complex behaviors in plants that enable them to flexibly adapt to variable environments. Here, we argue that such behavior can motivate new AI frameworks encompassing a range of problems overlooked by existing problem-solving frameworks such as supervised learning, tree search, and constraint satisfaction. We illustrate this idea with two examples of intelligent problem-solving in plants: (1) leaf mimicry in Boquila trifoliolata, a vine capable of altering its leaves' morphology to resemble those of multiple host trees simultaneously; and (2) coordinated root-shoot growth, wherein plants allocate resources across organ systems exploring distinct environments. While leaf mimicry is highly specific to Boquila, coordination of root-shoot growth is shared across most plants. For both examples, we capture underlying computational principles and identify problems fitting these frameworks that are currently unaddressed by AI. Finally, we outline preliminary task formulations and discuss how these formulations may be applied to non-plant problems.

</details>


### [56] [LiteSearch-VL: Small Multimodal Search Agents via Trajectory Distillation and Synthetic Step-DPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29357)
*Saeed Khaki,Nima Safaei,Kamal Ginotra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了将多模态搜索智能体的发布轨迹蒸馏到小型视觉语言骨干模型（Qwen3-VL-2B/4B）中的效果。实验表明，这种蒸馏传递的主要是"代理行为契约"而非均匀的准确率提升——即让模型学会输出可用答案的能力，而非直接提升答案质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强大多模态搜索智能体依赖专有前沿模型或大规模开源视觉语言骨干网络配合大量智能体数据与强化学习训练，计算成本高昂。作者提出一个不同的问题：在单节点预算下，将已发布的智能体轨迹蒸馏到更小的骨干模型中，究竟能传递什么能力？

Method: 提出LiteSearch-VL低计算量方案：(1) 基于Qwen3-VL-2B和Qwen3-VL-4B骨干；(2) 仅使用公开的OpenSearch-VL轨迹数据；(3) 使用参数高效的LoRA适配器；(4) 使用GPT-5生成硬负例构建合成逐步偏好，进行DPO训练，针对五个局部失败模式（过早作答、错误工具、弱查询、重复查询、忽略图像）。评估使用12,400条GPT-5评判的rollout，涵盖SimpleVQA、FVQA、LiveVQA和VDR-Bench-testmini。

Result: 核心发现是主导效应为行为层面的而非统一准确率提升：完整轨迹SFT让2B模型从几乎从不输出可用答案（1,237/1,240无回答rollout）提升到28.4%的macro Pass@1，媲美甚至略超现成4B基线（25.6%）。合成偏好学习和紧凑工具蒸馏只是精炼作用而非阶段转变（最佳4B配置达30.8% macro Pass@1）。受控VDR步数预算消融显示，额外搜索轮次将弃答转化为错误实体错误而非正确答案，表明答案验证而非搜索深度是小模型的下一瓶颈。

Conclusion: 蒸馏释放的智能体轨迹到小模型主要传递"行为契约"（学会以正确格式输出答案），而非直接带来质量跃升。小多模态智能体下一步瓶颈在于答案验证能力，而非搜索深度或查询优化。

Abstract: Multimodal search agents answer visual questions by interleaving image understanding, web retrieval, tool use, and evidence synthesis. Strong systems exist, but in two expensive regimes: proprietary frontier models such as GPT-5 and Gemini, or large open vision-language backbones trained with substantial agentic data and reinforcement learning. We ask a different question: when released agent trajectories are distilled into much smaller backbones under a single-node budget, what is actually transferred? We study this with LiteSearch-VL, a low-compute recipe for Qwen3-VL-2B and Qwen3-VL-4B that uses only released OpenSearch-VL trajectories, parameter-efficient LoRA adapters, and synthetic step-level preferences: DPO on GPT-5-generated hard negatives targeting five local failure modes (premature answer, wrong tool, weak query, repeated query, ignored image). Across 12,400 GPT-5-judged rollouts on SimpleVQA, FVQA, LiveVQA, and VDR-Bench-testmini, the dominant effect is behavioral rather than a uniform accuracy lift: full-trajectory supervised fine-tuning transfers the agent contract, taking the 2B model from almost never emitting a usable answer (1,237/1,240 no_answer rollouts) to 28.4% macro Pass@1, matching or slightly exceeding the off-the-shelf 4B base (25.6%). Synthetic preference learning and compact tool distillation act as refinements rather than phase transitions (best 4B configuration: 30.8% macro Pass@1). Finally, a controlled VDR step-budget ablation shows that extra search turns convert abstentions into wrong_entity errors rather than correct answers, identifying answer verification, not search depth, as the next bottleneck for small multimodal agents.

</details>


### [57] [TRACER: Per-Tool Context Retention for LLM Agents via Consequence-Attributed Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29363)
*Ziqi Lin,Ye Wu,Mengying Yang,Xu Liu,Yizhou Liu,Qiang Ke,Qin Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACER，一种面向企业数据代理（enterprise data agents）的后果感知（consequence-aware）上下文压缩方法。TRACER将压缩建模为逐工具（per-tool）的顺序决策问题，使用轻量级REINFORCE策略为每个工具输出分配查询条件化的保留比例，并通过学习的结果模型优化联合目标（任务成功率、总token消耗和压缩后工具重新调用次数）。在三个压缩后端上，TRACER相比保留全部上下文减少29-46%的token消耗，同时保持或提升任务成功率；相比静态策略额外节省15-18%的token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业数据代理在执行长任务时通常累积数十万token的上下文，现有压缩策略在分配保留预算时不考虑移除单个工具输出带来的下游后果，激进压缩可能触发昂贵的工具重新调用（tool re-invocations），抵消初始的压缩收益。作者将此称为"压缩-后果差距"（compression–consequence gap），即压缩决策与后续连锁反应之间的脱节。

Method: TRACER将上下文压缩构造为顺序的逐工具决策问题：1）使用轻量级REINFORCE策略，在每个压缩事件上仅利用当前可用信息，为每个工具输出分配查询条件化的保留比例；2）设计后果感知目标函数，联合优化任务成功、总token消耗和压缩后工具重新调用；3）引入学习的结果模型（learned outcome model），将选中的保留比例与完全保留每个工具输出的预测后果进行对比，以改进信用分配（credit assignment）。

Result: 在三个压缩后端的生产查询上，TRACER相比完全保留上下文减少29-46%的token消耗，同时保持或提升任务成功率；相比工具类型条件的静态策略额外节省15-18%的token。干预性回放（interventional rollouts）证明学习到的逐工具信用分数与实测的单工具后果相关。策略在跨agent骨干和压缩器架构迁移时仍产生正向收益，并在五个LOCA-bench环境上减少18-25%的token消耗。

Conclusion: 后果感知的逐工具上下文保留策略能有效提升长时程语言代理的效率。TRACER证明了将压缩视为考虑下游连锁反应的决策问题，而非单纯按启发式规则分配保留预算，可以显著降低token消耗并保持任务成功率。该方法具备跨架构迁移的良好泛化能力，为长时程语言代理的上下文管理提供了一种新的范式。

Abstract: Enterprise data agents answer business queries by chaining many tool calls over multiple reasoning steps, routinely accumulating hundreds of thousands of context tokens per session. Existing compression strategies typically allocate retention budgets without accounting for the downstream consequences of removing individual tool outputs. Aggressive compression may therefore trigger costly tool re-invocations that offset the initial savings. We call this the compression--consequence gap. To close it, we propose TRACER, which formulates compression as a sequential per-tool decision problem. A lightweight REINFORCE policy assigns query-conditioned retention ratios using only information available at each compression event. Its consequence-aware objective jointly accounts for task success, total token consumption, and post-compression tool re-invocations. To improve credit assignment, TRACER uses a learned outcome model to compare the predicted consequences of the selected retention ratio with those of fully retaining each tool output. On held-out production queries across three compressor backends, TRACER reduces total token consumption by 29--46% relative to keeping all context while maintaining comparable or higher task success. Compared with a tool-type-conditional static policy, TRACER provides an additional 15--18% of token savings. Interventional rollouts show that the learned per-tool credit scores correlate with measured single-tool consequences. The learned policy also yields positive savings when transferred across agent backbones and compressor architectures, and reduces token consumption by 18--25% on five held-out LOCA-bench environments. These results demonstrate the value of consequence-aware, per-tool context retention for improving the efficiency of long-horizon language agents.

</details>


### [58] [Toward Latent Language Model Skills Steering and Optimization: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29459)
*Xunyi Jiang,Junda Wu,Yuxin Xiong,Sheldon Yu,Tong Yu,David Arbour,Ritwik Sinha,Julian McAuley,Hongyi Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过实证研究，探讨了大型语言模型（LLM）的程序性技能（procedural skills）是否能在激活空间中表示为方向向量，以及这些方向向量能否通过向量运算来表达技能级行为。研究发现，程序性技能确实存在向量空间表征，技能方向可激活、可组合，且对比方向可实现上下文条件化的算法个性化，优化轨迹呈非单调演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常将技能视为通过提示或程序显式指定的表面级构造，而未深入探究程序性能力在模型内部是如何表征的，以及它们能否作为潜在空间中的结构化对象被操纵。作者旨在回答这一核心问题，探索技能能否以潜在空间方向的形式存在并支持直接干预。

Method: 本文采用实证研究方法：在激活空间中为程序性技能提取表征方向，通过干预实验验证方向激活是否能改变模型行为；测试独立提取的方向能否组合形成更高级技能；利用对比方向实现上下文条件化的算法个性化和优化；分析优化轨迹中技能方向的演化过程。

Result: 研究发现：1）程序性技能确实具有向量空间表征，个体技能方向可被激活以改变模型行为；2）独立提取的方向能够组合形成更高级别的技能；3）对比方向可实现上下文条件化的算法个性化和优化；4）技能方向的优化轨迹呈非单调演化，中间状态时常超越完全优化的解决方案。

Conclusion: 这些发现支持对LLM程序性技能的表征级视图：技能在潜在空间中存在向量化的组织方式，可通过内部干预实现直接操纵，为理解并控制LLM的程序性能力提供了新的理论框架和操作范式。

Abstract: Skills, as a useful abstraction for the procedural capabilities of large language models (LLMs), capture how models perform structured, multi-step reasoning and program execution. Existing approaches typically treat skills as explicit, surface-level constructs specified through prompts or programs, leaving open the question of how such procedural capabilities are represented inside the model and whether they can be manipulated as structured objects in latent space. In this empirical study, we investigate whether procedural LLM skills can be represented as directions in activation space and whether vector-space operations over these directions can express skill-level behaviors. We find that procedural skills admit a vector-space representation: individual skill directions can be activated to shift model behavior; independently extracted directions can compose to form higher-level skills. Contrastive directions yield context-conditioned algorithmic personalization and optimization trajectories over skill directions evolve non-monotonically, with intermediate states often surpassing fully optimized solutions. These results support a representation-level view of procedural LLM skills: they admit a latent vector-space organization that allows direct manipulation through internal interventions.

</details>


### [59] [Towards a Systems Foundation for Agentic Skills: Architecture, Lifecycle, and Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29596)
*Sanket Badhe,Deep Shah,Priyanka Tiwari,Nehal Kathrotia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个统一的系统基础和参考架构，用于"智能体技能"（Agentic Skills）生态系统。智能体技能被定义为将执行知识外部化为可重用、可执行、可移植的模块化程序抽象，连接高层认知规划与确定性执行环境。论文系统化地阐述了一个九阶段生命周期架构，并探讨了市场动态、安全威胁、运行时验证及跨领域系统实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主大语言模型（LLM）智能体在复杂、长时程任务中面临可靠性、上下文消耗和执行稳定性等瓶颈。传统的整体式提示工程和无状态工具调用范式难以扩展，而领域正快速向"智能体技能"这一模块化范式收敛，但缺乏统一的系统化理论框架。

Method: 论文提出了一种参考架构，将技能定义为外部化的程序性知识抽象，并将其生命周期划分为九个阶段：自主发现、编写与表示格式、记忆存储、动态检索与路由、组合与编排、执行与修复、终身适应、实证评估以及安全治理。此外还分析了市场动力学、公共注册表、对抗性威胁向量以及运行时验证与防御机制。

Result: 论文对智能体技能生态系统进行了系统化的分类与架构梳理，涵盖了从技能发现到安全治理的完整生命周期，并对软件工程、操作系统导航、具身机器人和科学发现等多个领域的系统实现进行了归类，同时指出了持续学习和基准真实感方面的关键开放挑战。

Conclusion: 本文确立了智能体技能作为构建可扩展、稳健且可验证的自主语言智能体的基础范式，为后续研究提供了统一的参考架构和系统化框架，填补了该领域缺乏系统性理论基础的空缺。

Abstract: Autonomous large language model (LLM) agents increasingly face reliability, context consumption, and execution stability bottlenecks when deployed on complex, long-horizon tasks. While monolithic prompt engineering and stateless tool-calling paradigms struggle to scale, the field is rapidly converging toward \emph{agentic skills}: modular procedural abstractions that externalize execution knowledge into reusable, executable, and portable artifacts. This paper establishes a unified systems foundation and reference architecture for the agentic skills ecosystem. We formalize skills as externalized procedural knowledge bridging high-level cognitive planning with deterministic execution environments, and systematically delineate the architecture across a nine-stage lifecycle: autonomous discovery, authoring and representation formats, memory storage, dynamic retrieval and routing, composition and orchestration, execution and repair, lifelong adaptation, empirical evaluation, and security governance. We further examine marketplace dynamics, public registries, and emerging adversarial threat vectors, alongside runtime verification and defense mechanisms. Finally, we categorize system implementations across software engineering, operating system navigation, embodied robotics, and scientific discovery, while highlighting critical open challenges in continual learning and benchmark realism. This work establishes agentic skills as a foundational paradigm for building scalable, robust, and verifiable autonomous language agents.

</details>


### [60] [FRAMEWORKERS: A Dynamic Multi-Agent Framework for AI-Generated Video Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29814)
*Zhendong Li,Lei Sun,Letian Shi,Deheng Zhang,Ruibo Ming,Mengshun Hu,Dannong Xu,Jian Wang,Danda Paudel,Luc Van Gool,Jinjin Gu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FRAMEWORKERS，一个任务中心、以工作空间为依托的多智能体框架，用于开放式视频制作。中央导演智能体将视频创作建模为动态任务管理，通过不断编辑任务栈决定下一步执行哪个子任务及调用哪个子智能体；助手智能体作为执行层，在工作空间中检索资源、调用子智能体并持久化产出。通过SFT+GRPO微调导演智能体以提升描述符条件下的任务路由可靠性，实验表明其在路由准确性、故障恢复、对未见子智能体的泛化以及端到端视频质量上均优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成系统擅长生成单个片段，但完整视频制作需要协调脚本撰写、故事板、生成和编辑等长序列相互依赖的创作步骤，同时需要持久的资产管理以及随中间输出和依赖关系演变的动态任务编排。现有自动化系统依赖刚性流水线难以适应多样输入和变化工作流，通用大语言模型在长程编排和多模态资源路由上又不可靠。

Method: 构建任务中心、工作空间为依托的多智能体框架。核心组件包括：(1) 中央导演智能体，将视频创作视为动态任务管理，持续编辑任务栈以决定子任务执行顺序和子智能体选择；(2) 助手智能体作为执行层，在工作空间中检索所需资源与上下文，调用指定子智能体并持久化产物；(3) 模块化子智能体通过注册描述符暴露能力，支持新子智能体无缝集成；(4) 通过SFT后接GRPO对导演智能体进行描述符条件下的任务路由微调以提升编排可靠性。

Result: 实验表明FRAMEWORKERS在路由准确性上优于强LLM规划器；能够从运行时故障中可靠恢复；无需重训练即可泛化到未见的子智能体；在端到端视频质量和任务覆盖广度上均优于固定流水线、单智能体系统和先前多智能体方法。

Conclusion: 任务中心的任务栈管理与工作空间支撑的执行层相结合，配合描述符条件化的路由微调（SFT+GRPO），是解决开放式视频制作中长程多步编排与资源管理的有效范式。

Abstract: Modern video generators excel at synthesizing individual clips, but complete video production requires coordinating a long sequence of interdependent creative steps, including scripting, storyboarding, generation, and editing. It further demands persistent asset management and dynamic task orchestration as intermediate outputs, dependencies, and execution states evolve over time. Existing automated systems typically rely on rigid pipelines that are difficult to adapt to diverse inputs and changing workflows, while general-purpose large language models (LLMs) remain unreliable for long-horizon orchestration and multimodal asset routing. We introduce FRAMEWORKERS, a task-centric and workspace-grounded multi-agent framework for open-ended video production. A central Director formulates video creation as dynamic task management, continuously editing a Task Stack to determine which subtask to execute next and which sub-agent to invoke. An Assistant serves as the execution layer, grounding each selected task in a shared Workspace, retrieving the required assets and context, invoking the assigned sub-agent, and persisting the resulting artifacts. Execution capabilities are exposed through modular sub-agents with registered descriptors, allowing new sub-agents to be integrated without redesigning the orchestration workflow. To improve orchestration reliability, we fine-tune the Director via supervised fine-tuning (SFT) followed by Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) for descriptor-conditioned task routing. Experiments show that FRAMEWORKERS outperforms strong LLM planners in routing accuracy, recovers reliably from runtime failures, generalizes to unseen sub-agents without retraining, and achieves higher end-to-end video quality and broader task coverage than fixed pipelines, single-agent systems, and prior multi-agent approaches.

</details>


### [61] [AcrossWAM1.0:A Modular Latent World-Action Stack for Compact Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29937)
*Yafei Zhang,Nan Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AcrossWAM1.0是对潜在世界-动作模型（Latent World-Action Models）的模块化与缩放研究，明确划分策略适配器、潜在世界解码器与流动匹配专家三大模块边界，并通过将Qwen3.5-0.8B替换Qwen3-VL-2B骨干网络实现了97.45%对98.00%的成功率，仅差0.55个百分点（McNemar p=0.266），满足预设的两点保留标准，且推理可达检查点参数减少42.4%，所有保留张量与源检查点逐位一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始的LaWAM框架将世界模型、多模态骨干网络与部署检查点紧密耦合，缺乏模块化边界，导致难以独立替换组件、评估各模块贡献并进行高效部署。作者希望解耦这些组件，使潜在子目标预测的框架更具可审计性、可缩放性和可部署性。

Method: 将潜在世界-动作栈显式划分为三个模块边界：(1)策略适配器生成潜在动作与动作生成上下文；(2)保留的潜在世界解码器将预测转换锚定在当前场景中；(3)流动匹配专家生成连续动作块。此外将仅用于训练的教师模型从推理图中分离，并提供可验证的部署导出。在2,000个LIBERO配对episode上进行骨干网络替换实验（Qwen3.5-0.8B替代Qwen3-VL-2B），并辅以MiniCPM-V适配器的跨家族冒烟测试。

Result: 在2,000个配对LIBERO episodes上，用Qwen3.5-0.8B替换Qwen3-VL-2B后成功率为97.45%，相比2B模型的98.00%仅差0.55个百分点（精确McNemar检验p=0.266）。压缩后的推理可达检查点含1,472.6M唯一参数，比原始2B策略少42.4%，且所有保留张量与源检查点逐位相同。MiniCPM-V适配器冒烟测试通过，但闭环跨家族迁移仍待评估。

Conclusion: AcrossWAM1.0并非提出新的潜在子目标算法，而是贡献了一个可审计的软件与评估边界，使紧凑的潜在世界-动作策略能够进行模块化替换、缩放与部署验证。虽然实验结果不证明骨干网络等价，但满足预设的保留阈值，为紧凑世界-动作模型的工程化与实证评估建立了新基准。

Abstract: Latent world-action models avoid rendering future pixels by predicting an action-relevant visual subgoal in feature space. LaWAM established this formulation, but its original presentation left the world model, multimodal backbone, and deployment checkpoint tightly coupled. We introduce AcrossWAM1.0, a modularization and scaling study of this latent world-action stack. Rather than presenting latent subgoals as a new algorithm, we make the module boundary explicit: a policy adapter produces latent-action and action-generation contexts; a retained latent world decoder grounds the predicted transition in the current scene;and a flow-matching expert generates continuous action chunks. We further separate training-only teachers from the inference graph and provide a verifiable deployment export. On 2,000 paired LIBERO episodes, replacing a Qwen3-VL-2B backbone with Qwen3.5-0.8B yields 97.45% success versus 98.00% for the 2B model (a-0.55percentage-point difference; exact McNemarp=0.266). This does not prove equivalence, but it meets a prespecified two-point retention criterion. The compact, inference-reachable checkpoint contains 1,472.6M unique parameters, 42.4% fewer than the original 2B policy, while all retained tensors are bitwise identical to the source checkpoint. Cross-family execution is additionally checked with a MiniCPM-V adapter smoke test; closed-loop cross-family transfer remains an open evaluation. AcrossWAM1.0 therefore contributes an auditable software and evaluation boundary for compact latent world-action policies, distinct from LaWAM's original latent-subgoal contribution.

</details>


### [62] [SearchWiki: Learning to Build and Navigate Knowledge Wikis for Active Information Seeking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29953)
*Guransh Singh,Vishwajeet Kumar,Arkadeep Acharya,Adnan Qidwai,Jaydeep Sen,Sachindra Joshi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SearchWiki是一个将语料库整理为层次化、带类型、可导航的wiki，并训练9B参数智能体WikiResearcher-9B进行多轮工具调用的检索增强生成框架。通过三层知识组织和强化学习优化导航策略，在多个基准上超越同规模基线和更大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统扁平检索增强生成将所有文档视为无关的chunks，丢弃了文档层次结构和跨文档结构，导致检索效率低下且无法利用语料库的结构性知识。

Method: SearchWiki将语料库合成为三层wiki结构：(1)文档概览、(2)跨文档主题页、(3)页面级来源记录。训练9B参数的智能体WikiResearcher-9B通过多轮工具调用来检索信息，采用on-policy强化学习优化导航策略，奖励函数包含答案正确性、检索质量和轨迹效率三个部分。

Result: 在ViDoRe-V3（8个领域）、FinanceBench以及LoCoMo、LongMemEval、PersonaMem-v2等记忆基准上的评估表明，WikiResearcher-9B（RL微调的Qwen 9B模型）显著优于同规模未训练基线，并超过或匹配更大规模的外部模型。

Conclusion: SearchWiki与WikiResearcher-9B的组合证明，在结构化语料上进行学习式导航是优于扁平检索的替代方案。

Abstract: Flat retrieval-augmented generation treats a corpus as a bag of chunks, discarding document hierarchy and cross document structure. We introduce SearchWiki, a harness framework that synthesizes a corpus into a hierarchical, typed, navigable wiki and trains an agent, WikiResearcher-9B, to retrieve information through multi-turn tool use. The wiki organizes knowledge into three layers - document overviews, cross- document topic pages, and page-level source records; enabling progressive refinement of retrieval when initial lookup misses. We optimize the agent's navigation policy with on-policy reinforcement learning with a multi-component reward function balancing answer correctness, retrieval quality and trajectory efficiency. Evaluation on ViDoRe-V3 (8 domains), FinanceBench, and memory benchmarks (LoCoMo, LongMemEval, PersonaMem-v2) shows that WikiResearcher- 9B which is our RL-tuned Qwen 9B model, significantly outperforms same-size untrained baselines and exceeds or matches larger external models. SearchWiki paired with WikiResearcher-9B demonstrates that learned navigation over structured corpora is a superior alternative to flat retrieval.

</details>


### [63] [Spec2Twin-Chain: Orchestrating Bi-Level Optimization with LLMs for Blockchain Digital Twin Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30050)
*Haoting Zhang,Haoxian Chen,Jiayuan Sheng,Donglin Zhan,Zeyu Zheng,David D. Yao,Wenpin Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Spec2Twin-Chain框架，将区块链数字孪生构建形式化为双层优化问题：上层由大语言模型基于系统规格和行为证据提出并修正架构，下层由仿真优化器在目标和约束下校准参数，两层迭代迭代，评估结果存入全局档案以指导后续提案。实验表明该框架能构建行为准确的数字孪生、通过反馈改进初始设计，并复用校准孪生支持下游决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统区块链数字孪生构建依赖应用特定的手工建模，涵盖领域知识翻译、参数校准和验证等步骤，但这类方法难以跨系统和跨决策问题复用，缺乏自动化手段。

Method: Spec2Twin-Chain将数字孪生构建建模为双层优化问题。上层利用LLM依据系统规格、行为证据和已评估设计的反馈来提议与修正结构上可行的架构；下层通过基于仿真的优化器在显式目标和护栏约束下校准架构条件化参数。两层迭代运行，下层的候选评估结果存入全局档案，用于指引上层的后续提案。

Result: 在孪生校准、反馈驱动的恢复、压力分析、下游策略优化和策略更新等受控实验中，该框架能够构建行为准确的孪生，通过迭代反馈改进初始设计，并能复用已校准孪生支撑下游决策任务。

Conclusion: Spec2Twin-Chain证明了将LLM驱动的架构探索与仿真优化相结合，可以自动化区块链数字孪生的构建过程，提升可复用性和下游决策支持能力。

Abstract: Building a blockchain digital twin largely requires translating domain knowledge and specific system descriptions into a simulator architecture, calibrating its parameters against behavioral evidence, and validating the constructed twin. These steps are commonly performed through application-specific modeling efforts that can be difficult to reuse across systems and downstream decision problems. We consider automating this process through Spec2Twin-Chain, a framework that formulates blockchain digital-twin construction as a bi-level optimization problem. At the upper level, a large language model proposes and revises structurally admissible architectures using system specifications, behavioral evidence, and feedback from evaluated designs. At the lower level, a simulation-based optimizer calibrates the architecture-conditioned parameters under explicit objectives and guardrail constraints. The two levels iterate. The evaluated candidates at lower levels are retained in a global archive and used to guide subsequent proposals at upper levels. We conduct controlled experiments involving twin calibration, feedback-driven recovery, stress analysis, downstream policy optimization, and policy updating. The results demonstrate that the framework can construct behaviorally accurate twins, improve initial designs through iterative feedback, and reuse calibrated twins to support downstream decisions.

</details>


### [64] [DiffPDE: Masked Diffusion Language Models as PDE Solver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30532)
*Wenxuan Guo,Yuyang Hong,Lubin Fan,Zhaojin Fu,Lin Chen,Kun Ding,Shiming Xiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DiffPDE是一个利用离散扩散语言模型进行偏微分方程求解器代码修复的框架，通过局部重掩码与填充策略只重新生成错误区域，并引入ID-GRPO强化学习方案实现在单轨迹内多轮调试，在PDEBench上优于同规模自回归模型且显著加速修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的PDE求解器合成方法主要依赖自回归模型，但其全局从左到右的解码方式在处理局部化的代码缺陷时会产生大量冗余计算。作者希望挑战这种低效范式，提出与PDE错误稀疏性天然对齐的修复机制。

Method: DiffPDE采用两个核心组件：(1) 离散扩散语言模型结合局部化重掩码与填充策略，仅重新生成存在错误的代码区域，同时保留正确的上下文；(2) 迭代调试GRPO（ID-GRPO），一种强化学习方案，通过中间奖励在单一轨迹内支持多轮调试，以处理需要顺序干预的耦合缺陷。

Result: 在PDEBench基准上的实验表明，DiffPDE达到了具有竞争力的精度，性能优于同规模的自回归模型，并且显著加速了代码修复过程。

Conclusion: DiffPDE验证了离散扩散模型在PDE求解器定向代码修复上的有效性，其局部重掩码策略与迭代强化学习机制共同克服了自回归模型在局部缺陷修复中的冗余解码问题，为代码合成与修复提供了新范式。

Abstract: Existing approaches for synthesizing Partial Differential Equation (PDE) solvers predominantly rely on autoregressive models, yet their global left-to-right decoding incurs substantial redundancy when addressing inherently localized bugs. In this work, we challenge this inefficient paradigm and propose DiffPDE, a framework leveraging discrete diffusion language models for targeted code repair. By introducing a localized re-masking and infilling strategy, DiffPDE regenerates only erroneous regions while preserving correct context, naturally aligning generation with the sparse nature of PDE errors. Furthermore, to handle coupled bugs requiring sequential interventions, we present Iterative Debugging GRPO (ID-GRPO), a reinforcement learning scheme that enables multi-round debugging within single trajectories via intermediate rewards. Experiments on PDEBench show that DiffPDE achieves competitive accuracy, outperforms same-scale AR models, and significantly accelerates repair.

</details>


### [65] [HiRS-Agent: A Hierarchical Multi-Agent System for Reliable Long-Horizon Remote Sensing Task Solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30672)
*Boyang Mu,Zhiwei Wei,Mugen Peng,Wenjia Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HiRS-Agent是一个用于长时程遥感任务求解的分层多智能体系统，采用管理器层与专家层的两级协作架构，结合两阶段监督微调和验证引导的分层强化学习，在Earth-Agent Benchmark和ThinkGeo上显著提升了工具使用能力和任务正确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感智能体系统大多依赖单一集中式决策框架，难以适应遥感任务多阶段、相互依赖的特性，导致任务执行不稳定、工具使用错误和跨阶段错误传播等问题。

Method: 提出HiRS-Agent两级协作架构：Manager Layer负责动态路由、步骤级验证、重规划和终止控制；Specialist Layer按遥感工作流组织领域专用工具，负责子任务推理和工具执行。并引入两阶段监督微调策略和验证引导的分层强化学习来联合优化协调策略和工具使用策略。

Result: 在Earth-Agent Benchmark和ThinkGeo基准上的实验表明，HiRS-Agent大幅提升了长时程工具使用能力和最终任务正确率，验证了结构化多智能体协作对可靠遥感智能体的有效性。

Conclusion: 结构化分层多智能体协作范式比集中式决策框架更适合长时程遥感任务的求解，HiRS-Agent提供了一种可靠、可扩展的遥感智能体架构方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models and multimodal models have pushed remote sensing (RS) processing from simple perception models to agentic systems designed to tackle complex, long-horizon RS tasks. However, existing systems often rely on monolithic decision-making frameworks, which fail to accommodate the multi-stage, interdependent nature of RS tasks. This centralized approach leads to challenges such as unstable task execution, incorrect tool usage, and error propagation across stages. To address these issues, we propose HiRS-Agent, a hierarchical multi-agent system for long-horizon RS task solving. HiRS-Agent adopts a two-level collaborative architecture: the Manager Layer handles dynamic routing, step-level verification, replanning, and termination control, while the Specialist Layer organizes domain-specific tools according to the RS workflow and is responsible for subtask reasoning and tool execution. To further enhance the system's capability, we introduce a two-stage supervised tuning strategy and a verification-guided hierarchical reinforcement learning stage to jointly optimize coordination and tool-use policies. Experiments on Earth-Agent Benchmark and ThinkGeo show that HiRS-Agent substantially improves long-horizon tool-use capability and final-task correctness, demonstrating the effectiveness of structured multi-agent collaboration for reliable RS agents. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/IntelliSensing/HiRS-Agent.

</details>


### [66] [MedAgent-R1: Faithfulness-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Evidence-Grounded Medical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30676)
*Jiangwang Chen,Chenghao Zhang,Hengxing Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了强化学习训练下的医疗检索智能体存在"自信幻觉"问题：仅用结果导向奖励会提升准确率但损害忠实性。作者提出"忠实性门控奖励"设计（MedAgent-R1），将引用伪造率从31.8%降至4.7%，证据完整性从58.7提升至82.6，精度保持75.1%。该方法在忠实性维度超越GPT-4o，表明显式忠实性训练能带来纯规模缩放无法获得的证据锚定收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗AI系统的幻觉不仅产生错误答案，其表面引用检索证据的伪造性论证会误导临床医生的治疗决策。因此医疗推理智能体不仅需要正确回答，还必须产生临床医生能够对照引用证据核验的忠实论证。作者发现强化学习训练下的检索智能体存在系统性失败模式：仅结果奖励的优化会在提升准确率的同时破坏论证忠实性。

Method: 提出忠实性门控（faithfulness-gated）奖励设计：通过硬门控将准确率奖励与证据锚定（evidence grounding）条件绑定；在此基础上补充检索有效性（retrieval validity）与简洁性（conciseness）信号，以封堵智能体检索场景特有的利用漏洞（exploitation paths）。

Result: MedAgent-R1将引用伪造率从31.8%降至4.7%，证据完整性从58.7提升至82.6，精度保持75.1%的准确率；HealthBench Safety上获得13.2分增益。在相同的智能体检索设置下，MedAgent-R1在忠实性专属维度（Factual Support 4.55 vs 4.25；Overclaiming 4.40 vs 4.15）上超过GPT-4o，但总体准确率仍低于GPT-4o。

Conclusion: 显式忠实性训练能够获得纯模型规模缩放无法实现的证据锚定收益。研究揭示"自信幻觉"这一系统性失败模式并给出可行的缓解方案，为医疗AI系统的安全部署提供了重要保障。

Abstract: When medical AI systems hallucinate clinical reasoning, the consequences extend beyond incorrect answers: fabricated justifications that superficially reference retrieved evidence can mislead clinicians into unsafe treatment decisions. Medical reasoning agents must therefore produce not only correct answers but also faithful justifications that clinicians can verify against cited evidence. We identify a systematic failure mode in RL-trained retrieval agents: outcome-only rewards improve accuracy while degrading faithfulness, a phenomenon we term confident hallucination. The agent learns to answer from parametric memory and backfill plausible but unsupported justifications; citation fabrication rates rise from 16.5% to 31.8% even as accuracy improves by 5 points over the supervised baseline. We address this with a faithfulness-gated reward design: accuracy credit is conditioned on evidence grounding via a hard gate, complemented by retrieval validity and conciseness signals that close exploitation paths unique to agentic retrieval. The resulting system, MedAgent-R1, reduces citation fabrication from 31.8% to 4.7% and raises evidence completeness from 58.7 to 82.6 while maintaining 75.1% accuracy, with 13.2-point gains on HealthBench Safety. Under the same agentic retrieval setup, MedAgent-R1 outscores GPT-4o on faithfulness-specific dimensions (Factual Support 4.55 vs. 4.25; Overclaiming 4.40 vs. 4.15) while remaining below GPT-4o in overall accuracy, suggesting that explicit faithfulness training yields evidence-grounding gains not achieved by scaling alone.

</details>


### [67] [ATLAS: Dual-Horizon Diagnostic Evaluation for Industrial Tool-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30685)
*Wei Chen,Peilun Zhou,Zhaoyu Hu,Jiajun Chai,Zhongni Hou,Yufei Zhang,Derong Xu,Guojun Yin,Wei Lin,Zhi Zheng,Tong Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ATLAS是一个面向工业工具调用型LLM代理的双视界诊断评估框架，在请求视界提供轨迹级诊断信号定位执行缺陷与能力问题，在交互视界提供用户级信号评估持续交互的服务响应性，并在美团小团生产流量上通过离线实验与在线A/B实验验证了其在诊断保真度和策略优化方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业级代理服务同时涉及当前请求的迭代轨迹和持续的用户交互，仅依据最终结果评估会掩盖缺陷产生的位置以及后期服务是否与早期对话上下文保持对齐的问题，因此需要更细粒度的双视界诊断评估框架。

Method: 提出ATLAS双视界诊断评估框架：(1) 请求视界下使用轨迹级诊断信号将缺陷关联到执行位置和具体能力维度；(2) 交互视界下使用用户级信号评估持续交互中的服务响应性。两者实例化为具有明确证据范围与决策边界的可执行信号；结合LLM评判接口，针对真实业务日志中的高置信参考进行校准，必要时将评判行为蒸馏为高效诊断模型以降低延迟和成本。

Result: 在美团小团生产流量上完成评估。离线实验验证了诊断信号的保真度以及基于回放的策略改进效果；在线A/B实验在用户参与度、下游业务结果和人工审计质量抽样方面均获得同步提升。

Conclusion: ATLAS通过双视界（请求轨迹与持续交互）的细粒度诊断信号，能够更全面地揭示工业工具使用型agent的能力缺陷与服务行为问题，其反馈支持策略优化，并在大规模生产环境中证明了实际收益。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly deployed in user-facing services that require iterative tool use under dynamic business conditions. Reliable evaluation is essential for sustained improvement: it must reveal capability deficiencies, inform priorities, and assess interventions. Yet industrial agent service unfolds both through the iterative trajectory of a current request and through continued user interaction. Final-outcome assessment can therefore obscure where deficiencies arise and whether later service remains aligned with context from earlier exchanges. We propose ATLAS, a dual-horizon diagnostic evaluation framework for industrial tool-use agents. At the request horizon, trajectory-wise diagnostic signals relate deficiencies to execution locations and capability concerns. At the interaction horizon, user-wise signals assess whether service remains responsive across continued interaction. Together, these views provide structured diagnostic evidence for analyzing execution deficiencies and sustained service behavior. ATLAS instantiates them as executable signals with explicit evidence scopes and decision boundaries. LLM judge interfaces are calibrated against high-confidence references from real business logs; when needed, their decision behavior is distilled into efficient diagnostic models for lower-latency, lower-cost evaluation. The resulting feedback supports policy optimization. We evaluate ATLAS on Meituan Xiaotuan production traffic. Offline experiments assess diagnostic-signal fidelity and replay-based policy improvement, while online A/B experiments show concurrent gains in user engagement, downstream business outcomes, and sampled human-audit quality.

</details>


### [68] [CAER: Causal Action Effect Reweighting for World Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30897)
*Jianjie Fang,Xvyuan Liu,Ziyou Wang,Rongze Tang,Zhaolu Wang,Zhuohang Li,Xin Zhang,Haisheng Su,Chen Gao,Wei Wu,Xinlei Chen,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CAER是一种通用的训练范式，通过在线对比有无动作条件下的模型预测，定位因果受动作影响的token并重加权监督信号，从而替代均匀MSE训练，提升动作条件视频生成世界模型在物理一致性、可控性和视觉质量上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作条件视频生成世界模型使用时空均匀的均方误差（MSE）训练，导致大量背景token主导梯度，而稀疏的动作交互动态被欠优化。均匀拟合奖励的是重建外观而非学习动作如何改变世界，缺乏对动作因果效应的针对性监督。

Method: 提出Causal Action Effect Reweighting (CAER)：通过在线对比模型自身在有/无动作条件下的预测结果，定位因果受动作影响的token，将得到的effect map归一化为权重，在保持权重总量不变的前提下重新分配监督信号的分布。该方法无需外部标注或离线预处理，不增加数据预处理时间，可随模型和数据规模自然扩展。

Result: 在多种异构的动作条件世界模型任务上，CAER相比均匀MSE训练收敛到更好的解，在生成视频的物理一致性、可控性和视觉质量上均获得一致的提升。

Conclusion: CAER为动作条件世界模型提供了一种无需额外标注、可扩展的统一训练范式，通过在训练过程中动态重加权监督信号，有效引导模型关注动作的因果效应，从而获得更优的物理一致性和可控的视频生成质量。

Abstract: World models are becoming core infrastructure for embodied intelligence, with action-conditioned video generation providing controllable predictions of how scenes evolve after agent interventions. Yet existing models are commonly trained with space-time-uniform mean squared error, allowing abundant background tokens to dominate the gradient while sparse interaction dynamics remain under-optimized; such uniform fitting rewards reconstructing appearance rather than learning how actions change the world. We introduce Causal Action Effect Reweighting (CAER), a general training paradigm that redistributes supervision toward the tokens whose predicted future is causally affected by the action. CAER contrasts the model's own predictions with and without action conditioning to localize these tokens online, then normalizes the resulting effect map into a weight that preserves the total coefficient mass and changes only where it is spent. This online signal requires no external annotations or offline preprocessing, avoids additional data-processing time, and scales naturally with model and dataset size. Experiments across heterogeneous action-conditioned world-model tasks show that CAER converges to better solutions than uniform MSE training, with consistent improvements in the physical consistency, controllability, and visual quality of generated videos.

</details>


### [69] [Learning Action Models with Conditional and Quantified Effects via Uncertainty-Guided Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30955)
*Jeffrey Jewett,William Solow,Sandhya Saisubramanian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出OHCAM，一种在线学习方法，用于从有限的环境交互中学习带条件效应和量化效应的动作模型，通过维护假设信念并主动选择信息性动作来减少不确定性，同时具备噪声鲁棒性。在六个基准规划领域和真实机器人上验证了其样本效率和实际适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作模型学习方法大多假设简单的动作表示，或在学习条件效应和量化效应时变得计算上难以处理。需要一种能够高效学习复杂动作模型的方法。

Method: OHCAM维护一个关于假设动作模型的信念分布，通过最大化竞争假设之间的分歧来主动选择信息性动作以降低不确定性，并对噪声观测保持鲁棒。为提升可扩展性，从简单假设集合出发，仅当当前假设与数据不一致时才扩展至更复杂的条件。

Result: 在六个基准规划领域的实验中，OHCAM在样本效率上优于基线方法，即使在观测噪声下也能解决更多任务。在Kinova Gen3机器人上验证了其在实际任务中的适用性。

Conclusion: OHCAM是一种样本高效且噪声鲁棒的动作模型学习方法，能够学习带条件效应和量化效应的复杂动作模型，并在仿真和真实机器人环境中均表现出良好性能。

Abstract: Accurate action models are critical for effective planning. Existing action-model learning methods largely assume simple action representations or become computationally intractable when learning conditional and quantified effects. We present Online Hypothesis-Driven Conditional Action Model Learning (OHCAM), an online approach for learning action models with conditional and quantified effects from limited interactions with the environment. OHCAM maintains a belief over hypothesized action models and actively selects informative actions to reduce uncertainty by maximizing disagreement among competing hypotheses, while being robust to noisy observations. To enable scalability, OHCAM begins with a small set of simple action model hypotheses and expands to more complex conditions only when the current hypotheses become inconsistent with the data. Experiments on six benchmark planning domains demonstrate that OHCAM is sample efficient in learning action models that solve substantially more tasks than baselines, even with observation noise. We validate OHCAM on two tasks using a Kinova Gen3 robot, demonstrating the real-world applicability of our approach.

</details>


### [70] [Scaling Large Reasoning Models beyond Human Supervision: A Path toward Superintelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31075)
*Zhiqin Yang,Jingwen Fu,Yuhan Liu,Hengyu Liu,Yonggang Zhang,Kainan Cao,Zizhuo Zhang,Chenxin Li,Ruibin Yuan,Jiahao Pan,Jiankai Sun,Zhenyuan Zhang,Yibo Li,Yunlong Lin,Jing Xiong,Sida Lin,Bo Han,Wei Xue,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了大型推理模型（LRM）在人类监督逐渐退出学习循环后如何继续提升的问题，提出了一条从L0到L5的五级梯度框架，分别沿"奖励轴"（从逐实例人类评判到可复用的自动化验证器/奖励）和"经验轴"（从人工策划任务到自生成课程、构建环境和自主协同进化）对学习过程进行结构化分析，并给出了围绕策略能力、反馈保真度和经验质量三个对象的评估体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LRM在数学和代码领域已通过可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）取得了显著进展，但将其推广到开放式和智能体任务十分困难，因为可靠奖励难以获得，且直接的人类监督无法跟上模型生成经验的规模和复杂度。作者希望理解LRM在人类监督逐渐淡出学习循环后如何持续提升，并系统梳理其中的关键问题与风险。

Method: 作者提出一个五级梯度框架（L0-L4），用以识别学习流程中哪些部分仍处于人类控制之下。该框架沿两个维度展开：奖励轴（从逐实例人类判断到可复用验证器再到无需人类反馈的自动化奖励）和经验轴（从人工策划的任务/环境到自生成课程、构建环境和自主协同进化）。此外，作者还提出了对策略能力、反馈保真度和经验质量三个互补对象的评估方案。

Result: 本文提供了对超越人类监督的LRM扩展方法的系统性梳理，识别了日益自主化的奖励与经验生成带来的关键风险（包括奖励破解、反馈漂移、课程坍塌和环境错误），并建立了围绕策略能力、反馈保真度、经验质量的三维评估体系。同时维护了一个持续更新的GitHub仓库追踪最新进展。

Conclusion: 该分析为如何将LRM扩展到超越人类监督提供了结构化理解，明确了发展自维持学习系统（朝向超级智能）过程中面临的开放问题，包括自主奖励/经验生成的风险管理以及评估体系的完善。

Abstract: Recent advances in large reasoning models (LRMs) have shown that reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) can substantially improve reasoning in mathematics and code, where outcomes can be checked automatically. Extending this progress to open-ended and agentic tasks remains difficult because reliable rewards are harder to obtain and direct human supervision cannot keep pace with the scale and complexity of model-generated experience. This paper studies how LRMs can continue to improve as human supervision gradually recedes from the learning loop. We examine two connected dimensions of this problem. The reward axis traces the development from per-instance human judgments to reusable verifiers and rewards that operate even without human feedback. The experience axis examines how learning can progress from human-curated tasks and environments toward self-generated curricula, constructed environments, and autonomous co-evolution. We connect these dimensions through a five-level ladder from L0 to L4 that identifies which parts of the learning process remain under continued human control. Our analysis further highlights the risks introduced by increasingly autonomous rewards and experience generation, including reward hacking, feedback drift, curriculum collapse, and environment errors. Consequently, we also provide the evaluation around three complementary objects: policy capability, feedback fidelity, and experience quality. This analysis provides a structured account of current approaches to scaling LRMs beyond human supervision and the open problems involved in developing self-sustaining learning systems toward superintelligence. Furthermore, we maintain a continuously updated \href{https://github.com/visitworld123/Awesome-Scaling-LRM-Beyond-Human-Supervision}{GitHub repository} to track the latest advances.

</details>


### [71] [Reconciling Process Supervision with Outcome-Based Credit in Agentic Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31077)
*Jingxiao Yang,Wangjie Gan,Yingxuan Zhuang,Wenqi Zhang,Jintao Chen,Xuhong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TASPO方法，将基于结果（outcome）的强化学习中的特权监督信号转换为基于执行动作的信用分配，解决了监督-信用差距（supervision-credit gap），在三个智能体基准上相比GRPO提升10.6%并具有更好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于结果的强化学习虽然能提供经过验证的反馈，但在长程交互中给轨迹级优势（trajectory-level advantage）进行统一分配，导致粗粒度的信用分配。虽然on-policy自蒸馏可以利用训练时才可用的特权信息（PI）提供更细粒度的监督，但细粒度监督不等于细粒度信用——PI引起的似然变化只描述额外信息如何改变策略偏好，并未直接决定可执行动作应如何继承验证过的任务结果，形成监督-信用差距。

Method: TASPO包含三个核心步骤：(1) 从验证成功的经验中构建决策可应用的特权信息（decision-applicable PI）；(2) 在可执行动作层面聚合PI引起的似然偏移；(3) 将相对动作支持转换为正的、有界的、均值保持的权重，作用于原始轨迹优势。这样，验证结果决定更新方向和平均尺度，而PI仅用于在动作间重新分配信用。

Result: 在三个智能体基准上，TASPO相比GRPO提升10.6%，在未见任务上具有更好的泛化能力。分析表明TASPO减少了监督不匹配，且动作级信用分配稳定了策略优化过程。

Conclusion: TASPO有效弥合了特权监督与结果导向信用之间的差距，为语言模型智能体的信用分配提供了一种新的视角和方法，提升了强化学习在长程交互任务中的效果和稳定性。

Abstract: Outcome-based reinforcement learning provides verified feedback for language-model agents, but assigns trajectory-level advantage uniformly to all decisions, yielding coarse credit over long-horizon interactions. On-policy self-distillation offers finer supervision by re-evaluating sampled behavior with privileged information (PI) available only during training. However, fine-grained supervision is not necessarily fine-grained credit: PI-induced likelihood changes describe how additional information alters policy preference, but do not directly determine how an executable action should inherit the verified task outcome. This creates a supervision-credit gap. Privileged signals may be irrelevant to the current interaction state, operate at a token granularity misaligned with executable decisions, and lack the outcome semantics required for reinforcement. We introduce TASPO, which converts privileged supervision into outcome-grounded action credit. TASPO constructs decision-applicable PI from verified successful experience, aggregates PI-induced likelihood shifts at the executable-action level, and converts relative action support into positive, bounded, mean-preserving weights on the original trajectory advantage. Thus, the verified outcome determines the update direction and average scale, while PI only redistributes credit across actions. Across three agentic benchmarks, TASPO improves over GRPO by 10.6\% and generalizes better to unseen tasks. Further analysis indicates that TASPO reduces supervision mismatch and that action-level assignment stabilizes the policy optimization process. These findings offer the community another interesting perspective.

</details>

### [72] [ERR+: Sequential Entropy Resolution for Efficient and Decisive LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28771)
*Xin Jiang,Minhao Wang,Wen Wu,Zhentao Xie,Shangheng Du,Jinxin Shi,Jiabao Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ERR+，一个基于token级熵降观测的两阶段RLVR框架。第一阶段使用Entropy Relief Reward（ERR）奖励思考阶段累积的熵降（按长度对数归一化），第二阶段使用Robust Relative Efficiency Reward，通过tanh变换的组内z-score对响应长度进行评分。实验在五个数据集上展示了一致的准确率和简洁性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法仅使用基于正确性的奖励信号，缺乏对推理过程质量本身的指导，导致内部推理结构在很大程度上未被优化。作者通过实证分析发现正确推理轨迹在思考阶段表现出更频繁、更大的token级熵降，并利用这一观测来设计奖励函数。

Method: 提出两阶段RLVR框架ERR+。第一阶段使用Entropy Relief Reward（ERR），奖励与思考阶段累积token级熵降成比例的bonus，并进行log-normalized长度归一化；与先前抑制熵的方法不同，ERR奖励不确定性消解而不约束高熵探索状态。第二阶段引入Robust Relative Efficiency Reward，对每个响应的长度相对共同生成的对等样本通过tanh变换的组内z-score评分。作者还提供形式化分析表明两个目标的联合优化会在早期训练中产生梯度冲突，从而支持顺序设计。

Result: 在五个数据集上的实验表明，该方法在多种模型骨干上一致地提升了准确率和响应简洁性。

Conclusion: ERR+通过奖励推理过程中的熵降（不确定性消解）而非单纯惩罚熵来优化推理结构，结合长度效率评分，在多个数据集上同时提升准确率和简洁性，验证了token级熵降作为推理质量信号的普适性。

Abstract: Large reasoning models achieve strong performance on complex tasks by generating extended chain-of-thought (CoT) traces via reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR). While current RLVR methods have achieved strong results with correctness-based reward signals, they provide limited guidance on the quality of the reasoning process itself, leaving the internal reasoning structure largely unoptimized. Through empirical analysis across multiple model families, we identify a consistent pattern: correct reasoning trac es exhibit more frequent and larger token-level entropy drops within the thinking phase than incorrect ones. We propose ERR+, a two-phase RLVR framework grounded in this observation. The first phase trains with the Entropy Relief Reward (ERR), a bonus proportional to cumulative token-level entropy drops in the thinking phase, log-normalized by response length. Unlike prior methods that suppress entropy, ERR rewards the resolution of uncertainty while leaving exploratory high-entropy states unconstrained. The second phase introduces the Robust Relative Efficiency Reward, which scores each response's length against co-generated peers via a $\tanh$-transformed within-group $z$-score. We provide a formal analysis showing that joint optimization of the two objectives induces gradient conflict in early training, motivating the sequential design . Experiments on five datasets demonstrate consistent improvements in both accuracy and response conciseness across model backbones. Our code is available at https://github.com/XrkArul/err_response

</details>


### [73] [The Halt Vector: Internalizing a Causal Steering Intervention for Efficient Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28859)
*Dylan Jayabahu,Tinuade Adeleke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过因果可解释性发现定位到一个"停止向量"（halt vector），并将其内化到模型权重中，在不使用强化学习的情况下，仅用24个问题拟合，即可在五个未见基准上约去除四分之一的多余思考，同时保持原有准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型（如DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B）在已知答案后仍继续思考，思维链长度约为答案概率稳定所需时长的两倍。这种多余思考量因问题而异，全局长度惩罚无法有效移除，因此需要更精准的机制来缩短推理长度。

Method: 首先通过因果可解释性分析发现第18层存在一个"停止向量"（halt vector）——一种均值差方向，其转向强度控制思考时长；同时复制出的值轴则无效。随后将该干预内化到权重中，关键挑战是仅最大化标量投影会破坏下游只读读者所依赖的离轴维度，导致生成变长而非变短。解决方案是重建整个被转向的激活，并将这些维度固定到其自然值。

Result: 仅用24个问题拟合且无强化学习，该停止机制在五个未见基准上以保持准确率为前提，移除了约四分之一的多余思考；削减量与每个问题自身的可移除松弛度的相关性为0.70。同时修复了一个随难度增长而出现的非终止病态，而解码时置信度钩子反而会加剧该问题。作者不声称在原始权衡上胜过精心调优的长度惩罚或解码时提前退出，贡献在于获取停止机制的方法本身。

Conclusion: 该工作的核心贡献不是超越已有的长度惩罚或提前退出方法的原始性能权衡，而是展示了一种基于因果可解释性发现将停止机制内化到权重中的方法论，能够在保持准确率的同时有效减少多余思考并修复非终止问题。

Abstract: Reasoning models do not stop when they know the answer. On DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B the chain of thought runs about twice as long as the model's own answer probability takes to settle, and how much of that excess is removable varies from problem to problem, so a global length penalty cannot take it out. We take it out by internalizing a causal interpretability finding into the weights. The mechanism is a halt vector: a difference-of-means direction at layer 18 of this model whose steering strength controls how long it thinks, while a replicated value axis does nothing. Installing that intervention in the weights is harder than it looks. Maximizing the scalar projection onto the direction corrupts the off-axis dimensions a frozen downstream reader depends on, and generation gets longer instead of shorter; what works is reconstructing the whole steered activation with those dimensions pinned to their natural values. Fit from 24 problems and no reinforcement learning, the halt removes about a quarter of the thinking at held accuracy across five unseen benchmarks, and the cut tracks each problem's own removable slack at 0.70. It also closes a non-termination pathology that grows with difficulty and that a decoding-time confidence hook makes worse. We do not claim to beat a well-tuned length penalty or decoding-time early exit on the raw trade-off; the contribution is how the halt is obtained.

</details>


### [74] [Continuity-Free Near-Minimax Leading-Order Regret for CVaR-UCBVI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28960)
*Yuanlong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明Bernstein CVaR-UCBVI算法在无需连续性假设的情况下即可达到有限时域表格CVaR强化学习的尖锐后悔界，即Õ(√(SAK/τ))的主导项，且该算法在整个回报分布类别上是极小极大最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作在CVaR强化学习中，对于任意归一化回报分布只能获得Õ(τ^{-1}√(SAK))的后悔界，只有在密度下界假设下才能得到更优的Õ(√(SAK/τ))速率。本文旨在消除这一连续性假设，证明同一算法对任意回报分布（包括原子、混合和连续）都能达到更优速率。

Method: 关键创新是一个"选定预算自界"（selected-budget self-bound）：回合短尾（episode shortfall）的条件方差至多为τ加上值估计宽度。将此自界代入原始的Bernstein分解中，即可在高概率下获得Õ(√(SAK/τ)+(SAHK^{1/4}+S^2AH)/τ)的后悔界。

Result: 对于任意归一化回报分布（包括原子、混合和连续分布），Bernstein CVaR-UCBVI算法达到Õ(√(SAK/τ)+(SAHK^{1/4}+S^2AH)/τ)的后悔界。其中τ^{-1/2}主导项与期望后悔的最小极大下界在对数因子内匹配。低阶项仍保留τ^{-1}依赖。

Conclusion: Bernstein CVaR-UCBVI在主导阶数区间内，于完整回报分布类别上是最小极大最优的；低阶项保持τ^{-1}依赖这一事实也被明确刻画。该结果打破了先前对密度下界等连续性假设的依赖，证明了算法在更广泛的分布类别上的最优性。

Abstract: For finite-horizon tabular CVaR reinforcement learning, prior work proves a $\widetilde{O}(τ^{-1}\sqrt{SAK})$ leading regret bound for arbitrary normalized return laws and the sharper $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{SAK/τ})$ rate under a density lower bound. We show that the same Bernstein CVaR-UCBVI algorithm attains the sharper rate without continuity assumptions. The key is a selected-budget self-bound: the conditional variance of the episode shortfall is at most $τ$ plus the value-estimation width. Substitution into the original Bernstein decomposition yields, with high probability, $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{SAK/τ}+(SAHK^{1/4}+S^2AH)/τ)$ regret for arbitrary normalized return laws, including atomic, mixed, and continuous laws. The $τ^{-1/2}$ leading term matches the expected-regret minimax lower bound up to logarithmic factors. Thus Bernstein CVaR-UCBVI is minimax-optimal over the full return-law class in the leading-order regime; the lower-order terms retain their $τ^{-1}$ dependence.

</details>


### [75] [Flow-JEPA: Flow Matching for Robust Latent Dynamics in JEPA World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29029)
*Yanchen Huo,Ziying Song,Yadan Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Flow-JEPA（F-JEPA），一种基于条件流匹配的动力学模型，用于联合生成未来潜在状态序列，替代JEPA框架中确定性的自回归预测器，在干净和含噪声观测条件下均显著提升了下游任务的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的JEPA世界模型（如LeWorldModel）采用确定性自回归预测器通过逐步转移生成未来状态，存在误差累积问题，且对任务无关的视觉扰动敏感。作者希望引入随机轨迹级预测来克服这些局限。

Method: F-JEPA采用条件流匹配（conditional flow matching）的动力学模型，以高斯分布作为流的源，联合生成当前观测和动作条件下的未来潜在状态序列。向量场在将扰动潜轨迹传输到干净未来表示的过程中暴露于扰动，从而学习更鲁棒的轨迹级预测。该方法保留了JEPA的无重建框架，但将逐点转移回归替换为随机轨迹级预测。

Result: 在干净观测条件下，平均成功率从86%提升到92%；在含噪声条件下，平均成功率从67%提升到86%。

Conclusion: 条件流匹配为JEPA世界模型中的确定性自回归动力学提供了一种有前景的替代方案，在鲁棒性和预测准确性方面均有显著提升。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) have shown strong potential for learning compact predictive representations, and LeWorldModel (LeWM) extends this paradigm to reconstruction-free latent world modeling from pixels. However, its deterministic autoregressive predictor generates future states through repeated one-step transitions, which can accumulate errors and remain sensitive to task-irrelevant visual perturbations. In this work, we propose Flow-JEPA (F-JEPA), a conditional flow matching dynamics model that jointly generates a sequence of future latent states conditioned on the current observation and actions. A Gaussian distribution serves as the flow source, exposing the vector field to perturbed latent trajectories as it learns to transport them toward clean future representations. This formulation retains the reconstruction-free JEPA framework while replacing point-wise transition regression with stochastic trajectory-level prediction. F-JEPA raises mean success from $86\%$ to $92\%$ under clean observations and from $67\%$ to $86\%$ under noisy conditions, suggesting that conditional flow matching provides a promising alternative to deterministic autoregressive dynamics in JEPA world models.

</details>


### [76] [PathBridger: Subgoal Bridges for Offline Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29061)
*Soohyun Choi,Seonvin Cho,Songnam Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PathBridger是一种层次化离线目标条件强化学习方法，通过显式构建状态空间"桥梁"连接子目标选择与短期执行，使用逆动力学模型将桥梁解码为可执行的动作序列，在OGBench基准测试中表现优异，尤其在多物体Cube操作任务上提升显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视野离线GCRL面临两大挑战：稀疏目标信号的长距离传播和执行误差无法通过环境交互修正。现有层次化方法中，高层选定的子目标只指明"去哪里"，但中间的隐式状态路径缺乏显式约束，导致子目标选择与低层执行之间存在脱节（interface gap），影响整体性能。

Method: PathBridger采用层次化架构，核心创新在于显式构建从当前状态到选定子目标之间的状态空间桥梁（state-space bridge），然后利用逆动力学模型将该桥梁解码为短程可执行的动作块（action chunk），从而将高层子目标决策与低层短程执行直接关联起来，消除两者之间的隐含路径问题。

Result: 在OGBench基准测试的多个任务上，PathBridger展现了强大的整体性能，特别是在多目标Cube操作任务上取得了显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 显式建模子目标与执行之间的状态路径可以有效解决层次化离线GCRL中高层与低层脱节的问题，为长视野离线目标条件强化学习提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: Offline goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL) aims to learn policies for reaching diverse goals entirely from fixed trajectory data. Long-horizon offline GCRL remains challenging because sparse goal-reaching signals must be propagated over many steps, while execution errors cannot be corrected through additional environment interaction. Existing methods address these challenges by improving long-range value estimation or reducing the effective decision horizon through subgoals, options, and action chunks. In several hierarchical methods, however, a selected subgoal specifies where to go, while the intervening state-space path remains implicit in an endpoint-conditioned low-level policy. To address this interface, we propose PathBridger, a hierarchical offline GCRL method that explicitly connects subgoal selection to short-horizon execution. PathBridger constructs a state-space bridge toward the selected intermediate endpoint and decodes it into a short executable action chunk using an inverse dynamics model. Experiments across the evaluated OGBench tasks demonstrate strong aggregate performance, with particularly large gains on the multi-object Cube manipulation tasks. Code: https://github.com/SChoish/PathBridger

</details>


### [77] [Titans-QFWP: A Regime-Aware Hybrid Quantum Fast Weight Programmer for Portfolio Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29093)
*Ming-Kai Hung,Jun-Hao Chen,Yun-Cheng Tsai,Samuel Yen-Chi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Titans-QFWP是一种混合强化学习架构，将量子快速权重编程器与Titans风格记忆（持久性、惊喜、遗忘）相结合用于自适应投资组合优化，在468只标普500股票上以约3000个可训练参数实现优异表现（中位ARR 0.4260，Calmar 8.5504，IR 0.8427），消融实验表明量子门控重塑了记忆组件角色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决高维市场特征下的自适应投资组合优化问题，结合量子计算和强化学习以提升投资组合管理的性能和鲁棒性。

Method: 提出Titans-QFWP混合强化学习架构，集成量子快速权重编程器（Quantum Fast Weight Programmer）与Titans风格记忆（持久性、惊喜、遗忘）；引入增强的A3C^2框架，采用匈牙利对齐的K均值聚类和缩放对数收益奖励函数；在Equal-Parameter-Count（EPC）基准下进行公平比较。

Result: 在468只标普500股票上，以约3000个可训练参数实现中位ARR 0.4260、Calmar 8.5504、IR 0.8427的优异性能。消融实验显示量子门控重塑了记忆组件的角色：持久性支持回撤控制、惊喜贡献收益生成、遗忘提供额外稳定化。

Conclusion: 通过稳定化量子表示，模型在市场回撤期间能够实现防御性资产配置，同时保留上行潜力，验证了量子强化学习在投资组合优化中的有效性。

Abstract: We propose Titans-QFWP, a hybrid reinforcement learning architecture integrating a Quantum Fast Weight Programmer with Titans-style memory (Persistence, Surprise, and Forgetting) for adaptive portfolio optimization. To address high-dimensional market features, we introduce an enhanced A3C^2 framework with Hungarian-aligned K-means clustering and scaled log-return rewards. Evaluated on 468 S&P 500 stocks under an Equal-Parameter-Count (EPC) benchmark with approximately 3,000 trainable parameters, Titans-QFWP achieves strong performance (median ARR 0.4260, Calmar 8.5504, IR 0.8427). Ablation results reveal that quantum gating fundamentally reshapes memory component roles, with Persistence supporting drawdown control, Surprise contributing to return generation, and Forgetting providing additional stabilization. By stabilizing these quantum representations, the model enables defensive allocation during market drawdowns while preserving upside potential.

</details>


### [78] [Locked at the Entrance, Open Inside: Where RLVR Narrows the Solution Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29188)
*Qiancheng Zhou,Ruizhe Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了RLVR训练导致推理轨迹解空间收缩的问题，发现收缩主要发生在推理的"入口"（首个操作符之前），而非推理过程中。通过Countdown任务上的精细分析，作者证明策略并非无法执行备选解，而是不再启动它们；并提出针对入口的干预（晚期层参数插值）可在不损失pass@1的情况下将解覆盖率提升37%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR能显著提升pass@1精度，但会导致策略解空间收缩，削弱test-time scaling的收益。作者希望定位这一广度损失发生在推理轨迹的哪个环节：是策略无法访问有效的解族，还是无法执行已启动的计算？这有助于理解RLVR的机理并指导缓解策略。

Method: 以Countdown任务为研究对象（其解空间可穷举枚举为按首个操作数和运算符划分的离散入口族），在PPO(Qwen2.5-3B)和GRPO(Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct)两种设置下训练。通过逐token似然分析入口前后分布变化、提供未选择的入口前缀进行干预、测试表面提示与入口定向干预（晚期层参数插值）等方法，并在6个数学基准上扩展验证。

Result: 解覆盖率最多下降67%，即使在所有checkpoint都解对的问题上覆盖率也减半。收缩集中在入口：首个算术运算前逐token似然偏移量是下游推理的11-16倍。仅提供未选择的入口前缀即可将低访问族完成率提升一个数量级以上（0.018->0.212）。晚期层参数插值增加37%解覆盖率且不损失pass@1。表面提示无法恢复多样性，但入口定向干预有效。该现象在6个数学基准的7B/14B模型上普遍存在，但SFT保留2倍以上覆盖率，分段SFT-DPO-RLVR能保留入口熵。

Conclusion: 推理广度在入口处丢失而非推理过程中——这是RLVR导致解空间收缩的关键机制。这种损失并非推理优化的必然副产品，可通过入口定向干预有效缓解。核心洞见是"推理广度丢失在门口，而非房间内"。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) substantially improves single-sample accuracy (pass@1) but causes the policy's solution space to contract, diminishing the returns of test-time scaling. In this work, we investigate where inside a reasoning trajectory this breadth is lost: does the policy fail to access a valid solution family, or does it fail to execute computation once initiated? To disentangle access from execution, we analyze the Countdown task, whose solution space can be exhaustively enumerated into discrete entrance families defined by the first operand and operator, across PPO on Qwen2.5-3B and GRPO on Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct. Across both training setups, solution coverage falls by up to 67%, halving even on problems solved across all checkpoints. We show that this contraction is heavily concentrated at the entrance: per-token likelihood shifts are 11x--16x larger prior to the first arithmetic operation than during downstream reasoning. Supplying only an unselected entrance prefix restores completion rates in low-access families by over an order of magnitude (0.018 -> 0.212 under PPO), demonstrating that alternative solutions remain executable but are no longer initiated. Guided by this localization, we find that while surface prompting fails to recover diversity, entrance-targeted interventions succeed: late-layer parameter interpolation with early checkpoints increases solution coverage by 37% at no loss in pass@1. Finally, we show that early-step entropy collapse recurs across six math benchmarks with 7B and 14B models, but is not an inevitable byproduct of reasoning optimization: an SFT baseline preserves more than double the coverage, and staged SFT--DPO--RLVR pipelines retain early-step entropy. In summary, reasoning breadth is lost at the door, not inside the room. Code: https://github.com/ershiyidian/early-branch-locking.

</details>


### [79] [RL-FAT: Reinforcement Learning for Fair Adversarial Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29247)
*Tejaswini Medi,Levan Mikeladze,Margret Keuper*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RL-FAT，一种基于强化学习的公平对抗训练框架，通过策略梯度反馈和公平强调正则化，在提升平均鲁棒精度的同时显著降低类别间的鲁棒性不平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络仍易受对抗扰动攻击，对抗训练虽能提升平均鲁棒精度，但常掩盖类别间鲁棒性的显著差异——部分类别更鲁棒，而其他类别仍易受攻击，这引发对抗公平性问题，尤其在视觉任务中要求所有类别均具备可靠鲁棒性。

Method: RL-FAT将预测分布视为策略，结合基于正确性的预测奖励与类别级价值估计，计算类别特异性优势（advantage）用于策略梯度优化；同时引入公平强调对抗损失，对高对抗损失的类别施加更强的训练压力，通过强化驱动适应与公平正则化相结合来缓解类别间鲁棒性差异。

Result: 大量实验表明，与标准对抗训练基线相比，RL-FAT在达到有竞争力的鲁棒精度的同时，显著降低了类别间的鲁棒性不平衡。

Conclusion: RL-FAT通过强化学习驱动的类别自适应关注与公平强调正则化相结合，实现了对抗鲁棒性与类别间鲁棒性平衡的双重提升，为对抗公平性问题提供了一种有效解决方案。
