Abstract: Deep neural networks remain highly vulnerable to adversarial perturbations, and adversarial training (AT) has become a widely used approach for improving robustness. However, improvements in average robust accuracy often mask substantial class-wise disparities: while some classes become more robust, others may remain disproportionately vulnerable under attack. This imbalance raises an important adversarial fairness concern, particularly in vision tasks where reliable robustness is expected across all categories. To address this challenge, we propose \textbf{RL-FAT}, a reinforcement-learning-inspired fair adversarial training framework that uses policy-gradient based feedback from adversarial predictions. RL-FAT interprets the prediction distribution as a policy and combines correctness-based prediction rewards with class-wise value estimates to compute class-specific advantages for policy-gradient optimization. This enables the model to adaptively focus on class-wise misclassification. Furthermore, we introduce a fairness-emphasis adversarial loss that assigns stronger training pressure to classes with high adversarial loss, thereby mitigating class-wise robustness disparity. By combining reinforcement-driven adaptation with fairness-emphasis regularization, RL-FAT improves adversarial robustness while promoting a more balanced robustness distribution across classes. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves competitive robust accuracy and substantially reduces class-wise robustness imbalance compared with standard adversarial training baselines.

</details>


### [80] [When Do Larger Batches Help Scale LLM Reinforcement Learning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29296)
*Ziniu Li,Jinbo Wang,Guanhua Huang,Feiyuan Zhang,Pengbo Li,Alex Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了强化学习（RL）中大语言模型训练时batch size增大对端到端训练时间的影响。作者将算法效果与系统效果分离，在等累计样本数下重调batch相关超参数（如学习率），发现采用sqrt学习率缩放可使学习曲线近似batch-size不变；同时利用rollout生成（内存带宽受限）与训练（算力随token数扩展）的不对称性，提出直接决策规则：更大batch只有在吞吐增益超过样本惩罚时才能降低训练时间。GRPO和PPO实验证明该分解有效，GRPO中结合更高吞吐与学习率重调可将训练时间降低最多29%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大batch通常被认为能降低随机梯度方差从而加速训练，但在RL for LLM场景中，更大batch每次更新消耗更多样本且可能执行更慢，统计收益是否能转化为实际端到端时间节省并不清楚。作者旨在阐明batch size在算法和系统两个维度上的权衡，以指导实际训练配置选择。

Method: 将batch size的影响分解为两个正交层面：(1) 算法层面：在固定累计样本数的条件下比较不同batch size配置，并重调依赖batch的超参数（如采用平方根学习率缩放），评估是否能在样本索引的学习轨迹上近似等价；(2) 系统层面：利用自回归生成（通常低并发时受内存带宽限制）与训练（算力随token数扩展）的计算不对称性，量化不同batch size下的吞吐量差异。基于二者提出决策规则：当更大的batch带来的吞吐增益超过其达到目标所需的样本数惩罚时，才值得采用。

Result: 实验在GRPO和PPO上验证了分解的有效性。算法层面，Adam结合平方根学习率缩放能在一定batch范围内产生近似batch-size不变的样本学习曲线。系统层面，更大batch可将生成吞吐提升最多2.29倍。在GRPO中，结合更高吞吐与学习率重调可将时间到目标降低最多29%；而不重调学习率时，即使吞吐更高，增大batch反而更慢。

Conclusion: 在RL for LLM中，batch size对训练时间的影响并非单纯由统计方差决定，而是需要通过算法和系统两层面联合评估。仅考虑吞吐收益可能误判，结合batch-dependent超参数重调（如学习率缩放）才能充分发挥大batch在系统层面的优势并实质加速训练。

Abstract: Larger batches reduce the variance of stochastic gradients per update and are therefore often expected to accelerate training. Yet whether this statistical benefit translates into lower wall-clock time-to-target remains unclear, because each update consumes more samples and may take longer to execute. We study this tradeoff in reinforcement learning for large language models. We separate its algorithmic and systems effects by comparing learning and execution along their natural axes. At the algorithmic level, we compare configurations at equal cumulative sample counts while retuning batch-dependent hyperparameters. Over a bounded range of batch sizes, this procedure yields an approximately batch-size-invariant family whose members follow similar sample-indexed learning trajectories. At the systems level, we exploit the computational asymmetry between rollout generation and training: autoregressive generation is often memory-bandwidth-bound at low concurrency, whereas training work scales approximately with the number of processed tokens. Combining these two views yields a direct decision rule: a larger-batch configuration reduces time-to-target only when its throughput gain exceeds its samples-to-target penalty. Experiments with GRPO and PPO support both sides of this decomposition. At the algorithmic level, square-root learning-rate scaling with Adam produces approximately batch-size-invariant learning curves over a bounded range of batch sizes. At the systems level, larger batches improve generation throughput by up to 2.29x on fixed hardware. In GRPO, combining higher throughput with learning-rate retuning reduces time-to-target by up to 29%, whereas increasing the batch without retuning is slower despite its higher throughput.

</details>


### [81] [Fully Distributed GNE Algorithms for Multi-Robot Placement without Consensus on Multipliers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29388)
*Shao-An Yin,Mingyi Hong,Nicola Elia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种全分布式的连续时间算法，用于求解具有共享线性等式约束的强单调博弈的广义纳什均衡问题（GNEP），该算法无需交换拉格朗日乘子即可收敛到任意GNE，降低了通信开销并提升了隐私性，同时提供了离散时间方案并在多机器人布置任务上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法计算共识型变分GNE（v-GNE）时依赖交换拉格朗日乘子，这带来了较高的通信开销和隐私泄露风险。为克服这些局限，需要设计一种无需乘子交换的分布式算法。

Method: 提出一种全分布式连续时间算法，针对共享线性等式约束采用共识机制但不交换乘子，可收敛到任意广义纳什均衡点；同时提供对应的离散时间迭代方案。

Result: 算法在多机器人布置任务上的仿真验证表明其有效性，能在不交换拉格朗日乘子的前提下收敛到GNE，同时降低通信成本。

Conclusion: 该方法通过消除乘子交换需求，在保持收敛性的同时显著减少通信开销并增强隐私保护，为强单调博弈中共享线性等式约束的GNEP求解提供了更优的分布式方案。

Abstract: Recent machine learning research has increasingly focused on equilibrium analysis in non-cooperative games rather than solely on optimal solutions. Many such problems involve shared constraints and can be formulated as Generalized Nash Equilibrium Problems (GNEPs). For strongly monotone games, existing methods compute consensus-based variational GNEs (v-GNEs) by exchanging Lagrange multipliers. We propose a fully distributed continuous-time algorithm for shared linear equality constraints that converges without multiplier exchange and reaches any GNE, reducing communication overhead and improving privacy. Discrete-time schemes are also provided, and the method is validated on a multi-robot placement task.

</details>


### [82] [Does Latent Planning Survive Point Clouds? Action-Conditioned JEPA World Models for Geometric Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29434)
*Fabio F. Oberweger,Michael Schwingshackl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将三种经典JEPA世界模型设计（冻结编码器、分布先验、动作敏感）迁移到点云几何观测上，验证了潜在空间规划在3D几何数据上的可行性。实验表明分布先验模型在所有基准上与图像对应模型统计等效，动作敏感模型在几何移动最多的场景中取得最强结果，且规划对随机丢失点具有鲁棒性，并能通过构造目标潜在向量实现无目标观测的3D目标导向规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA世界模型在图像领域的潜在空间规划已成熟，但能否适用于几何观测（点云）尚不明确。点云具有稀疏、无序、自遮挡等特点，且仅有0.3-15%场景点运动，使潜在预测的慢特征最优与3D自监督的几何捷径相互叠加，可能导致规划崩溃。需要探究潜在预测在几何观测下是否依然成立。

Method: 将三种经典JEPA设计（冻结编码器Frozen-Encoder、分布先验Distribution-Prior、动作敏感Action-Sensitive）提升到点云表示，并重新构建稳定世界模型基准，使得仅观测模态与图像基线不同，进行严格对照实验。另外设计了目标潜在构造方法，利用目标点云几何与当前潜在向量构造目标潜在表示。

Result: 三种JEPA模型在点云上均无崩溃现象；分布先验模型在全部基准上与重新评估的图像对应模型统计等效；动作敏感模型在几何移动最多的受控比较中取得最强结果；物体位置近乎完美线性可解码，注意力集中于少量运动点；规划对训练中未见过的高丢失率点云具有鲁棒性，但范围噪声会使最薄场景失效；通过构造目标潜在向量可实现几何目标导向规划且无成功率损失。

Conclusion: 潜在预测在几何观测（点云）上完全可行，点云JEPA世界模型能达到与图像基线相当甚至更优的表现。几何观测天然适合作为目标接口——可仅从目标几何与当前状态构造目标潜在，无需目标观测即可完成3D目标导向规划，为几何世界模型的应用开辟了道路。

Abstract: JEPA world models make latent-space planning a practical route to control, but they are built almost exclusively on images. Whether latent prediction survives geometric observations is unclear: point clouds are sparse, unordered, and self-occluded, and with 0.3-15% of scene points moving, the slow-feature optimum of latent prediction compounds with the geometric shortcut of 3D self-supervision. We lift three canonical JEPA designs to point clouds, frozen-encoder, distribution-prior, and action-sensitive, and re-sense the stable-worldmodel benchmark so that only the observation differs from the image baselines. All three plan without collapse: the distribution-prior model is statistically equivalent to its re-evaluated image counterpart on every benchmark, and the action-sensitive model attains the strongest result in our controlled comparison where the most geometry moves. Probing explains why: object positions are almost perfectly linearly decodable and attention falls on the few moving points. Planning withstands heavy dropout never seen in training, though range noise defeats the thinnest scene. Geometry finally makes a commanded 3D target a natural goal interface: we construct the goal latent from the target and the current latent, at no cost in success rate, without a goal observation.

</details>


### [83] [Reference-Grafting Matches Fine-Tuning at Eliciting Sandbagged Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29458)
*Linh Le,Hong Kiat Tan,David Williams-King*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"参考嫁接"(reference-grafting)方法，通过将激活沿对比方向对正到诚实参考值，在11个密码锁定的大模型（1.5-32B）上恢复了94-101%的诚实-装傻差距，匹配了微调诱发效果但无需权重更新或训练标签，仅需2-5对样例即可拟合方向。装傻是由阈值门控机制导致的，而非简单的线性偏移，这解释了为何加性激活引导和坐标置零会失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 装傻(sandbagging)行为会导致模型在评估中故意表现低下，威胁前沿模型治理所依赖的安全评估。此前"诱发游戏"(Elicitation Game)发现微调可以诱发隐藏能力但加性激活引导(activation steering)失败。作者希望寻找一种既能发现隐藏能力、又不需要权重更新或训练标签的干预方法，并理解为何此前方法失败的机制原因。

Method: 参考嫁接(reference-grafting)：将激活沿对比方向(contrast direction)的坐标设定为其在诚实参考中取值，在少量由主动学习选择的换路(circuits)上进行。涉及65M参数搜索空间，通过主动学习选择关键换路，对比方向由2-5对配对样例拟合。

Result: 在11个微调密码锁定模型（1.5-32B，三种架构谱系）上，参考嫁接恢复了+94%至+101%的诚实-装傻差距，匹配微调诱发效果但无需权重更新或训练标签。对强化学习诱导的装傻和密码锁定代码生成也有类似恢复效果。研究发现装傻由阈值门控实现：嫁接坐标需跨过接近诚实参考的阈值方可解锁，这解释了加性引导和坐标置零失败的原因。方向追踪装傻的诱导方式（提示、微调、强化学习各不同）而非被隐藏的内容。电路断裂(circuit-breaking)方法则因每次前向传播重路由激活而无法被固定编辑恢复。

Conclusion: 参考嫁接是一种高效、无需训练标签或权重更新的装傻能力恢复方法，且揭示了装傻的底层机制是阈值门控而非线性偏移。该方法对安全评估中检测和缓解装傻行为具有重要价值，但对于动态破坏电路的方法（如电路断裂）可能束手无策。

Abstract: Sandbagging, in which a model deliberately underperforms on an evaluation despite retaining the underlying capability, threatens the safety evaluations that frontier-model governance depends on. The Elicitation Game found that fine-tuning elicits hidden capability from sandbagging model organisms whereas additive activation steering fails. We revisit that verdict with reference-grafting, which sets an activation's coordinate along a contrast direction to the value it takes in an honest reference, at a small set of circuits chosen by active learning. Across eleven fine-tuned password-locked models (1.5-32B, three architecture lineages), it recovers +94 to +101% of the honest-sandbagging gap, matching fine-tuning elicitation without weight updates or training labels; two to five paired examples suffice to fit the direction. Similar recovery holds for reinforcement-learning-induced sandbagging and for password-locked code generation. Grafting works because the fine-tuned lock is a thresholded gate: held-out accuracy stays at the sandbagged level until the grafted coordinate crosses a threshold near the honest reference, which is why additive steering and zeroing the coordinate often fail. The direction tracks how the sandbagging was induced rather than what is withheld -- fit on grade-school science (ARC) it elicits withheld hazardous knowledge (WMDP), yet prompting, fine-tuning, and reinforcement learning each carry a different direction. Circuit-breaking marks the boundary: it reroutes activations on every forward pass, so the fixed edits we test are re-broken downstream and do not restore coherent generation.

</details>


### [84] [A Causal Model for Locating and Unlocking Sandbagging in Model Organisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29461)
*Hong Kiat Tan,Linh Le,David Williams-King*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种关于"沙袋效应"(sandbagging)的因果模型，解释模型如何在残差流中沿单一轴写入沙袋意图并由后续层读取，从而在评估中策略性降低表现。研究者测试了三种指令微调模型和四种安装沙袋锁的方式，并提出两种干预审计技术（单层参考嫁接和上下文嫁接）来恢复模型被隐藏的能力，其中上下文嫁接能在所有模型上完全恢复能力且对密码内容不敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿模型的部署和治理依赖于评估结果，但如果模型可以通过沙袋效应在评估中策略性表现不佳，同时保留其真实能力，那么评估结果将低估模型能力，威胁模型治理的可信度。理解沙袋效应的内部机制是设计有效审计技术的前提。

Method: 1) 提出残差流中的因果模型：早期层将沙袋意图写入残差流的单一轴，后期层读取该轴并决定回答。2) 在三种指令微调模型（Qwen2.5-7B, Llama-3-8B, Mistral-7B）上通过四种方式（提示、微调、强化学习、电路打断）安装沙袋锁。3) 提出两种干预：单层参考嫁接（将沙袋轴替换为诚实值）和上下文嫁接（重放密码的缓存键/值激活，使各层注意力将其作为额外上下文读取）。

Result: 在33次实验中的28次，单层参考嫁接在提示、微调和RL沙袋锁上恢复了模型能力，中位恢复率为96%。电路打断式沙袋锁通过一个层带重写整个状态，单层嫁接在所有层均失败。上下文嫁接则在所有三个模型上完全恢复能力，且对密码具体内容不敏感。

Conclusion: 因果模型为理解沙袋效应的内部机制提供了可靠框架，且该模型可指导审计者设计有效的干预性审计技术来揭穿沙袋模型。单层参考嫁接和上下文嫁接分别适用于不同类型的沙袋锁，扩大了可审计沙袋模型的覆盖范围。

Abstract: Sandbagging models strategically underperform on evaluations while retaining the capabilities being measured. The evaluations that guide frontier-model deployment and governance then understate what these models can do. To understand the mechanism, we propose a causal model of how sandbagging is carried in the residual stream. Early layers write the sandbagging intent onto a single axis of the stream, and a later layer reads that axis and commits the answer. We study three instruction-tuned models (Qwen2.5-7B, Llama-3-8B, and Mistral-7B) and four ways of installing a sandbagging lock (prompting, fine-tuning, reinforcement learning, and circuit breaking). Each lock decides from the prompt whether to sandbag, and the fine-tuned and circuit-broken locks answer honestly whenever a password appears. The causal model predicts a window of layers, after the last sandbagging write and before the answer commit, in which a single-layer reference graft of the sandbagging axis to its honest value restores the full capability. The single-layer graft recovers the capability in 28 of the 33 runs of the prompted, fine-tuned, and RL-trained locks, with a median held-out recovery of 96%. The circuit-broken lock rewrites the whole state through a band of layers, and the single-layer graft fails at every layer. We therefore introduce a second intervention, context grafting, which replays the password's cached key/value activations so that every layer's attention reads them as additional context. Context grafting provably and empirically restores the full capability on all three models, and the recovery is surprisingly insensitive to the exact password content. More broadly, an auditor can use this causal model to design interventional auditing techniques for sandbagging models.

</details>


### [85] [Asynchronous Cooperative Online Learning for Multi-Robot Control under Computational Delays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29562)
*Xiaobing Dai,Zewen Yang,Wei Ren,Sandra Hirche*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向不确定环境下多智能体系统的异步协同学习与控制策略，利用高斯过程回归实现预测精度、查询点差异和通信延迟的显式建模，并基于伴随多智能体系统设计分布式控制律，在无人水面艇仿真中验证了显著优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统在不确性环境中运行面临外部扰动和模型不精确的挑战，现有分布式高斯过程聚合方法忽略了异构计算延迟和查询点差异，导致学习与控制性能受影响。

Method: 提出异步协同学习策略，显式考虑预测精度、查询点变化和延迟效应；基于伴随多智能体系统开发分布式控制律以保证期望控制性能。

Result: 在无人水面艇上开展的仿真实验验证了所提方法的有效性，相比最先进方法，学习性能和控制性能均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模异构延迟和查询点差异，异步协同学习策略能有效提升不确定环境下多智能体系统的学习与控制可靠性。

Abstract: Ensuring the safe operation of multi-agent systems (MASs) under uncertain environments is crucial for cooperative robotic, where external disturbances and inaccurate dynamic models can significantly compromise performance and reliability. To address this challenge, calibrated machine learning models, particularly Gaussian process (GP) regression, are extensively employed due to their interpretable performance quantification. As the interconnected communication of MASs facilitates cooperative learning, agents are able to enhance learning performance by exchanging local GP inferences with their neighbors and aggregating the received information via distributed GP strategies. However, variations in computational power and prediction tasks among agents inevitably lead to heterogeneous computational delays and differences in query points, which are often overlooked in existing aggregation methods. To overcome these limitations, this work proposes an asynchronous cooperative learning strategy that explicitly accounts for prediction accuracy, query point variations and delay effects. Additionally, a distributed control law based on an adjoint MAS is developed to ensure the desired control performance. Simulations on unmanned surface vehicles validate the effectiveness of the proposed approach, demonstrating substantial improvements in both learning and control performance compared to the state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [86] [Event-triggered Control and Online Learning for Networked Systems under Computational Delays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29576)
*Xiaobing Dai,Armin Lederer,Zewen Yang,Sihua Zhang,Lu Wan,Yang Tang,Sandra Hirche*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对在线学习控制中的计算延迟问题，提出了一种端到端（in-network）在线学习控制架构，将学习控制器部署在远程计算节点上，通过通信信道连接。论文首次推导了该架构下考虑计算延迟的跟踪误差界，并在该界下支持多种通信与计算策略（含时间/事件触发机制）。进一步提出了一种在控制和在线学习中均采用异步事件触发机制的高效控制框架，在存在计算延迟的情况下实现了与时间触发场景相同的控制性能，同时排除了Zeno行为，并通过仿真验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线学习控制能有效处理不确定系统，但资源密集型在线学习算法在本地计算资源受限时会产生不可忽略的计算延迟，严重影响控制性能。现有工作缺乏对这种带延迟的端到端在线学习控制架构的理论性能保证，因此需要建立考虑计算延迟的跟踪误差界并对通信与计算资源使用进行权衡优化。

Method: 1) 建立端到端在线学习控制架构的跟踪误差界，同时显式考虑计算延迟的影响；2) 在推导的误差界条件下，论证不同通信/计算策略（时间触发与事件触发）的适用性；3) 设计异步事件触发机制，分别应用于控制更新和在线学习更新，并对该策略进行稳定性分析；4) 证明所提出的事件触发策略能达到与时间触发相同的控制性能且排除Zeno行为；5) 对指数可稳定系统推导出事件触发条件的显式表达式，并通过仿真验证。

Result: 推导出了端到端在线学习控制架构下考虑计算延迟的跟踪误差界；证明了在特定条件下可支持多样化的通信与计算策略；揭示了给定期望控制性能下通信与计算性能之间的权衡关系；提出的异步事件触发策略被证明与时间触发场景具有相同的控制性能，同时排除Zeno行为；为指数可稳定系统提供了事件触发条件的显式表达式，仿真结果验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为端到端在线学习控制架构提供了严格的理论性能保障，解决了计算延迟带来的控制性能退化问题。通过异步事件触发机制在控制与学习两个层面同时降低通信与计算开销，且不牺牲控制性能，为资源受限系统的在线学习控制提供了既高效又可靠的理论框架。

Abstract: Online learning-based control is a promising approach to control uncertain systems, where unknown components are identified during operation to improve control performance. However, resource-intensive online learning algorithms introduce non-negligible computational delays, especially when executed on systems with limited local computational resources. To mitigate this, an in-network online learning-based control structure is employed by deploying the learning-based controller on a remote computation node and connecting it via a communication channel. In this paper, control performance guarantee is first established by deriving tracking error bound for the in-network control architecture, while accounting for computational delays. The derived tracking error bound allows for diverse communication and computation strategies under a specific condition, including time-/event-triggered mechanisms. Additionally, the trade-off between communication and computation performances is shown for a given desired control performance. Furthermore, to enhance the efficiency in both communication and computation, an efficient control framework with an asynchronous event-triggered mechanism in both control and online learning is devised under the existence of computational delay. The proposed event-triggered strategy is proven to achieve the same control performance as time-triggered scenario while excluding Zeno behavior. Finally, we derive an explicit expression of the proposed event-trigger condition for exponentially stabilizable systems, and demonstrate its effectiveness through simulations.

</details>


### [87] [Uncertainty-Driven Replay Memory for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29860)
*Sheeraja Rajakrishnan,Alexander G. Ororbia,Travis Desell,Daniel E. Krutz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于不确定性驱动的经验回放缓冲区（UDRM），通过利用RL训练过程中的不确定性估计来偏向存储更不确定的转换，从而提高RL智能体的泛化能力和训练奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不确定性估计能够帮助强化学习智能体减少训练时间并在训练过程中获得更高的奖励——通过区分环境中已知和未知区域的动作。现有RL方法通常使用时序差分误差或转换分布来更新回放缓冲区，缺乏利用不确定性信息来优化记忆存储的机制。

Method: 提出UDRM（不确定性驱动的回放记忆）方案，该方案基于RL模型在训练过程中获得的不确定性估计来更新内部存储的记忆，使回放缓冲区偏向存储更多不确定性较高的转换，从而提升智能体在训练过程中的泛化能力。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的不确定性感知回放缓冲区使RL智能体在训练过程中获得比现有不确定性感知RL框架更高的奖励。

Conclusion: 通过不确定性驱动的经验回放缓冲区设计，可以有效利用不确定性信息改进RL训练过程，相比现有的不确定性感知方法取得更优的训练性能。

Abstract: Uncertainty estimation provides promising capabilities for reinforcement learning (RL) agents. Notably, estimating uncertainty can reduce the training time and enable agents to obtain greater rewards over time by exploiting information related to whether an action would facilitate exploration of portions of an environment that are well-known versus those that are relatively unknown. In this work, we propose a novel formulation of the experience replay buffer commonly used in RL that we call uncertainty-driven replay memory (UDRM), which entails an update scheme for internally stored memories based on uncertainty estimates obtained by an RL model during training. In contrast to existing forms of RL, which typically use temporal difference error or the distribution of transitions to update the replay memory buffer and train RL controllers, our scheme biases the memory buffer to store more uncertain transitions that will improve an RL agent's generalization throughout training. Experimental results demonstrate that our proposed uncertainty-aware replay buffer enables an RL agent to obtain higher rewards during training compared to other existing uncertainty-aware RL frameworks.

</details>


### [88] [The Intervention Gap in Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29998)
*Donna Vakalis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"规划期干预保真度"这一概念，即世界模型自身的开环转移是否像真实环境干预一样推动任务变量。研究发现该属性无法由奖励拟合或任务锚定训练保证，并通过多种实验（TD-MPC2、LeWorldModel、PreJEPA、DreamerV3）证明了其可独立测量、需直接审计的特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型评估主要依赖奖励预测误差或任务锚定训练效果，但这些指标无法揭示模型在规划期（开环想象中）的干预行为是否与真实环境一致。作者希望提出一个新的可直接测量的属性——干预保真度，作为独立于奖励拟合的诊断维度，帮助定位世界模型失败的根本原因。

Method: 引入"捕获门控匹配干预审计"方法：(1) 先捕获当前任务查询相关的模型内部表征；(2) 在模型原生接口上施加与环境干预匹配的干预；(3) 比较模型想象的多步效果与环境端点oracle的表现。同时对比任务锚定模型与自监督模型、不同检查点和种子下的表现，并在DreamerV3上检验后验分布与观测采样、集成不一致性等实践侧问题。

Result: 三个LeWorldModel检查点能捕获任务查询并解码真实干预效果，但其五步想象效果比"预测无效"还差，也比环境端点oracle差；失败模式是任务方向旋转加过度增益而非特征坍缩。该模式有条件性：五个PreJEPA种子保持oracle相关缺陷但不具有该模式；Finger Spin结果显示缺陷超越运动学且种子间异质性严重。在DreamerV3上，后验分布（而非样本）携带当前查询，集成不一致性仅在训练支持附近有效排序误差，冻结的支持感知评分在两种迁移方向上均损害排序能力。

Conclusion: 干预保真度必须在模型原生接口上以"捕获优先"的方式进行直接审计，不能依赖奖励拟合或任务锚定训练作为代理指标。该属性是一种独立可测量的世界模型性质，对诊断和改善规划性能至关重要。

Abstract: Planning-time intervention fidelity is a distinct, measurable property of a learned world model: whether the model's own open-loop transitions move task variables the way matched environment interventions do. In the settings we test, it is neither revealed by reward fit nor ensured by task-anchored training. Across released TD-MPC2 checkpoint sizes, episode return falls as an operator-error diagnostic on task observables grows, while reward-prediction error stays small and nearly flat, and a self-supervised world model trained without task signal preserves the same operator substantially better than a task-anchored model on the shared task. A capture-gated matched-intervention audit then localizes what fails. On Cheetah, three LeWorldModel checkpoints capture the current task query and support decodable real intervention effects; however, their imagined five-step effects are worse than predicting no effect and worse than an environment-endpoint oracle. The failure is task-direction rotation with excess gain, not feature collapse. This severe pattern is conditional: five PreJEPA seeds retain an oracle-relative deficit without it, Finger Spin experiments extend the deficit beyond locomotion with heterogeneous severity across seeds, and shared-bank effect geometry is both candidate- and support-dependent. We also test practice-side questions. In DreamerV3 the posterior distribution, not its sample, carries the current query; ensemble disagreement ranks error only near training support; and a frozen support-aware score degrades held-out error ranking in both tested transfer directions while native disagreement remains informative in both. We conclude that intervention fidelity must be audited directly, capture-first, on the model's native interface.

</details>


### [89] [Forget or Fine-tune? A Comparative Study of Machine Unlearning Strategies for Noisy Label Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30046)
*João L. P. Santana,Filipe R. Cordeiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对五种机器遗忘（MU）方法在噪声标签校正中的效果进行了系统性实证比较，发现最优遗忘策略取决于噪声结构：在大部分闭集噪声下FT是强基线，RL和SalUn在实例依赖噪声下接近重训练的精度且计算成本大幅降低，而开集噪声下重训练目标不合适。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络训练中噪声标签会导致模型记忆错误信息并降低泛化能力。传统方案是在识别噪声样本后从头重训练模型，但随着数据集和模型规模增大，重训练计算成本日益昂贵。机器遗忘虽作为高效替代方案出现，但不同遗忘策略在噪声标签校正中的相对效果尚不清楚，缺乏系统的比较研究来指导方法选择。

Method: 本文在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和真实噪声数据集Food-101N上，对五种机器遗忘方法（NegGrad、Fine-Tuning、Random Labeling、SalUn和MUNBa）在四种噪声类型（对称、非对称、实例依赖和开集噪声）下进行对比实证研究。

Result: 核心发现：最优遗忘策略取决于噪声结构。FT在多数闭集场景下是强基线；RL和SalUn最稳健，在实例依赖噪声下能以极低计算成本接近重训练精度；MUNBa仅在极端对称噪声下表现优势；开集噪声下，重训练反而会降低精度，因此逼近重训练模型在此场景下不是合适目标；在Food-101N上所有MU方法均接近重训练精度且运行时间降低一个数量级。

Conclusion: 研究为训练后噪声标签校正场景下的机器遗忘策略选择提供了实用指南，指出应依据噪声结构选择不同的遗忘方法，并揭示了开集噪声下以重训练为目标本身的不合理性。

Abstract: Noisy labels remain a critical challenge for training deep neural networks, since memorizing incorrect labels degrades generalization. Once noisy samples are identified after training, the standard solution is to retrain the model from scratch on the cleaned dataset, which is increasingly expensive as datasets and models grow. Machine Unlearning (MU) has recently emerged as a computationally efficient alternative, but the relative effectiveness of different MU strategies for noisy-label correction remains poorly understood. In this work, we conduct a comparative empirical study of five MU methods (NegGrad, Fine-Tuning (FT), Random Labeling (RL), SalUn, and MUNBa) across symmetric, asymmetric, instance-dependent, and open-set noise on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and the real-world noisy dataset Food-101N. Our central finding is that the appropriate unlearning strategy is conditioned on the noise structure. Simple FT is a strong baseline across most closed-set scenarios; RL and SalUn are the most consistently robust methods and, under instance-dependent noise, approach retraining accuracy at a fraction of the computational cost; MUNBa shows advantages mainly under extreme symmetric noise. Under open-set noise, in contrast, we show that retraining on the cleaned subset degrades accuracy relative to the noisy baseline, so approximating the retrained model is not an adequate objective in this regime. On Food-101N, all MU methods remain competitive and achieve accuracies close to retraining despite reducing runtime by an order of magnitude. These findings provide practical guidelines for selecting MU strategies for post-training noisy-label correction.

</details>


### [90] [How do World Models and Policies Compose in LLM Agents? A Joint Spectral and Behavioral Account](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30067)
*Ruize Xu,Xiao Yu,Yujin Tang,Chenming Shang,Nikhil Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过受控实验研究LLM智能体如何同时理解环境（世界模型训练）和掌握任务（策略训练），发现这两类更新在参数空间中呈几何互补关系：共享输入特征子空间但写入近似正交的输出方向。同时发现顺序训练比分离训练更具鲁棒性，并提出基于几何基础的训练免费合并方法和在线世界模型损失，两者均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解LLM智能体如何在理解环境与掌握任务之间建立联系是构建可靠智能体的关键问题。现有方法通常将世界模型训练与策略训练分开或简单地顺序进行，但两者之间的参数更新如何相互作用、是否互补、以及是否能在不损失世界知识的前提下提升任务能力，尚未被系统研究。

Method: 通过受控实验结合世界模型训练（下一状态预测）和策略训练（奖励最大化）。使用加性参数更新分解来分析模型，进行几何分析以考察两类更新的低秩结构和输入/输出子空间关系；进行投影干预测试鲁棒性；设计基于几何输入基的训练免费合并方法，并在策略RL中引入在线世界模型损失。

Result: 1) 有效世界模型更新是低秩的，与策略更新共享输入特征子空间，但写入近似正交的输出方向（分离或顺序训练均如此）。2) 顺序训练在移除世界模型主导输入方向时比分离的策略RL更具鲁棒性，表明学到了替代输入路径。3) 顺序训练的智能体探索更广的状态和动作空间。4) 基于几何激励的输入基的训练免费合并以及在线世界模型损失均优于未处理的基线。

Conclusion: 世界知识和任务导向能力可以在几何上以互补形式学习，两类更新共享输入但不干扰输出。未来的后训练流程应精心设计世界模型与策略之间的接口，以同时保持世界理解能力和任务表现。

Abstract: How do LLM agents come to both understand environments they act in and master tasks set within them? Through controlled experiments combining world-model training (next-state prediction) and policy training (reward maximization), we investigate this question. We dissect the resulting models through their additive parameter updates. Geometrically, we find effective world-model updates are low-rank and share an input-feature subspace with policy updates while writing to nearly orthogonal output directions, whether trained separately or sequentially. However, we find that, in projection interventions, the sequential update induces more robustness than separate policy RL when removing the world model's leading input directions, suggesting that it has learned alternative input pathways. Behaviorally, we find the sequentially trained agent explores a wider range of states and actions. Based on this, we ask: does policy training preserve world knowledge as well as it could? We probe this with training-free merging built on the geometrically motivated input basis plus an online world-model loss during policy RL, and show both improve over the untreated baseline. Our findings suggest world knowledge and task-directed ability can be learned in geometrically complementary forms, and that future post-training pipelines should consider how best to engineer the interface between them.

</details>


### [91] [Reinforcement Learning for Symbolic Equation Solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30162)
*Kevin P O Keeffe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个强化学习智能体，能够逐步求解符号方程，覆盖闭合方程与四类受限开放方程族（二次、三次、四次、指数型），采用树结构策略（TreeMLP）仅从奖励中学习，无需监督求解轨迹；在闭合方程上与ConPoLe持平，在开放方程上超越A*搜索，但明确不声称能求解一般开放方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有符号方程求解方法要么依赖大量监督求解轨迹，要么搜索效率低；需要一种无需监督轨迹、仅靠奖励信号即可学习逐步求解的策略，同时探索超越闭合方程（如换元法）的受限开放方程求解能力。

Method: 将代数求解建模为MDP，采用动态动作空间；策略使用树结构MLP（TreeMLP）从纯奖励中学习逐步求解步骤；换元（CoV）来自监督生成器，且可与CAS调用互换；涵盖闭合方程（根式、指数、三角）及四类受限开放方程族，并引入UCB学习进度课程缓解10倍规模下的种子级双峰现象。

Result: 闭合方程上单一策略在CommonCore达到0.93（贪心），超过ConPoLe的0.925；四类手设计开放方程族上达到0.79（beam）/0.67（贪心），超过最强非学习搜索A*（0.64）；仅在需要嵌套换元的指数族中，学习到的换元时机有实质性贡献，策略从纯奖励中解决75%的留出方程，而自然规则解率为0。

Conclusion: 纯奖励驱动的树结构策略能有效求解闭合方程及四类受限开放方程，学习到的换元时机在嵌套换元场景中显著优于自然规则；但10倍规模下出现种子级双峰现象，UCB课程缓解效果不显著；所有开放方程结论严格限定于四类受控族，不推广至一般开放方程。

Abstract: We present a reinforcement-learning agent that solves symbolic equations step by step, covering both nonlinear closed equations (radicals, exponentials, trigonometric) and a controlled class of restricted-open families requiring a change of variables (CoV) such as completing the square. We cast algebra as an MDP with a dynamic action space and a tree-structured policy (TreeMLP). The main policy learns from reward alone with no supervised solution traces; the CoV substitution comes from a supervised generator interchangeable with a CAS call. On closed equations the agent matches the prior best on CommonCore (0.93 greedy vs. ConPoLe's 0.925) under a single policy. On four hand-designed restricted-open families (quadratic, cubic, quartic, exponential) it reaches 0.79 beam / 0.67 greedy, exceeding the strongest non-learned search (A-star, 0.64). Learned CoV timing has content only on the exponential family, the one requiring a nested CoV, where a natural rule solves none of the held-out equations while the policy solves 75% from reward alone. At 10x scale a sharp seed-level bimodality emerges; a UCB learning-progress curriculum shows a non-significant positive trend toward mitigating it. We do not claim general open-equation solving: every open-equation result is confined to these four controlled families.

</details>


### [92] [BCPPO: Bachelier-Inspired Constrained Proximal Policy Optimization for Tail-Risk-Aware Safe Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30283)
*Dongsheng Hou,Yanqiao Chen,Yuhan Rui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BCPPO（Bachelier启发的约束近端策略优化），通过分离初始化的成本预测网络（critics）训练中的分歧，结合Bachelier公式将不确定度转化为平滑的策略更新惩罚项，在不改变TD critics学习的前提下实现约束优化，部署时仅保留策略网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 期望成本约束仍可能允许罕见的高成本事件；蒙特卡洛条件风险价值（CVaR）梯度在高置信度下可能噪声较大，而建模结果分布的critics会增加复杂度。因此需要一种既能控制尾部风险又保持简洁的方法。

Method: BCPPO包括：(1) 分离初始化的成本预测网络（critics），通过随机样本掩码训练，其分歧标记预测对训练数据中出现的状态-动作区域以及critic训练的敏感性；(2) 使用Bachelier公式计算参考水平以上的期望金额，将分歧转换为平滑的策略更新惩罚；(3) 惩罚梯度不改变critics，保证TD critic学习不变；(4) 饱和感知控制器调整平均成本惩罚，在惩罚被裁剪时防止累积误差增长；(5) 部署时仅保留策略网络。

Result: 在175次运行（共享任务、成本、预算、训练步数和评估种子）中，没有比较方法在任意任务中同时获得比BCPPO更高的平均回报和更低的平均CVaR。在Push1任务上，BCPPO相比所有比较方法都没有更低的回报和更高的CVaR，且至少有一个严格指标占优。

Conclusion: BCPPO在奖励、成本预测不确定性（跨训练critics的差异）和策略单网络部署之间实现了实用的平衡。需要注意的是，分歧惩罚既不是尾部事件概率也不能保证误差界，不提供安全保障。

Abstract: Expected-cost constraints can still permit rare, high-cost events. Monte Carlo conditional value at risk (CVaR) gradients can be noisy at high confidence, whereas critics that model an outcome distribution add complexity. We propose BCPPO (Bachelier-Inspired Constrained Proximal Policy Optimization), a proximal policy optimization (PPO) method. Separately initialized cost-prediction networks (critics), trained with random sample masks, produce disagreement that marks predictions sensitive to which state-action regions occur in the training data and to critic training. A Bachelier formula for the expected amount above a reference level converts this disagreement into a smooth policy-update penalty. Gradients from this penalty do not alter the critics, so temporal-difference (TD) critic learning is unchanged. A saturation-aware controller adjusts the mean-cost penalty and stops accumulated error from growing while that penalty is clipped. Deployment retains only the policy network. The disagreement penalty is neither a tail-event probability nor a guaranteed error bound, and it provides no safety guarantee. Across 175 runs with shared tasks, costs, budgets, training steps, and evaluation seeds, no comparator attains both higher mean return and lower mean CVaR than BCPPO in any task. On Push1, BCPPO has no lower return and no higher CVaR than every comparator, with at least one strict gain. These results support a practical balance among reward, caution around cost predictions that vary across trained critics, and policy-only deployment.

</details>


### [93] [Online Estimation of Dynamic Origin-Destination Matrices Using Reinforcement Learning with Link-Flow Propagation Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30317)
*Donggyu Min,Dong-Kyu Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LFPG-RL方法，将链路流量传播引导（LFPG）集成到近端策略优化（PPO）中，用于在线动态OD矩阵估计。该方法将聚合的链路流量误差转化为OD特定的优势整形信号，仅需单次前向传播即可在线部署，在墨尔本路网250个工作日的15分钟链路流量数据上取得了RMSE 4.69、MAPE 20.15%、皮尔逊相关系数0.995的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线动态OD矩阵估计（DODE）需要从当前观测和传播的网络状态中估计时变OD需求，但后续观测和随机动态网络加载（DNL）结果存在不确定性。强化学习虽能降低计算负担并适用于随机环境，但因其策略是离线训练、在线部署，需应对变化的目标链路流量轨迹——不同目标轨迹会定义不同的奖励误差，相同OD需求向量可能需要不同的调整，导致传统标量反馈存在歧义，影响策略学习的有效性。

Method: 提出LFPG-RL方法，将链路流量传播引导（LFPG）集成到近端策略优化（PPO）中。LFPG结合链路流量误差敏感性（link-flow error sensitivities）与每个OD-时间需求分量对仿真链路流量的贡献，将聚合的不匹配信息转化为OD特定的优势整形（advantage shaping）信号，用于PPO actor更新。部署时策略仅需单次前向传播。

Result: 在墨尔本主干路网上，使用链路传输模型（link transmission model）结合随机路径选择建模，对250个工作日15分钟链路流量数据进行训练和评估。在保留轨迹上，LFPG-RL达到RMSE为4.69，MAPE为20.15%，皮尔逊相关系数为0.995。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，LFPG-RL是一种比现有方法更高效、更准确的在线路OD需求校准方法，其单次前向传播的部署方式有效降低了计算负担，同时通过OD特定的优势整形解决了传统标量奖励反馈的歧义问题。

Abstract: Online dynamic origin-destination (OD) matrix estimation (DODE) calibrates time-dependent OD demand to reproduce observed link-flow trajectories. In online, OD demand should be estimated from current observations and propagated network states while subsequent observations and stochastic dynamic network loading (DNL) outcomes remain uncertain. Recently, reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a promising alternative, reducing computational burden by replacing iterative algorithms while being applicable to stochastic environments. However, because the policy is trained offline and deployed online, it must handle varying target link-flow trajectories; since each target trajectory defines the link-flow error used in the reward, the same OD demand vector can require different adjustments, making conventional scalar feedback ambiguous. To address this gap, this study proposes LFPG-RL, which integrates link-flow propagation guidance (LFPG) into proximal policy optimization (PPO). LFPG combines link-flow error sensitivities with the contribution of each OD-time demand component to simulated link flows, transforming aggregate mismatch into OD-specific advantage shaping for PPO actor updates. At deployment, the policy requires only a single forward pass. LFPG-RL is developed and evaluated on 250 weekday trajectories of 15-min link-flow data from a Melbourne arterial network modeled by a link transmission model with stochastic route choice. On held-out trajectories, LFPG-RL achieved an RMSE of 4.69, MAPE of 20.15%, and Pearson correlation of 0.995. These results support the contention that our method is a more efficient and accurate online OD demand calibration method compared to existing ones.

</details>


### [94] [Locally-Guided Actor-Critic: Training a Goal-conditioned Actor with a Subgoal-aware Critic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30406)
*Olivier Serris,Stéphane Doncieux,Olivier Sigaud*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对目标条件强化学习在稀疏奖励长时程任务中的难题，分析了两类现有方法（基于动作正则化的RIS和基于势函数奖励塑形的PBRS）的失败案例，提出了一种名为Locally-Guided Actor Critic (LG-AC)的新方法，通过显式地将值函数分解为子目标条件值函数之和，实现密集的hindsight重标注，在具有挑战性的目标链任务中取得了最佳综合性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标条件强化学习在奖励稀疏且时程较长时表现不佳。规划器可以在训练时提供子目标辅助学习，但测试时引入规划器会带来子目标管理困难；而不依赖规划器的方法（如RIS和PBRS）又分别存在目标链失败和终端状态欺骗性奖励的问题，亟需一种既能利用子目标信息又不牺牲部署简便性的方法。

Method: 本文首先分析并验证了RIS的动作正则化在低维中间目标下导致目标链问题（goal-chaining issues）以及PBRS在终端状态产生欺骗性奖励的失败案例。在此基础上提出两种方法：（1）一种改进的奖励塑形方法（RS），去除终端状态的欺骗性奖励，但牺牲了PBRS的理论最优性保证；（2）Locally-Guided Actor Critic (LG-AC)，显式地将值函数表示为子目标条件值函数之和，使值估计器以完整子目标序列为条件，同时支持密集的hindsight重标注。

Result: 在具有挑战性的目标链需求任务上评估了所有方法，实验结果显示RIS的动作正则化与PBRS的奖励塑形各自在特定情况下表现不佳，而LG-AC在所有任务中取得了最好的综合性能。

Conclusion: LG-AC通过将值函数分解为子目标条件值函数的和，实现了对中间目标奖励的显式利用与密集hindsight重标注，在避免RIS和PBRS各自失败模式的同时取得了最优的性能，为目标条件强化学习中的规划器辅助学习提供了一种更稳健的范式。

Abstract: Goal-conditioned reinforcement learning struggles with long horizons when rewards are sparse. While a planner can provide subgoals to guide a low-level policy, its use at test time may introduce practical subgoal management difficulties. An alternative paradigm utilizes a high-level planner to assist learning, while the policy remains conditioned only on the final goal, enabling planner-free deployment. Among these methods, Reinforcement Learning with Imagined Subgoals (RIS) introduces a regularization term that encourages the policy to take the same actions for the final goal as it does for an intermediate goal. This regularization, however, may lead to goal-chaining issues when intermediate goals are low-dimensional. Potential-based reward shaping (PBRS) translates plans into an additional reward while ensuring that the optimal policy remains unchanged. Yet, it can generate deceptive rewards in terminal states. We study these failure cases and first propose an alternative reward shaping method (RS) that removes these deceptive rewards at the expense of theoretical guarantees of PBRS. Similar to this RS variant, we then propose another method named Locally-Guided Actor Critic (LG-AC) that rewards the agent for reaching intermediate goals. Unlike RS, where intermediate rewards are implicit in the shaping signal, we explicitly condition a value estimator on the full sequence of intermediate goals but represent the value function as a sum of subgoal-conditioned value functions, enabling dense hindsight relabeling. We evaluate all these methods in tasks with challenging goal-chaining requirements and empirically highlight specific cases in which either action regularization or reward shaping yield low performance, while LG-AC achieves the best overall performance across tasks.

</details>


### [95] [Confounding Masquerading as Improvement: A Systematic Evaluation of Offline Reinforcement Learning for Stroke Antithrombotic Treatment in a 129,000-Patient Registry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30442)
*Kihun Rhee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对5类离线强化学习算法家族和14种奖励设计在44,894名急性缺血性卒中患者（来自129,033例全国注册数据）上进行了系统性、部分交叉的评估，发现标准FQE估计的临床策略改进表面上是正信号，但进一步分析揭示这是由奖励嵌入混杂（reward-embedded confounding）引起的假象。在去混杂后，改进信号衰减至统计不显著（p=0.291），最终结论是离线RL策略并未实现有临床意义的聚合改进，并提供了六步评估检查清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来离线强化学习在临床领域的研究声称其策略优于医生决策，但这些结论可能受评估偏差影响。作者旨在通过大规模真实卒中患者数据，系统地检验离线RL策略改进信号是否真实存在，揭示潜在的评估方法学陷阱（尤其是奖励设计中编码基线严重程度与预后的混杂问题），为临床RL评估建立稳健的可复现框架。

Method: 采用部分交叉设计，对5类离线RL算法家族（含FQE等）和14种奖励设计进行系统评估。使用标准Fitted Q-Evaluation（FQE）估计策略改进；通过2×2因子分析量化终末奖励混杂的贡献；运用DML启发的GBM奖励残差化方法去除奖励中的混杂；结合T-learner诊断、直接复发分析、1年mRS因子分析以及按NIHSS分层分析进行多维度交叉验证。

Result: 标准FQE给出+0.0069的表观策略改进，加入早期神经功能恶化惩罚后升至+0.0101；但2×2因子分析显示终末奖励混杂占了观察到的信号变化的218.6%。经DML-GBM残差化后FQE估计降至+0.0033（p=0.132），完全去混杂后为+0.0025（p=0.291），均不显著。FQE诊断、T-learner分析及复发分析一致支持缺乏有临床意义的聚合改进；1年mRS分析复现了衰减结果。NIHSS分层异质性为前瞻性试验设计提供假设，医院层面分歧在完全去混杂后消失。

Conclusion: 离线RL声称的卒中临床策略改进很大程度上是由奖励嵌入混杂（即终末奖励同时编码基线严重程度与治疗效果）所驱动的伪信号。去混杂后，改进证据不成立。作者据此提出一个经验驱动的六步评估清单，呼吁未来临床RL研究在报告策略改进时必须系统性地检验和处理奖励混杂，以避免误导性的临床结论。

Abstract: Recent offline reinforcement learning (RL) studies report policies that outperform physician decisions on clinical outcomes. We conduct a systematic, partially crossed evaluation of five offline RL algorithm families and 14 reward designs in 44,894 post-2018 acute ischemic stroke patients from a nationwide registry (N = 129,033).
  Standard Fitted Q-Evaluation (FQE) yields an apparent policy-improvement estimate of +0.0069; adding an Early Neurological Deterioration penalty increases it to +0.0101. We identify reward-embedded confounding, in which a proxy terminal reward encodes baseline severity and prognosis as well as treatment efficacy. A 2 x 2 factorial analysis finds that terminal reward confounding accounts for 218.6% of the observed signal change, so its removal overshoots the null.
  After DML-inspired GBM reward residualization, the FQE estimate attenuates to +0.0033 (p = 0.132), and full deconfounding yields +0.0025 (p = 0.291). FQE-based diagnostics, T-learner analyses, and direct recurrence analyses converge away from a clinically meaningful aggregate improvement. A 1-year mRS factorial analysis replicates the attenuation. We provide an empirically motivated six-step evaluation checklist. NIHSS-stratified heterogeneity is hypothesis-generating for prospective trial design; hospital-level disagreement does not persist after full reward deconfounding.

</details>


### [96] [Learning Where Outcomes Change:Credit-Addressable Reasoning for Multimodal Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30457)
*Jiani Guo,Junjie Wang,Jie Wu,Pengxiang Zhao,Dongdong Zhang,Shaohan Huang,Yujiu Yang,Furu Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"可信用寻址推理"（credit-addressable reasoning）框架，通过Code-CoT将多模态几何推理表示为可寻址的代码形式，并配合CE-GRPO在结构先验和类型归一化熵选定的事件边界处分配梯度信用。在九个几何基准上平均准确率达76.04，超越Qwen3-VL-8B 8.09分、超越轨迹级GRPO 3.43分，且优势随中间事件数量增加而扩大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态几何推理中，自由形式的推理轨迹模糊了决定答案的关键决策；轨迹级强化学习将单一终端信号均摊到整个响应上，无法精确区分哪些推理步骤贡献了最终结果。缺乏将推理单元与信用分配位置对齐的机制。

Method: 提出可信用寻址推理原则：推理过程中暴露的语义单元同时定义了学习比较备选方案和分配信用的位置。具体实现包括两个组件：(1) Code-CoT——保留图表、将视觉关系表示为可按行寻址的可执行代码、将推理组织为带类型的事件；(2) CE-GRPO——利用结构先验和类型归一化熵选择事件边界、从共享前缀采样完整续接、将结果差异转化为局部优势值。

Result: 在九个几何基准数据集上，CE-GRPO平均准确率达到76.04，比Qwen3-VL-8B高8.09个百分点，比轨迹级GRPO高3.43个百分点。相对优势随中间事件数量的增加而增大，验证了表示-优化协同设计对长链路、强依赖多模态推理的有效性。

Conclusion: 表示与优化的一致设计（representation-optimization co-design）对于长链条、强依赖的多模态推理至关重要。通过将推理单元显式化并使其与信用分配对齐，可以显著提升模型在复杂几何推理任务上的表现，且复杂度越高优势越明显。

Abstract: Multimodal geometry reasoning requires VLMs to extract precise visual relations and preserve them through multi-step deduction. Existing free-form traces obscure the decisions that determine the answer, and trajectory-level reinforcement learning distributes a single terminal signal across the entire response. We introduce credit-addressable reasoning, in which the semantic units exposed during inference also define where learning compares alternatives and assigns credit. We instantiate this principle with Code-CoT, which retains the diagram, represents visual relations as line-addressable executable code, and organizes reasoning into typed events, and CE-GRPO, which selects event boundaries using structural priors and type-normalized entropy, samples complete continuations from shared prefixes, and converts outcome differences into localized advantages. Across nine geometry benchmarks, CE-GRPO achieves an average accuracy of 76.04, outperforming Qwen3-VL-8B and trajectory-level GRPO by $8.09$ and 3.43 points, respectively. Its relative advantage increases with the number of intermediate events, demonstrating the value of representation--optimization co-design for long, dependency-heavy multimodal reasoning.

</details>


### [97] [Trajectory-Initialized Neural Double Q-Routing for Large-Scale Overhead Hoist Transport Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30512)
*Cheng Gu,Qiusheng Zhao,Anbang Liu,Shaochong Lin,Max Z. J. Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Neural Double Q-routing框架，用于半导体晶圆厂中高架升降运输(OHT)系统的多机器人路径规划。该方法以共享状态-动作值网络替代传统的destination-indexed表格，通过混合模拟轨迹的回归初始化（return-to-go regression）进行预热，再结合Double-Q更新、局部拥塞修正和事件分层结构化回放进行在线优化。在9种舰队规模-到达率组合下，相比tabular Double Q-routing，平均完成时间降低0.8%-8.8%，在6个150和200 OHT场景中取得最低平均完成时间；离线初始化在启动场景中可提升完成任务数最多23%，降低尾部完成时间最多15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业机器人舰队共享受限物理基础设施，行驶时间受安全间距、交叉口通行、下游阻塞和站点争用等时变因素影响。静态最短路径路由无法适应动态交通成本，而tabular Q-routing独立学习每个目标-节点-动作价值，缺乏跨稀疏访问路由上下文的信息共享，导致启动行为对不准确的价值估计敏感。

Method: 提出Neural Double Q-routing：1) 用共享状态-动作值网络替代目标索引表格；2) 通过混合模拟器生成轨迹上的return-to-go回归进行离线预热；3) 在线阶段采用Double-Q更新、局部拥塞修正和事件分层结构化回放（event-stratified structured replay）进行精细化。

Result: 在9个匹配的舰队规模-到达率设置（100、150、200 OHT）中，相比tabular Double Q-routing，平均完成时间降低0.8%-8.8%。在6个150和200 OHT设置中取得最低平均完成时间，但Dijkstra在3个100 OHT设置中仍为最优。完成任务数在8/9设置中与baseline差距在1%以内，95百分位完成时间在8个设置中降低。启动场景中离线初始化最多提升23%完成数和降低15%尾部时间。

Conclusion: Neural Double Q-routing通过价值网络泛化和离线预热，有效应对OHT系统中时变交通成本下的路由挑战，在中等到大舰队规模下超越tabular方法和静态路由，但在小规模（100 OHT）低拥塞场景下，静态Dijkstra仍然更具优势，表明方法的选择应依据系统规模与拥塞程度。

Abstract: Large-scale industrial robot fleets share constrained physical infrastructure, making vehicle travel times dependent on safety separation, intersection access, downstream blocking, and station contention. We study this problem in overhead hoist transport (OHT) systems, a representative ceiling-mounted material-handling system used in semiconductor fabs. Static shortest-path routing cannot account for these time-varying traffic costs, whereas tabular Q-routing adapts online but learns each destination--node--action value independently, limiting information sharing across sparsely visited routing contexts and making startup behavior sensitive to inaccurate value estimates. We propose Neural Double Q-routing, which replaces destination-indexed tables with a shared state--action value network. The network is warm-started through return-to-go regression on mixed simulator-generated routing trajectories and then refined online using Double-Q updates, local congestion correction, and event-stratified structured replay. Across nine matched fleet-size--arrival-rate settings with 100, 150, and 200 OHTs, the proposed framework reduces mean completion time relative to tabular Double Q-routing by $0.8\%$--$8.8\%$. It achieves the lowest mean completion time among all compared methods in the six 150- and 200-OHT settings, whereas Dijkstra remains best in the three 100-OHT settings. Completed-task counts remain within $1\%$ of tabular Double Q-routing in eight of nine settings, and 95th-percentile completion time decreases in eight settings. In two matched startup scenarios, offline initialization increases the number of completed tasks by up to $23\%$ and reduces tail completion time by up to $15\%$.

</details>


### [98] [PAC: Progress-Augmented Advantage Curriculum for Multi-Task Reinforcement Learning of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30528)
*Yuanqiang Yu,Yanzhao Zheng,Zhentao Zhang,Tianze Xu,Chao Ma,Jihuai Zhu,Jiashun Liu,Xinle Deng,Baohua Dong,Hangcheng Zhu,Ruohui Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PAC（Progress-Augmented Advantage Curriculum），一种结合优势信号与奖励增益的双信号在线课程学习方法，用于LLM多任务RL后训练的rollout资源分配，在两级推理设置中均优于随机采样和纯优势基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM多任务RL后训练依赖固定或人工设计的任务混合，忽略了任务实用性随训练进行的变化；同时在线课程方法仅以更新幅度定义可学性，未考虑更新是否转化为奖励增益，导致rollout预算可能被分配到更新幅度大但效果不佳的任务上。

Method: PAC结合两个任务级信号：从优势推导的可学性（衡量任务能引发的策略更新幅度）和近期奖励增益（衡量这些更新是否改善任务性能）。通过贝叶斯Thompson Sampling控制器在GRPO训练期间分配不同任务间的rollout预算。

Result: 在多层次推理和跨领域推理两种设置下，PAC均提升样本效率和最终性能：以更少rollout步数达到可比的验证分数，并在两种设置中取得比随机采样和基于优势的课程基线更高的最终平均分数。

Conclusion: 结论表明同时追踪优势信号与实际奖励增益能构建有效的LLM后训练在线课程。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is used to improve the reasoning abilities of LLMs, while training data span heterogeneous tasks. However, most RL post-training pipelines rely on fixed or manually designed task mixtures, even though task usefulness changes as training progresses. Online curriculum methods often define learnability by update magnitude, ignoring whether the update translates into reward gains, which can misallocate rollout budget toward tasks with large but ineffective updates. We propose PAC, a Progress-Augmented Advantage Curriculum for multi-task RL of LLMs that combines two task-level signals: advantage-derived learnability, which measures the magnitude of the policy update a task can induce, and recent reward gains, which show whether those updates have improved task performance. A Bayesian Thompson Sampling controller uses these signals to allocate rollouts across tasks during GRPO training. We evaluate PAC under two settings: a multi-level reasoning setting and a multi-domain reasoning setting. PAC improves sample efficiency and final performance: it reaches comparable validation scores with fewer rollout steps and achieves higher final averages than random sampling and advantage-based curriculum baselines in both settings. These results show that jointly tracking advantage signals and actual reward gains yields an effective online curriculum for LLM post-training.

</details>


### [99] [Three Steps at a Time: Learning Representations from Action Sequences in Contrastive RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30640)
*Michal Korniak,Kamil Dybek,Benjamin Eysenbach,Marco Bagatella,Michał Bortkiewicz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文扩展了对比强化学习（CRL）以在动作块（action chunks）上运行，而非标准的单步动作，在18个离线环境和11个在线环境中分别提升了31.7%和93.1%的性能。与传统解释不同，作者发现动作块带来的增益并非主要源于对非马尔可夫策略的建模或无偏多步回报的传播，而是因为动作块比单步动作携带更多关于目标的信息，从而改善了评判器（critic）的表征学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督强化学习方法在表示学习上取得了强结果，但关于动作应在何种时间尺度上建模仍是一个悬而未决的关键问题。标准的对比强化学习（CRL）基于单步动作，作者希望探索在动作块（多步动作序列）上建模是否能带来更好的性能。

Method: 作者将对比强化学习（CRL）从单步动作扩展为在动作块（action chunks）上运行，并在离线与在线基准环境上进行系统评估。同时通过实证研究深入分析了动作块带来的增益机制，检验了非马尔可夫策略建模、无偏多步回报传播等传统解释是否适用。

Result: 动作块化的CRL在18个离线环境中平均提升31.7%，在11个在线环境中平均提升93.1%，取得了大幅且广泛的性能增益。实证研究表明，传统的解释（非马尔可夫策略建模和多步回报传播）仅部分适用于CRL；真正的关键机制是动作块比单步动作携带更多关于目标的信息，从而显著改善了评判器的表征质量。

Conclusion: 在对比强化学习中，动作块之所以有效，核心原因在于其提供了比单步动作更丰富的目标信息，增强了评判器的表征学习能力。这一发现为自监督RL中的时间尺度选择提供了新的理论视角，并展示了动作块化作为简单有效的改进手段的巨大潜力。

Abstract: While self-supervised approaches to reinforcement learning have achieved strong results by learning representations of states and actions, a key open question is the time scale over which actions should be modeled. Departing from the standard formulation relying on single-step actions, we extend contrastive reinforcement learning (CRL), a prototypical self-supervised method, to operate over action chunks, and find that this results in large, pervasive gains across established offline and online benchmarks: +31.7% and +93.1% across 18 and 11 environments respectively. While action-chunking-driven gains are generally explained through the ability to model non-Markovian, temporally extended policies, and to propagate unbiased multi-step returns, interestingly, we find that these arguments only partially apply to CRL. Our empirical studies suggest that, in the context of CRL, an action chunk carries more information about the goal than a single action, measurably improving the critic's representations, and rendering the algorithm significantly more effective.

</details>


### [100] [PRACTICE: From Experience to Expertise in Self-Evolving Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30760)
*Ziyi Bai,Siqi Li,Tinglei Huang,Börje F. Karlsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PRACTICE框架训练一个技能学习者，从历史交互轨迹中持续发现和维护持久技能库（支持增、改、合、删），同时保持任务执行器冻结，通过两阶段课程学习和在线技能编辑蒸馏，使紧凑的技能学习者为多个冻结执行器带来持续性能提升，在EB-ALFRED和EB-Habitat上超越最强基于经验的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型可作为具身智能体，但如何通过交互持续改进并快速适应环境仍具挑战。现有基于经验的方法依赖手动设计的提示流程来提取和更新技能，此类固定流程难以从多样化的新经验中学习更新后的技能，缺乏自适应能力。

Method: 提出PRACTICE框架：1）训练技能学习者（skill learner）从历史交互轨迹中发现并维护持久技能库，执行器保持冻结；2）技能学习者根据历史技能和新轨迹产生结构化批量编辑（add/refine/merge/remove），并通过分层整合将所有编辑汇总为一致的技能库；3）采用两阶段课程训练：先基于oracle轨迹学习基础技能生成和库维护，再通过对比异构执行器在同一任务上的成功与失败轨迹，学习识别无效动作模式和恢复策略；4）应用在线技能编辑蒸馏，使技能学习者与更强教师模型在其当前编辑分布上对齐。

Result: 实验表明：紧凑的技能学习者在多轮技能库更新中对多个冻结执行器带来一致的性能提升；在EB-ALFRED和EB-Habitat基准上，PRACTICE超越了最强的基于经验的方法。

Conclusion: 通过将技能学习与任务执行解耦，并以自适应批量编辑而非固定流程维护技能库，可以更有效地实现具身智能体的持续学习与环境适应。PRACTICE证明了利用成功与失败轨迹对比学习恢复策略、加上在线蒸馏对齐的有效性。

Abstract: Recent studies have shown that multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can serve as embodied agents, translating language instructions and visual observations into executable plans. However, building agents that can continually improve through interaction and rapidly adapt to their environments remains challenging. Summing up experience from past interaction trajectories provides a promising solution, but existing experience-based methods often rely on manually designed prompting workflows to extract and update skills. Such fixed procedures may struggle to learn updated skills from new and diverse experiences. We introduce PRACTICE, which trains a skill learner to discover and maintain a persistent skill library from past interaction trajectories while keeping the task executor frozen. Given the historical accumulated skills and incoming trajectories, the skill learner produces structured batch-edits that add, refine, merge, or remove skills, and then hierarchical consolidate all collected edits into a consistent updated skill library. We train the learner with a two-stage curriculum. First, it learns basic skill generation and library maintenance from oracle trajectories. Then, by contrasting successful and failed trajectories from heterogeneous executors on the same tasks, it learn to identify invalid action patterns and recovery strategies. Finally, we apply online skill-edit distillation to align the skill learner with a stronger teacher on its current edit distribution to further improves the policy. Experiments demonstrate that a compact skill learner delivers consistent performance improvements across successive library-update rounds for multiple frozen executors. On EB-ALFRED and EB-Habitat, PRACTICE further outperforms the strongest experience-based baselines. Project resources are publicly available at: https://baai-agents.github.io/PRACTICE

</details>


### [101] [T3S: Improving Multi-Task Reinforcement Learning with Task-Specific Feature Selector and Scheduler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30765)
*Yuanqiang Yu,Tianpei Yang,Yongliang Lv,Yan Zheng,Jianye Hao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: T3S是一个多任务强化学习框架，通过超网络构建任务特定的软掩码来选择特征，并通过基于任务进度和学习速度的调度器选择训练任务，在多个机器人操作任务上优于SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MTRL方法通过参数共享训练单一模型解决多任务，但未解决哪些参数应跨任务共享的问题，导致任务间干扰（inter-task interference），显著降低学习效率。

Method: T3S包含两个核心组件：1）特征选择器（Feature Selector）：利用超网络（hypernetworks）为每个任务构造任务特定的软掩码（soft masks），作用于全局共享表示以生成任务特定特征；2）任务调度器（Task Scheduler）：通过两个指标（任务进度如成功率、任务学习速度）选择训练任务，选择概率与任务进度和任务学习速度成反比，即优先选择进度慢、学习慢的任务。

Result: 实验证明T3S在多种机器人操作任务上持续优于现有的最先进MTRL算法。

Conclusion: 通过任务特定特征选择解决参数共享时的干扰问题，并引入自适应任务调度提升多任务学习效率，T3S为MTRL提供了有效且可推广的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-task reinforcement learning (MTRL) is a technique to train multiple tasks simultaneously, where previous works usually train a single model to solve different tasks by sharing parameters across various tasks. However, these methods are faced with inter-task interference since what parameters should be shared across tasks is not addressed, dramatically reducing learning efficiency. To solve these problems, we propose a novel MTRL framework called Task-Specific feature Selector and Scheduler (T3S), which consists of two components: a feature selector and a task scheduler. Specifically, the feature selectors employ hypernetworks to construct task-specific soft masks, which can be applied by globally shared representation to construct task-specific features. The task scheduler selects tasks for learning through two metrics, where the selection probability is inversely proportional to task progress (e.g., success rate) and task learning speed. Experimental results show that T3S consistently outperforms the state-of-the-art MTRL algorithms on various robotics manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [102] [Geometric Attractor Monitoring: A Robust and Frugal Framework for Multi-modal Industrial Robotic Cycles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30804)
*Martin Bonsergent-Brachet,Jesse Read,Dany Abboud*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于相空间重构（PSR）的工业机器人健康监测框架，将单变量传感器数据转化为几何吸引子，并通过离散支持估计作为健康指标，在21台异构机器人的真实数据集上优于标准深度学习基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业异构机器人集群的健康监测面临两大挑战：运行周期的多模态特性以及退化数据（run-to-failure）的稀缺。传统深度学习方法（尤其是基于序列重建的架构）容易过度平滑复杂动力学，掩盖早期退化迹象，难以在工业约束下有效工作。

Method: 将监测问题重构为相空间重建（PSR）框架：不预测时间序列，而是将单变量传感器数据转换为几何吸引子，显式展开机械状态并独立于时间发生顺序。在该空间中评估多种异常评分技术，最终采用离散支持估计作为计算高效的健康指标（HI）。

Result: 在包含21台异构机器人、跨越三年的真实数据集以及合成Langevin系统上验证，该方法优于标准深度学习的基线方法。

Conclusion: 将算法偏差与目标系统的几何属性对齐，能够产生实用、可追溯且易于部署的方法，完美适配工业约束的实际情况。

Abstract: Monitoring the health of heterogeneous industrial robot fleets is severely challenged by the multi-modal nature of their operational cycles and a persistent scarcity of run-to-failure data. Standard data-driven approaches, particularly deep learning architectures relying on sequential reconstruction, often struggle in this specific setting; they tend to over-smooth complex dynamics, masking early signs of degradation. To address these industrial constraints, we reframe the monitoring problem through a framework based on Phase Space Reconstruction (PSR). Instead of predicting temporal sequences, this framework transforms univariate sensor data into a geometric attractor, explicitly unfolding the mechanical states independently of their temporal occurrence. By evaluating various anomaly scoring techniques within this space, we demonstrate that discrete support estimation provides an effective and computationally frugal Health Indicator (HI). Validated on a real-world dataset of 21 heterogeneous robots over three years and a synthetic Langevin system, our approach outperforms standard deep learning baselines. We show that aligning the algorithmic bias with the geometric properties of the target system yields a pragmatic, traceable and easily deployable approach perfectly tailored to the realities of industrial constraints.

</details>


### [103] [What Emerges and What Breaks in Self-Play Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30819)
*Laur Sisask,Ardi Tampuu,Tambet Matiisen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究通过纯自我对弈训练自动驾驶策略，将模型从MLP扩展到Transformer，并在真实城市的高精地图上训练。在CARLA和Waymax基准上性能不及Gigaflow，差距归因于特定失败模式（如红绿灯处的奖励黑客行为、缺乏停车标志的停车激励）。文章进一步分析了自我对弈中涌现的交通规则与人类驾驶的匹配程度，并验证了奖励条件化能产生预期的驾驶行为多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶策略的纯自我对弈训练近期展现出良好前景，但现有工作（如Gigaflow、Puffer-Drive）主要使用MLP模型且训练环境与真实部署场景存在差距。作者希望通过将模型升级为Transformer并在真实城市高精地图上训练，探索自我对弈方法在更接近实际部署环境中的表现。

Method: 采用与Gigaflow和Puffer-Drive类似的自我对弈训练框架，但将策略模型从MLP替换为Transformer架构，并在真实城市的高精度地图上进行训练。训练后针对CARLA和Waymax基准进行系统性评估，并诊断具体失败模式，同时分析涌现的交通规则行为以及奖励条件化对行为多样性的影响。

Result: 在CARLA和Waymax基准上，所提策略的性能不及Gigaflow。性能差距可追溯到具体失败模式：在红绿灯处出现奖励黑客行为，且策略缺乏在停车标志处停车的激励。此外，研究确认奖励条件化能够产生预期范围内的驾驶行为多样性，并对自我对弈中涌现的交通规则与人类驾驶的匹配程度进行了分析。

Conclusion: 将自我对弈扩展到Transformer架构和真实城市地图虽然可行，但在当前设置下性能仍不及更强的基线方法。失败模式的定性分析揭示了奖励设计在塑造合规驾驶行为中的关键作用，尤其是与交通信号相关的规则。自我对弈中涌现的行为多样性与奖励条件化相关，但仍需进一步研究提升安全性及与人类驾驶习惯的兼容性。

Abstract: Training autonomous driving policies through pure self-play has recently shown promising results. Following Gigaflow and Puffer- Drive, we train driving policies in a similar self-play fashion, but extend the models from MLPs to Transformers and train on the high-definition map of a real city, where we ultimately aim to deploy them. On the CARLA and Waymax benchmarks, our policies fall short of Gigaflow, and we trace the gap to specific failure modes, including reward hacking at traffic lights and a missing incentive to stop at stop signs. We further analyze which traffic rules emerge from self-play and how closely they match human driving, and we confirm that reward conditioning yields the intended diversity of driving behaviors. A demonstration of a trained policy is available at https://laursisask-ut.github.io/eccvdemo.

</details>


### [104] [S3C-LLM: Skill-Code Guided Agentic Language Models for Spectrum-to-Structure Elucidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30910)
*Xuanle Zhao,Xinyuan Cai,Xiang Cheng,Bo Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: S3C-LLM是一个技能引导且基于代码的代理式大语言模型，用于光谱到结构解析。它通过检索模态特定的光谱学技能、执行分析代码在输入光谱上实例化这些技能，并结合峰值级证据和分子式约束来生成SMILES，而非直接预测分子结构。该模型采用自进化技能库、思维增强的技能-代码轨迹构建流水线，以及SFT+步级RL的两阶段训练策略，在多个基准上以不到SpectraLLM十分之一的训练语料实现了更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于LLM的光谱解析方法主要将任务简化为直接从光谱生成SMILES，这种范式虽然能利用配对光谱数据，但未能显式建模光谱学家实际使用的分析方法（如诊断峰解释、碎片推理、分子式约束及化学一致性检查），缺乏对专业分析工作流的建模。

Method: S3C-LLM采用三种关键策略：(1) 构建自进化的光谱学技能库，支持模态特定的技能检索；(2) 设计思维增强的技能-代码轨迹构建流水线，将分析代码执行在输入光谱上以实例化技能；(3) 提出两阶段训练策略，先对Qwen3-4B进行监督微调（SFT），再采用步级强化学习（RL）进一步优化。

Result: 在多种基准数据集上的实验表明，S3C-LLM在跨光谱任务中一致优于现有的通用大语言模型和光谱专用模型，同时仅使用SpectraLLM训练语料不到十分之一的数据量。

Conclusion: 通过将光谱学家的专业分析工作流（技能引导、代码执行、证据整合）显式建模到LLM中，并以高效的两阶段训练策略（SFT+RL）优化，可以在显著降低训练数据需求的同时获得更好的光谱解析性能，证明技能引导和代码接地的方法优于直接生成范式。

Abstract: Spectroscopic structure elucidation is central to molecular analysis, but recent Large Language Model (LLM)-based methods mostly formulate it as direct spectrum-to-SMILES generation. Although this paradigm can leverage paired spectral data, it does not explicitly model the analytical workflow used by spectroscopists, such as diagnostic peak interpretation, fragment reasoning, formula constraints, and chemical consistency checking. In this paper, we introduce S3C-LLM, a skill-guided and code-grounded agentic LLM for spectrum-to-structure elucidation. Rather than directly predicting a molecule, S3C-LLM retrieves modality-specific spectroscopy skills, executes analysis code to instantiate these skills on the input spectra, and integrates the resulting peak-level evidence and formula constraints before generating SMILES. Specifically, we contribute a self-evolving spectroscopy skill library, a thinking-augmented skill-code trajectory construction pipeline, and a two-stage training strategy that teaches Qwen3-4B through supervised fine-tuning (SFT) followed by our proposed step-level reinforcement learning (RL). Experiments on diverse benchmarks show that S3C-LLM consistently outperforms current general LLMs and spectrum-specific models across spectra, while using less than 1/10th of SpectraLLM's training corpus.

</details>


### [105] [One Policy Is Enough: Single-Agent Reinforcement Learning Outperforms Tree Search for Chemistry Tool Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30952)
*Armin Dariani,Sifan Wu,Bang Liu,Entao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种简化化学工具使用的方法：用单一策略模型替代CheMatAgent中分层演化MCTS+双critic的复杂框架，通过监督式预热加结果级强化学习（以金标准调用链的程序化奖励）训练，在ChemToolBench多工具综合化学任务上显著提升Tool F1和Return F1，且单次模型调用即达到优于代价高昂的树搜索的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学问题需要精确计算和数据库查询，语言模型必须调用外部工具。现有系统CheMatAgent采用分层演化MCTS，依赖两个学习得到的critic和一个GPT赋分的回归目标，训练循环中引入学习式评判者（judge），复杂且脆弱。作者认为一个单一策略就足够，旨在去除学习式critic和judge，简化训练流程同时提升性能。

Method: 采用单一策略模型，在单次从左到右生成中交替进行推理、工具调用和返回值的处理。训练分为两个阶段：先进行监督式预热（supervised warm-up），然后使用程序化奖励（直接从金标准调用链读取）进行结果级强化学习（outcome-level RL）。整个训练循环不依赖任何学习式critic或judge，奖励完全由金标准调用链程序化给出。

Result: 在ChemToolBench多工具综合化学基准上，基于Qwen-2.5-7B骨干网络，Tool F1提升5.5%，Return F1提升9.6%；基于Llama-3.1-8B骨干网络，Tool F1提升3.7%，Return F1提升3.9%。均与CheMatAgent最强搜索配置相比，而本文方法每道题仅需一次模型调用，相比之下对比系统搜索成本随树增长。此外在Qwen-2.5-7B上答案通过率（Pass Rate）也领先。

Conclusion: 证明了在化学工具使用场景中，单一策略模型经过监督预热和程序化奖励强化学习即可超越复杂的多组件搜索系统。去除学习式critic和judge不仅简化了训练流程，还带来了更优的性能和更低的推理成本，表明复杂的分层搜索架构在该任务上并非必要。

Abstract: Chemistry questions often demand exact computation and database lookups that a language model cannot supply from its parameters, so it must reach for external tools. Tool use here is a three-part problem: select the right tool from a large pool, fill it with correctly typed arguments, and chain calls so that each consumes the outputs of the last. CheMatAgent, a previously published system, addresses this with hierarchical evolutionary MCTS: separate policy and execution models searching tool-call trees under two learned critics, one regressed partly onto GPT-assigned scores. We show that a single policy suffices. Our model interleaves reasoning, tool calls, and returns in one left-to-right generation, trained by a supervised warm-up and then outcome-level reinforcement learning against a programmatic reward read directly off the gold call chain, which leaves no learned critic and no judge in the training loop. On ChemToolBench multiple-tool comprehensive chemistry, on both backbones CheMatAgent use, we improve Tool F1 by 5.5% and Return F1 by 9.6% on Qwen-2.5-7B, and by 3.7% and 3.9% on Llama-3.1-8B, compared with their strongest search configuration, at one model invocation per question, against a search whose cost grows with the tree; we also lead answer Pass Rate on Qwen-2.5-7B.

</details>


### [106] [Normalized Low-Rank Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31036)
*Jiale Kang,Ziyin Yue,Zheng Zhan,Yangyi Huang,Weiyang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NoRA（Normalized Low-Rank Adaptation）方法，通过对LoRA的下投影矩阵进行归一化来正则化其训练动力学，从而在预训练、监督微调和强化学习等任务中加速收敛、提升性能与训练稳定性，并缓解灾难性遗忘，且无需额外参数或推理计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA虽被广泛用于参数高效模型适配，但其训练动力学的正则化方式尚未被充分探索。由于LoRA将上投影初始化为零，早期优化动力学主要由下投影主导，这一观察为通过归一化下投影来稳定训练提供了契机。

Method: NoRA在训练过程中对下投影矩阵进行归一化，利用LoRA早期优化由下投影主导的特性来稳定训练。同时，作者还证明该归一化只需在初始化时应用一次即可改进标准LoRA，无需在整个训练过程中反复归一化。

Result: 在预训练、监督微调和强化学习三个场景中，NoRA均一致地加速收敛、提升性能与训练稳定性，并缓解灾难性遗忘。该方法既不需要额外可训练参数，也不增加推理时计算开销。

Conclusion: NoRA是一种简单且具有广泛适用性的LoRA增强方法，通过轻量的归一化操作即可有效正则化LoRA的训练动力学，在多种任务上带来一致且稳定的性能提升。

Abstract: While low-rank adaptation (LoRA) is widely used for parameter-efficient model adaptation, how to regularize its training dynamics for stable and effective optimization remains underexplored. Because LoRA initializes the up-projection to zero, its early optimization dynamics are largely governed by the down-projection. Building on this observation, we introduce Normalized Low-Rank Adaptation (NoRA), a simple yet effective method that normalizes the down-projection matrices during training. We further show that the same normalization can be applied only at initialization, improving standard LoRA without requiring repeated normalization throughout training. Across pretraining, supervised finetuning, and reinforcement learning, NoRA consistently accelerates convergence, improves performance and training stability, and mitigates catastrophic forgetting. These benefits require neither additional trainable parameters nor inference-time computation, making NoRA a simple and broadly applicable enhancement to LoRA.

</details>


### [107] [Does On-Policy Distillation Really Distill? From Noisy Teacher to Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31046)
*Yi Ding,Ruqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过定量分析发现，同策略蒸馏（OPD）中教师提供的密集token级监督存在大量噪声，且噪声随教师规模增大而增多，但学生策略对这些噪声不敏感。进一步分析发现OPD的性能提升主要来自抑制低log-probability token，而非真正的知识蒸馏。基于此，作者提出无监督方法OPSA（On-Policy Self-Adaptation），利用熵自适应负优势来抑制尾部token并重新分配概率质量，在AIME24等基准上显著超越基线及OPD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同策略蒸馏（OPD）作为RLVR的替代方案提供密集的token级监督，但其教师对学生的轨迹评分时存在本质上的off-policy问题，导致监督的可靠性及其真正作用机制不明确。作者希望弄清OPD改进的来源：是否真的在蒸馏知识，还是通过其他机制起作用，并据此设计更高效的方法。

Method: 首先对OPD训练过程中教师监督进行定量噪声分析；然后通过消融实验（保留或移除噪声监督）检验学生策略的敏感性；进一步通过分析学习集中在哪些token上（低log-probability token）以及用固定负优势替代教师优势，揭示OPD真实机制——抑制低概率token。基于此提出OPSA，使用熵自适应负优势，对高熵位置施加强学习信号以压制尾部token，并将概率质量均匀重分配到头部token，完全无需教师监督。

Result: OPD的教师监督噪声显著且随教师规模增大而增加，但学生对其不敏感。基于Qwen3-1.7B，OPSA在AIME24上Avg@32提升35.41点（相对提升263%），Pass@32在三个基准上均翻倍以上；OPSA在AIME24的Avg@32上比OPD高出16.77点。跨模型家族和任务的实验验证了其有效性与泛化性。

Conclusion: OPD的实际作用并非传统意义上的知识蒸馏，而主要是通过抑制低log-probability token来提升性能，这一机制甚至不需要教师。无监督的OPSA通过熵自适应负优势实现同样的效果且性能更优，提供了更简洁高效的替代方案。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) offers dense token-level supervision as an alternative to the sparse outcome-level advantages of reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR). However, the teacher scores student-generated trajectories that are inherently off-policy for it, so the reliability of its supervision, and hence the source of the student's improvement, remains unclear. We quantitatively analyze teacher supervision during OPD training and find substantial noise whose prevalence increases with teacher scale. Surprisingly, the student policy is insensitive to such noise, converging to comparable performance regardless of whether noisy supervision is retained or removed. Does OPD distill at all? By analyzing what drives its gains, we find that learning concentrates on low log-probability tokens, and using a single fixed negative advantage matches the performance of teacher-provided ones. This suggests that OPD works largely by suppressing low log-probability tokens, which requires no teacher. These findings motivate On-Policy Self-Adaptation (OPSA), a supervision-free method using entropy-adaptive negative advantages. It assigns stronger learning signals to high-entropy positions, suppressing tail tokens, and evenly redistributing probability mass among head tokens. Compared with the base \texttt{Qwen3-1.7B}, OPSA improves Avg@32 by 35.41 points on AIME24, corresponding to a 263\% relative gain, and more than doubles Pass@32 across all three benchmarks. It also outperforms OPD by 16.77 points in Avg@32 on AIME24. Extensive experiments and analyses across model families and tasks further demonstrate its effectiveness and generalizability.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [108] [RedLight-VLA: Models for traffic-rule grounding and behavioral emphasis in driving policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28656)
*Bala Murali Manoghar Sai Sudhakar,Sourab Bapu Sridhar,Sandipan Das,Rahul Ahuja,Meda Lazar,Ashish Garg,Pratik Likhar,Senthil Yogamani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RedLight-VLA，通过轨迹导出的行为重加权（BR）和并行辅助头（AUX）监督红绿灯与停止线状态，改善行为克隆VLA驾驶策略在信号交叉口的罕见规则驾驶动作（如刹车和起步）表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为克隆的VLA驾驶策略在信号交叉口难以处理由规则控制的罕见驾驶动作。刹车和起步样本在平均轨迹损失中占比极小，且融合表示缺乏对主导规则（红绿灯和停止线状态）的显式监督，导致模型在红绿灯场景下表现不佳。

Method: RedLight-VLA包含两个组件：(1) 轨迹导出的行为重加权（BR），利用旋转不变的纵向动力学和保尺度缩减来强调罕见的减速和加速样本，且禁用时可精确恢复基线；(2) 并行辅助头（AUX），在连续的后融合规约token上锚定红绿灯和停止线状态，无需自回归语言生成或修改轨迹解码器。

Result: 与相同主干网络、训练数据、解码器和评估群体的VLA基线相比，RedLight-VLA将红灯停止线超调从7.3%降至6.8%，停止线速度误差降低12.7%，3秒红绿灯切片的ADE/FDE从0.274/0.964 m提升至0.247/0.897 m。绿灯误停从3.2%增至3.9%，但BR+AUX组合缓解了单独AUX导致的更大增幅（4.0%）。组合模型在非红绿灯切片的ADE/FDE也从0.268/0.956 m提升至0.241/0.876 m。

Conclusion: RedLight-VLA通过行为重加权和辅助监督有效提升了VLA驾驶策略在信号交叉口的规则驾驶表现，且组合使用BR和AUX在全部四个位移度量上优于单独使用任一机制，验证了两种机制互补的有效性。

Abstract: Behavior-cloned Vision-Language-Action (VLA) driving policies struggle with rare rule-governed maneuvers at signalized intersections. Braking and launching examples contribute little to averaged trajectory loss, while fused representations lack explicit supervision for the governing traffic-light and stop-line state. We present RedLight-VLA, a training objective that uses expert futures and automatically generated perception targets without additional manual rule annotation. First, trajectory-derived behavioral reweighting (BR) emphasizes rare deceleration and acceleration using rotation-invariant longitudinal dynamics and a scale-preserving reduction that exactly recovers the baseline when disabled. Second, parallel auxiliary (AUX) heads ground traffic-light and stop-line state in continuous post-fusion rule tokens, without autoregressive language generation or changes to the trajectory decoder. We evaluate on a curated set of 20 s sequences with a 5 s prediction horizon. Controlled variants share the same backbone, training data, decoder, and evaluation population. Against an otherwise identical VLA baseline, RedLight-VLA reduces red-light stop-line overshoot from 7.3% to6.8%, reduces stop-line velocity error by 12.7%, and improves 3 s trafficlight-sliced ADE/FDE from 0.274/0.964 m to 0.247/0.897 m. Green-light false stops increase from 3.2% to 3.9%; however, combining BR with AUX supervision mitigates the larger increase observed for AUX alone (4.0%). The combined model also improves non-traffic-light ADE/FDE from 0.268/0.956 m to 0.241/0.876 m and outperforms either mechanism alone on all four sliced displacement measures.

</details>


### [109] [The Potential of Haptic Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28664)
*Jianquan Wang,Haiwei Dong,Abdulmotaleb El Saddik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了触觉基础模型（HFMs）的变革潜力与发展路径，旨在将触觉感知引入基础模型框架，以解决当前触觉模型因硬件异构性和数据采集困难而局限于特定任务的问题，并对现有大型触觉数据集和基准进行了综合评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基础模型在语言和视觉领域取得了显著成功，但将其扩展到具身AI领域受到缺乏通用触觉感知的瓶颈限制。消费电子设备（如智能手机、可穿戴设备、VR控制器、家庭机器人和健康监测设备）需要安全且自适应的物理交互，而现有触觉模型因硬件异构性及依赖主动物理数据采集而僵化地局限于特定任务。

Method: 本文首先阐明从被动的LLM/VLM向主动的HFM转变所需的范式迁移，涵盖四个核心维度：动作耦合（action coupling）、物理动态表示空间（physical dynamical representation space）、连续时间序列数据粒度（continuous time-series data granularity）以及动作条件下的未来状态预测（action-conditioned future state prediction）。随后综合现有的大规模触觉数据集，并在TacBench基准上评估UniTouch、AnyTouch、T3和Sparsh四个模型在力估计、滑移检测和相对位姿估计任务上的表现。

Result: 本文系统梳理了HFM所需的四大核心维度转变，整合了现有大规模触觉数据集，并在TacBench上对UniTouch、AnyTouch、T3和Sparsh进行了力估计、滑移检测和相对位姿估计的基准评估。

Conclusion: 触觉基础模型（HFMs）是实现具身AI向消费电子领域拓展的关键方向，需要通过动作耦合、物理动态表示、时序处理和未来状态预测四个维度的范式转变，才能突破当前触觉模型的通用性瓶颈。

Abstract: Despite the success of foundation models in language and vision, their expansion into embodied AI is bottlenecked by a lack of generalized touch sensing. This limitation is especially relevant to consumer electronics, where smartphones, wearables, VR controllers, home robots, and health monitoring devices require safe and adaptive physical interaction. Constrained by hardware heterogeneity and the necessity of active physical data collection, current haptic models remain rigidly task-specific. To overcome these limitations, this article explores the transformative potential and developmental trajectory of Haptic Foundation Models (HFMs). We detail the paradigm shift required to transition from passive Large Language Models and Vision Language Models into active HFMs across four core dimensions: action coupling, physical dynamical representation space, continuous time-series data granularity, and action-conditioned future state prediction. Furthermore, we synthesize existing large-scale tactile datasets and benchmark UniTouch, AnyTouch, T3, and Sparsh on TacBench for force estimation, slip detection, and relative pose estimation.

</details>


### [110] [Cognitively-Grounded On-Device Runtime Learning for Ground Robots in Unknown Physical Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28677)
*Yihao Cai,Yanbing Mao,Christian Lebiere*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CogRun框架，使安全关键的ground机器人在无先验地图和感知知识的未知物理环境中，完全在边缘AI设备上进行认知驱动的运行时学习，并通过四足机器人真实森林实验和越野自动驾驶车辆仿真实验验证其安全性和有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人运行时学习方法在安全关键场景中面临挑战：依赖无线通信和云端计算限制了在无连接环境中的应用；缺乏认知驱动的学习机制，难以在未知环境中安全地进行持续学习；需要一种能够完全在边缘设备上运行、兼顾安全性与学习效率的自主框架。

Method: CogRun由三个组件构成：Learning-Agent采用创新的认知神经学习架构，包括专用重放缓冲区、认知驱动的经验采样，以及与实例学习（IBL）相结合的行动者-评论家强化学习（RL）的安全感知行动混合；Rational-Agent作为非学习模块专门处理安全关键功能；Coordinator管理两个agent之间的交互以促进安全高效的运行时学习。整个自主堆栈（感知、学习、控制）均部署在边缘AI设备上，消除了对无线通信的依赖。

Result: 在真实野外森林中的四足机器人实验和模拟野外森林中的越野自动驾驶车辆实验表明，CogRun能够实现安全高效的运行时学习，使机器人能够在复杂未知环境中安全且持续地与物理世界交互，从而提升任务性能。

Conclusion: CogRun通过在边缘设备上实现完整的认知驱动运行时学习自主堆栈，消除了对通信基础设施的依赖，为安全关键的ground机器人在未知、无连接环境中进行持续、安全的学习和任务执行提供了一种可行的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents \ul{CogRun}, a framework that enables safety-critical ground robots to perform cognitively-grounded runtime learning entirely on edge-AI devices in unknown physical environments, without prior maps or perceptual knowledge. CogRun consists of three components: a Learning-Agent, a Rational-Agent, and a Coordinator. The Learning-Agent is novel in cognitive-neural learning architecture, which featurs dedicated replay buffers, cognition-driven experience sampling, and a safety-aware action blending of actor-critic reinforcement learning (RL) with instance-based learning (IBL). The Rational-Agent is a non-learning module that complements the Learning-Agent by exclusively handling safety-critical functions, while the Coordinator manages interactions between the two agents to promote safe and efficient runtime learning. CogRun's full autonomy stack (i.e., perception, learning, and control) on edge-AI devices eliminates dependence on wireless communications, enabling broader applications in challenging environments with limited or no connectivity. Experiments on a quadruped robot in real-world wild forests and on an off-road autonomous vehicle in a simulated wild forest demonstrate that CogRun enables safe and efficient runtime learning, allowing robots to safely and continuously interact with the physical world for enhancing task performance in complex, unknown environments.

</details>


### [111] [RoboGesture: Real-Time Semantic-aligned Co-Speech Gestures Generation for Humanoid Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28693)
*Zifan Wang,Ziang Ren,Pengyang Shi,Zirui Wang,Chenghuai Lin,Tianze Wang,Zekun Qi,Liangliang Zhao,He Wang,Li Yi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboGesture是一个面向人形机器人的端到端框架，通过协同设计数据、建模与安全控制，实现机器人实时倾听人类语音并以同步且语义匹配的手势做出响应。系统包括包含300+手势类别的数据集、基于扩散Transformer的分层语义-声学对齐生成器、反惯性CFG掩蔽策略以及MPC安全滤波器，在Unitree G1人形机器人上验证了其安全性、节奏感和语义契合度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在实时对话中需产出与语音同步且语义有意义的手势，但该任务面临三大障碍：(1)缺乏语义丰富的数据集；(2)"模态遮蔽"问题，即模型倾向于依赖运动惯性而忽略音频线索；(3)物理安全问题导致的仿真到现实迁移鸿沟。现有方法难以同时解决这些问题。

Method: RoboGesture采用"数据-建模-控制"协同设计：(1)构建包含300+手势类别的RoboGesture数据集，并通过自动化流程合成大规模无碰撞、机器人专属的音频-动作对；(2)提出分层语义-声学对齐器(Hierarchical Semantic-Acoustic Aligner)，从原始音频token中提取多粒度韵律和语义线索；(3)基于条件流匹配的扩散Transformer构建流式条件运动生成器(Streaming Conditional Motion Generator)；(4)引入反惯性CFG掩蔽(Anti-Inertia CFG Masking)防止模型塌缩到历史重复模式，强迫其主动挖掘音频模态的控制信号；(5)基于MPC的安全滤波器保障物理硬件上的实时无碰撞执行。

Result: 在Unitree G1人形机器人上的实验表明，RoboGesture相比最先进的基线方法，生成的手势更加安全、节奏感更强、语义契合度更高，且能够在实时交互中自然响应人类语音。

Conclusion: RoboGesture通过数据、建模与控制的联合设计，系统性地解决了语义手势生成中的三大核心挑战，为自然的人机交互提供了一套完整可部署的实时手势生成方案，并在真实物理平台上验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Enabling humanoid robots to respond to human speech with synchronized and semantically meaningful gestures is fundamental to natural human-robot interaction. However, this task faces three critical barriers: the scarcity of semantically rich datasets, the "modality eclipse" where models ignore audio cues in favor of kinematic inertia, and the sim-to-real gap regarding physical safety. We propose RoboGesture, a robot-centric framework that co-designs data, modeling, and control to power a complete interactive human-humanoid system in which the robot listens, responds, and gestures in real time. We first establish the RoboGesture dataset featuring over 300 gesture categories and develop an automated pipeline to synthesize large-scale collision-free, robot-specific audio-motion pairs. Our architecture features a Hierarchical Semantic-Acoustic Aligner that extracts multi-granular prosodic and semantic cues directly from raw audio tokens. These cues drive a Streaming Conditional Motion Generator based on a diffusion transformer with conditional flow matching. To ensure high responsiveness, we introduce Anti-Inertia CFG Masking, which prevents the model from collapsing into repetitive historical patterns by compelling it to proactively mine control signals from the audio modality. Finally, an MPC-based safety filter ensures real-time, collision-free execution on physical hardware. Experiments on a Unitree G1 humanoid demonstrate that RoboGesture generates safer, more rhythmic, and more semantically appropriate responses compared to state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [112] [Multi-Group Pipe Routing under Permanent Geometric Occupancy: Problem, Benchmark, and Classical Baselines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28697)
*Deng Quan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究增材制造中液压元件内部多组流体通道的永久几何占位路由问题，形式化为多组管道布线问题，构建了带可行性证人的三维基准测试集，并评估了CBS、PBS和优先级规划三种经典算法在该耦合约束下的基线性能。


<details>
